<!-- FORMAT:usage-context-v1 -->

<!-- WORD:W001 -->
# accommodate

**词源**：accommodate 源于拉丁语 accommodare，意为“使一物适合另一物”。可把“使合适”作为记忆线索：空间容得下、安排顾得上、人肯通融，都是现代意义的发展；accommodation 也有独立的住宿、协调和眼调节义。

<!-- USE:U001_01 -->
## accommodate /əˈkɑmədeɪt/ v. · 容纳得下
`表示建筑、空间或设施有足够容量容纳人或物；直接接宾语，通常不用进行时，强调装得下，不必已经提供住宿。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_01 USE:U001_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"容纳得下","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/66597/pg66597-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_01 USE:U001_01 -->
In Gilbey’s account, a horse-litter accommodated only one person, usually a high-ranking lady on ceremonial occasions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_01 -->
在吉尔比的记述中，马抬轿只能容纳一人，通常是在仪式场合乘坐的一位身份高贵的女士。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_01 -->
<!-- USE:U001_02 -->
## accommodate /əˈkɑmədeɪt/ v. · 为人安排住宿
`及物动词，给某人提供住处或临时住宿；被动结构 be accommodated in/at 后说明安置地点，与单纯的容量义有别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_02 USE:U001_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"为人安排住宿","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/student-route-caseworker-guidance/student-and-child-student"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_02 USE:U001_02 -->
A non-boarding applicant under the UK Child Student route who is not being accommodated by their licensed student sponsor must have enough money to cover living costs while studying in the UK.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_02 -->
申请英国儿童学生签证的非寄宿申请人，如果并非由获许可的学生签证担保机构安排住宿，就必须备有足够资金支付在英国学习期间的生活费。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_02 -->
<!-- USE:U001_03 -->
## accommodate /əˈkɑmədeɪt/ v. · 调整以兼顾条件
`较正式，安排或改变事物以顾及某种需求、变化或条件。可直接接 needs、changes 等，也可用 accommodate A to B 表示使两者协调；不是不加原则地满足一切要求。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_03 USE:U001_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"调整以兼顾条件","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/sickness-absence-and-health-in-the-workplace-understanding-employer-behaviour-and-practice/sickness-absence-and-health-in-the-workplace-understanding-employer-behaviour-and-practice-report"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_03 USE:U001_03 -->
In interviews about workplace adjustments, a firm of accountants explained that it could not always accommodate requests to work from home when staff needed to carry out audits at clients’ premises.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_03 -->
在关于工作安排调整的访谈中，一家会计师事务所解释说，员工需要到客户办公地点开展审计时，事务所无法总是满足在家办公的要求。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_03 -->
<!-- USE:U001_04 -->
## accommodate /əˈkɑmədeɪt/ v. · 帮忙通融或提供所需
`为帮助、使人满意而提供便利，直接接人；accommodate somebody with something 表示向某人提供所需之物。语境可带迁就意味，不限于住宿。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_04 USE:U001_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"帮忙通融或提供所需","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/30373/30373-h/30373-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_04 USE:U001_04 -->
Book collectors who accommodate their friends with books will feel the sting of this reply to a man who could not persuade friends to return his books.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_04_2 -->
“Your acquaintances find, I suppose, that it is much more easy to retain the books themselves, than what is contained in them.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_04 -->
把书借给朋友、与人方便的藏书人，读到有人讨不回借出的书时得到的这番回答，也会感到刺痛：“我想，您的熟人们发现，留住书本本身比记住书里的内容容易得多。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_04 -->
<!-- USE:U001_05 -->
## accommodate /əˈkɑmədeɪt/ v. · 使自己适应
`较正式，行为或观念适应新条件，用 accommodate to something，或 accommodate oneself to something；后者明确表示调整自己。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_05 USE:U001_05 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使自己适应","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/54463/pg54463-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_05 USE:U001_05 -->
He will have to go into a new home and accommodate himself to that, at a time of life and in a condition of health unfitting him for a change.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_05_2 -->
You are unfeeling, Countess.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_05 -->
他将不得不搬入一个新家并使自己适应那里，而他的年纪和健康状况都不适合这样的改变。伯爵夫人，你太无情了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_05 -->
<!-- USE:U001_06 -->
## accommodating /əˈkɑmədeɪtɪŋ/ adj. · 乐于助人且肯通融
`形容人愿意帮忙、配合或照顾别人的需要，通常带赞许；不是形容房间容量大。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_06 USE:U001_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"乐于助人且肯通融","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/54463/pg54463-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_06 USE:U001_06 -->
The old lender was so accommodating that I borrowed money from him to invest in Polpluggan.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_06_2 -->
But speculating with borrowed capital was ruinous: he led me into a trap.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_06 -->
那个老放贷人如此肯通融，我便向他借钱投资波尔普卢根。但借钱投机带来了灾难：他把我引进了圈套。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_06 -->
<!-- USE:U001_07 -->
## accommodatingly /əˈkɑmədeɪtɪŋli/ adv. · 通融地配合
`表示以愿意帮忙、迁就需要的方式做事，修饰具体回应或行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_07 USE:U001_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"通融地配合","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/39050/pg39050-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_07_intro -->
Ike threatened to settle their quarrel after work.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_07 USE:U001_07 -->
"Just as you like, Ike," assented Ralph accommodatingly—"only, drop it long enough just now to start me in at my duties, or we’ll both have Mr. Forgan in our hair."
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_07 -->
艾克扬言，下班后要把两人的争执了结。“随你的便，艾克，”拉尔夫通融地答应，“不过，眼下先放一放，带我开始干活吧，不然福根先生会来找我们俩的麻烦。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_07 -->
<!-- USE:U001_08 -->
## accommodation /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən/ n. · 住宿与住处
`指居住或暂住的房屋、房间。英式英语通常不可数；美式常用复数 accommodations。常说 hotel/student/emergency accommodation；overnight/rental/sheltered/temporary accommodation；affordable/cheap/comfortable/suitable accommodation；include/offer/provide/book/find accommodation。housing 偏住房供给，lodging 偏寄宿或住宿，board 指膳食，room and board 才兼含食宿；dwelling 是一处住宅。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_08 USE:U001_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"住宿与住处","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/housing-and-universal-credit"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_08 USE:U001_08 -->
If you live in temporary accommodation arranged by your council because you are homeless, you cannot get Universal Credit to help with housing costs.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_08_2 -->
Apply for Housing Benefit instead.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_08 -->
如果你因无家可归而住在地方议会安排的临时住处，就不能通过统一福利金获得住房费用补助，应改为申请住房补贴。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_08 -->
<!-- USE:U001_09 -->
## accommodation /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən/ n. · 可供使用的场所空间
`较正式的不可数名词，指建筑或交通工具内为人员、物品或活动提供的空间设施，可说 office accommodation；不必是过夜住处。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_09 USE:U001_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可供使用的场所空间","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/66597/pg66597-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_09 USE:U001_09 -->
An advertisement of 1750 offered "accommodation behind the coach for baggage and passengers", with servants riding in the basket behind or on the box beside the driver.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_09 -->
一则1750年的广告提供“马车后部供行李和乘客使用的空间”，仆人可坐在后面的篮式座厢，或车夫旁边的驾驶座上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_09 -->
<!-- USE:U001_10 -->
## accommodation /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən/ n. · 为特殊需要作的调整
`指为了适应特殊目的或不同需要而作的调整安排；重点是适配，不一定是双方让步达成协议。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_10 USE:U001_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"为特殊需要作的调整","sourceUrls":["https://www.eeoc.gov/laws/guidance/small-employers-and-reasonable-accommodation"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_10 USE:U001_10 -->
For employers covered by the U.S. ADA, a modified schedule can be a reasonable accommodation for an employee with a disability, unless it causes undue hardship.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_10_2 -->
That could mean changing arrival or departure times, or providing periodic breaks.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_10 -->
对于受美国《残疾人法》约束的雇主，如果不会造成过度困难，调整作息时间可以是为残障员工提供的一项合理便利安排。这可能包括调整上下班时间，或提供定时休息。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_10 -->
<!-- USE:U001_11 -->
## accommodation /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən/ n. · 使双方相安的妥协安排
`较正式，指通过协调达成的安排、和解或妥协，可数，可用 an accommodation with somebody、between groups 或 make accommodations；侧重让不同利益能够共存。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_11 USE:U001_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使双方相安的妥协安排","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/speeches/northern-ireland-protocol-foreign-secretarys-statement-17-may-2022"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_11 USE:U001_11 -->
In May 2022, the UK Foreign Secretary said the planned Bill would give the government power to implement a revised Northern Ireland Protocol if it could reach an accommodation that met its goal of protecting the Belfast Good Friday Agreement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_11 -->
2022年5月，英国外交大臣表示，如果政府能够达成符合其保护《贝尔法斯特耶稣受难日协议》目标的协调安排，拟议法案就将赋予实施修订版《北爱尔兰议定书》的权力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_11 -->
<!-- USE:U001_12 -->
## accommodation /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən/ n. · 眼睛对焦的调节
`眼睛通过改变晶状体的厚度和曲率，使不同距离的物体在视网膜上清晰成像的自动调节；不是住所或心理迁就。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_12 USE:U001_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"眼睛对焦的调节","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/16593/pg16593-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_12 -->
The eye changes the shape of its lens when we shift our view between near and distant objects.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_12_2 USE:U001_12 -->
This ability to adjust to different distances is called accommodation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_12 -->
当我们把视线在近处与远处的物体之间转换时，眼睛会改变晶状体的形状。这种针对不同距离进行调节的能力称为眼调节。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_12 -->
<!-- USE:U001_13 -->
## accommodation address /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən əˈdrɛs/ n. · 代收信件的通信地址
`英式用语，供不能或不愿提供固定通信地址的人收取信件的地址；并不保证本人住在那里。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_13 USE:U001_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"代收信件的通信地址","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/compulsory-purchase-process-guidance/guidance-on-the-compulsory-purchase-process"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_13 USE:U001_13 -->
For compulsory purchase notices in England, an accommodation address can be used only if the acquiring authority has checked that the person supplied the address or authorised service in that way.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_13_2 -->
Where known, the person’s home or current address should also be shown.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_13 -->
在英格兰送达强制征购通知时，征购机关只有确认受送达人提供了该代收通信地址，或授权按这种方式送达，才能使用这个地址。如已知其住址或现住址，也应一并列明。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_13 -->
<!-- USE:U001_14 -->
## accommodation bill /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən bɪl/ n. · 通融融资票据
`金融语境中的通融票据，用他人信用帮助融资；不是旅馆开出的住宿账单。其核心是信用融通，而非把日常住宿费记成票据。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_14 USE:U001_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"通融融资票据","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/2860/pg2860-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_14 -->
Mark Robarts said he had received neither money nor money’s worth from the bills, but had signed them to oblige a friend.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_14_2 USE:U001_14 -->
The dean could not understand why a clergyman would sign accommodation bills that he could not pay when they fell due.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_14 -->
马克·罗巴茨说，自己没有从这些票据中得到钱款或等值利益，签名只是为了帮朋友。教长不明白，一个牧师为何会在自己到期无力偿付的通融票据上签字。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_14 -->
<!-- USE:U001_15 -->
## accommodation ladder /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən ˈlædɚ/ n. · 登轮舷梯
`航海用语，沿船侧供人从小船等处登上大船的梯道；不是船舱内的普通楼梯。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_15 USE:U001_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"登轮舷梯","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/maib-reports/person-overboard-from-container-vessel-varmland-while-alongside-at-hamburg-germany-with-loss-of-1-life"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_15 USE:U001_15 -->
In November 2005, an able seaman fell from the upper platform of Varmland’s starboard accommodation ladder while the ship was in Hamburg.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_15_2 -->
He disappeared and was presumed dead.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_15 -->
2005年11月，瓦姆兰号在汉堡港时，一名一等水手从船舶右舷舷梯的上平台坠落。他落水后失踪，被推定死亡。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_15 -->
<!-- USE:U001_16 -->
## accommodation road /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən roʊd/ n. · 通向处所的专用通路
`英式用语，为不直接临公共道路的处所提供通达条件的道路；重点是出入通路，不是为旅馆命名的道路，也不能仅凭词义断定公众有通行权。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_16 USE:U001_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"通向处所的专用通路","sourceUrls":["https://assets.publishing.service.gov.uk/media/5a80afbfe5274a2e87dbb3f4/fps_l3055_7_86.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_16 USE:U001_16 -->
A route might have been excluded from the taxable land parcels on the Finance Act maps because an accommodation road served several landholdings in different ownerships.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_16_2 USE:U001_16 -->
A public right of way could still be acquired even if the route was an accommodation road.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_16 -->
一条路可能因为是供不同所有人的若干地块出入的专用通路，而没有被计入《财政法》地图上的土地估税单元。即使某条路是这种专用通路，也并不妨碍公共通行权的取得。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_16 -->
<!-- USE:U001_17 -->
## accommodationist /əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃənɪst/ n. · 寻求妥协协调的人
`美式用语，尤指在政治关系中寻求调解或妥协的人；不是负责订住宿的工作人员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_17 USE:U001_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"寻求妥协协调的人","sourceUrls":["https://www.nps.gov/people/jane-edna-hunter.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_17 -->
In Cleveland, Jane Edna Hunter sought to reconcile Black women’s needs with the policies of white supporters funding her association.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_17_2 USE:U001_17 -->
Her Black critics called her an accommodationist, like her hero Booker T. Washington.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_17 -->
在克利夫兰，简·埃德娜·亨特试图协调黑人女性的需要与出资支持其协会的白人支持者的方针。她的黑人批评者把她称为主张妥协协调的人，就像她所推崇的布克·T.华盛顿一样。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_17 -->
<!-- USE:U001_18 -->
## sheltered accommodation /ˈʃɛltɚd əˌkɑməˈdeɪʃən/ n. · 有支援设施的独立住所
`为老人或残障人士提供的住宅形式，住户各有独立居住单元，同时可使用部分共用设施并获得管理员、医疗等支援。不能一概理解为集体病房或全天护理院。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_18 USE:U001_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"有支援设施的独立住所","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/housing-benefit-guidance-for-supported-housing-claims/housing-benefit-guidance-for-supported-housing-claims"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_18 USE:U001_18 -->
The guidance says that, when completing a Universal Credit claim, a claimant who believes they live in supported housing should declare, "I’m in supported or sheltered accommodation."
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_18_2 -->
The local authority then determines whether the accommodation meets the relevant definition.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_18 -->
指南说，填写统一福利金申请时，认为自己住在支持性住房中的申请人应申报：“我住在支持性住房或有支援设施的独立住所中。”然后由地方当局判定该住所是否符合相应定义。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_18 -->
<!-- USE:U001_19 -->
## unaccommodating /ˌʌnəˈkɑmədeɪtɪŋ/ adj. · 不肯帮忙通融
`形容人或态度不愿提供便利、配合或照顾他人需要，与 accommodating 相反。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C001_19 USE:U001_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"不肯帮忙通融","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/2834/pg2834-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_19 -->
Isabel assured Mr. Goodwood that she would remain his friend after her marriage.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_19_2 -->
“That will make it all the worse,” he answered grimly.
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_19_3 USE:U001_19 -->
“Ah, you’re unaccommodating!
<!-- SENTENCE:S001_19_4 -->
I can’t promise to dislike you in order to help you to resign yourself.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C001_19 -->
伊莎贝尔向古德伍德先生保证，自己结婚后仍会是他的好朋友。“那只会更糟，”他冷冷地回答。“啊，你可真不肯通融！我总不能为了让你接受现实，就答应讨厌你吧。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C001_19 -->

<!-- WORD:W002 -->
# acknowledge

**词源**：acknowledge 的词形演变说明它由中古英语表示“承认”的旧形式与 knowlechen 相互影响而来，后者关联 knowledge。记忆可抓住“认出之后表示承认”：现代词既能承认事实，也能认可身份、确认收件或向人致意；不能把每个用法都直译成“知道”。

<!-- USE:U002_01 -->
## acknowledge /əkˈnɑlɪdʒ/ v. · 承认事实存在或属实
`较正式，承认事实、问题或情况为真或存在，未必乐于承认。可接名词、that 从句，或 acknowledge somebody/something to be…、as…；说 I acknowledge that… 不等于仅仅“知道”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_01 USE:U002_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"承认事实存在或属实","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_01 -->
"You quite mistook Mr. Darcy," Elizabeth told her mother.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_01_2 USE:U002_01 -->
"He only meant that there was not such a variety of people to be met with in the country as in town, which you must acknowledge to be true."
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_01 -->
“你完全误会达西先生了，”伊丽莎白对母亲说。“他只是说，乡下能遇见的人不像城里那么多样，这一点你必须承认是真的。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_01 -->
<!-- USE:U002_02 -->
## acknowledge /əkˈnɑlɪdʒ/ v. · 认可地位能力或效力
`认可人的成就、资格、身份或某事的有效性，可用 acknowledge somebody as/to be…，常见被动 be acknowledged as；也可直接接 legitimacy 等，不只承认事实存在。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_02 USE:U002_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"认可地位能力或效力","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/105/pg105-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_02 -->
Elizabeth had liked Mr Elliot, especially because he was her father’s heir.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_02_2 USE:U002_02 -->
There was not a baronet from A to Z whom her feelings could have so willingly acknowledged as an equal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_02 -->
伊丽莎白曾喜欢埃利奥特先生，尤其因为他是她父亲的继承人。在所有从A排到Z的从男爵中，没有一个能让她像对他那样，心甘情愿地视为与自己地位相当的人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_02 -->
<!-- USE:U002_03 -->
## acknowledge /əkˈnɑlɪdʒ/ v. · 确认已经收到
`确认收到信件、消息、申请等，通常通过回信或通知；不保证已经处理或同意内容，acknowledging receipt 与 substantive reply 有别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_03 USE:U002_03 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"确认已经收到","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/38776/pg38776-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_03 USE:U002_03 -->
I have hideously delayed to acknowledge your so interesting letter from Paris, and now the manner of my response does little to repair the missing grace of my silence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_03 -->
我竟拖延了这么久才确认收到你从巴黎寄来的那封极有意思的信，而我如今这样回信，也实在难以弥补沉默所欠的礼数。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_03 -->
<!-- USE:U002_04 -->
## acknowledge /əkˈnɑlɪdʒ/ v. · 示意注意到某人
`通过点头、微笑、声音等表示注意到或认出了某人，直接接人或其到来；不一定是在感谢。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_04 USE:U002_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"示意注意到某人","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/700/pg700-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_04 USE:U002_04 -->
Mr. Swiveller acknowledged the woman’s presence with a nod.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_04_2 -->
He then struggled into his tight jacket before taking his seat at the desk.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_04 -->
斯威夫勒先生点头表示注意到了那个女人。接着，他费力地穿上那件紧身外套，才坐到书桌旁。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_04 -->
<!-- USE:U002_05 -->
## acknowledge /əkˈnɑlɪdʒ/ v. · 对帮助或赞赏致意
`用言语或动作对服务、好意、掌声、赞扬等表示感谢或赞赏，可用 acknowledge something with a bow 等；与仅确认看到某人有别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_05 USE:U002_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"对帮助或赞赏致意","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/882/pg882-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_05 USE:U002_05 -->
Mrs Tippin condescendingly acknowledged the clapping of hands and shouts of “bravo!” that greeted her appearance.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_05_2 -->
She then began to sing “Bid me discourse”, accompanied on the piano by Mr Tippin.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_05 -->
蒂平太太带着居高临下的姿态，向迎接她出场的掌声和“好！”的喝彩致意。随后，她开始演唱《请让我倾诉》，由蒂平先生在钢琴上伴奏。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_05 -->
<!-- USE:U002_06 -->
## acknowledgeable /əkˈnɑlɪdʒəbəl/ adj. · 可以承认或认可的
`表示能够被承认、认可或确认；-able 表可能性，不是“知识渊博”的 knowledgeable。需依所修饰对象判断具体承认什么。技术语境也可表示“可确认接收的”：下列2006年DCCP网络协议原句限定的是包头处理，不表示应用数据也已处理成功。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_06 USE:U002_06 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可以承认或认可的","sourceUrls":["https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc4340.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_06 USE:U002_06 -->
A received packet is classified as acknowledgeable if and only if its header was successfully processed by the receiving DCCP.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_06 -->
一个收到的数据包，当且仅当其包头已被DCCP接收端成功处理时，才被归为可确认接收的数据包。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_06 -->
<!-- USE:U002_07 -->
## acknowledged /əkˈnɑlɪdʒd/ adj. · 获得承认或公认的
`来自过去分词，作形容词表示事实已获承认，或能力、身份获得认可；可说 an acknowledged risk/expert。含义随修饰对象而定，不暗示一定值得赞许。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_07 USE:U002_07 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"获得承认或公认的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_07 USE:U002_07 -->
It is a truth universally acknowledged, that a single man in possession of a good fortune must be in want of a wife.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_07 -->
凡是拥有可观财产的单身男子，必定需要一位妻子——这被公认为一条真理。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_07 -->
<!-- USE:U002_08 -->
## acknowledgement /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n.; acknowledgment /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n. · 对事实的承认
`两种拼写意义相同。通常作单数，指承认某事存在或属实的言行，常说 an acknowledgement that…、acknowledgement of…；不是未经证实的猜测。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_08 USE:U002_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"对事实的承认","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_08_intro -->
Jane had asked to use the carriage.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_08 USE:U002_08 -->
Elizabeth finally extracted from her father an acknowledgment that the horses were already engaged.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_08_2 -->
Jane therefore had to go on horseback.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_08 -->
简本想坐马车去。伊丽莎白终于从父亲那里逼问出一个承认：马已经另有用处。因此简不得不骑马前往。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_08 -->
<!-- USE:U002_09 -->
## acknowledgement /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n.; acknowledgment /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n. · 示意认出了对方
`指点头、微笑等表示注意到对方的动作；常用 in acknowledgement，通常不可数，也可 a gesture/an acknowledgement。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_09 USE:U002_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"示意认出了对方","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/700/pg700-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_09 USE:U002_09 -->
"She has actually passed me without the slightest acknowledgment of my presence!" the governess cried.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_09 -->
“她竟然就这样从我身边走过去，丝毫没有表示注意到我的存在！”那位女教师叫道。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_09 -->
<!-- USE:U002_10 -->
## acknowledgement /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n.; acknowledgment /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n. · 致谢声明
`表达感谢、赞赏或承认他人帮助的声明，可数或不可数；可用于公开感谢捐助、许可或服务，并非仅作者书后栏目。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_10 USE:U002_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"致谢声明","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_10 USE:U002_10 -->
Mr. Bennet wrote a brief, grateful acknowledgment of his brother-in-law’s kindness and confirmed his approval of the arrangements made for him.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_10 -->
班纳特先生简短地写信感谢妻子的兄弟的好意，并确认自己赞同对方为他作出的安排。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_10 -->
<!-- USE:U002_11 -->
## acknowledgement /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n.; acknowledgment /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n. · 答礼与回报
`为感谢某项服务而给予的东西或作出的行动；常见 in acknowledgement of，重点是回报，不只是印出来的一句感谢。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_11 USE:U002_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"答礼与回报","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/700/pg700-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_11 USE:U002_11 -->
The gentleman tried to press an acknowledgment of their kindness to the friendless child upon the bride and bridegroom.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_11_2 -->
They steadily refused to accept it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_11 -->
那位先生想硬把一份答谢礼送给新郎新娘，感谢他们善待那个无依无靠的孩子。但两人始终不肯收下。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_11 -->
<!-- USE:U002_12 -->
## acknowledgements /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənts/ n.; acknowledgments /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənts/ n. · 著作中的致谢部分
`通常用复数，指作者在书中感谢给予帮助者的致谢文字或栏目；不是多封收件回执的唯一解释，仍须看语境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_12 USE:U002_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"著作中的致谢部分","sourceUrls":["https://service-manual.ons.gov.uk/content/content-types/articles"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_12 USE:U002_12 -->
ONS publications should not include an acknowledgements section crediting individual ONS authors, because the organisation presents its content collectively.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_12 -->
英国国家统计局的出版物不应设置致谢栏来列出本机构各位作者的贡献，因为该机构以集体名义呈现内容。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_12 -->
<!-- USE:U002_13 -->
## acknowledgement /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n.; acknowledgment /əkˈnɑlɪdʒmənt/ n. · 确认收件的回执
`可数，通知发件人信件、申请或其他材料已经收到的回信或消息；回执不等于对内容作了答复。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_13 USE:U002_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"确认收件的回执","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/organisations/cabinet-office/about/complaints-procedure"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_13 USE:U002_13 -->
The Cabinet Office’s complaints procedure sets target times of five working days for an acknowledgement and twenty working days for a full response.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_13_2 -->
If a very complex complaint requires more time, the complainant will be kept informed of progress, the reasons for the delay and the new deadline.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_13 -->
英国内阁办公厅的投诉程序列明的目标时限为：五个工作日内发出收件确认，二十个工作日内作出完整答复。如果某项非常复杂的投诉需要更多时间，投诉人会获知调查进展、延期原因和新的截止日期。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_13 -->
<!-- USE:U002_14 -->
## unacknowledged /ˌʌnəkˈnɑlɪdʒd/ adj. · 未被注意或正视的
`指存在、到来或感受未被注意、承认或正视，常在名词前；可能是本人未意识到，也可能是他人忽略，不能一概解释成官方拒绝承认。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_14 USE:U002_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未被注意或正视的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/55123/pg55123-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_14 -->
I was hiding an uncanny secret.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_14_2 USE:U002_14 -->
Perhaps my real reason for objecting to Gwendolen’s presence was my fear, unacknowledged to myself, that I should be tempted eventually to tell her the amazing tale of Jack’s ridiculous reincarnation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_14 -->
我心里藏着一个离奇的秘密。也许我反对格温多琳来住的真正原因，是一种连我自己都不承认的担心：到头来，我会忍不住把杰克那个荒唐的转世故事告诉她。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_14 -->
<!-- USE:U002_15 -->
## unacknowledged /ˌʌnəkˈnɑlɪdʒd/ adj. · 未获公开或正式认可的
`存在、重要性、贡献或身份未获正式、公开承认；不一定完全没人知道。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_15 USE:U002_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未获公开或正式认可的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_15 USE:U002_15 -->
The acknowledged lovers talked and laughed; the unacknowledged were silent.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_15_2 -->
Darcy did not show his happiness in laughter, and Elizabeth was agitated and confused.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_15 -->
已经公开关系的恋人谈笑着；尚未公开关系的那一对却沉默着。达西并不以欢笑流露喜悦，伊丽莎白则激动又慌乱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_15 -->
<!-- USE:U002_16 -->
## unacknowledged /ˌʌnəkˈnɑlɪdʒd/ adj. · 尚未确认收到的
`用于信件或请求等，表示没有回执或收件确认；不能据此认定对方没有收到。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_16 USE:U002_16 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"尚未确认收到的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/38776/pg38776-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_16 USE:U002_16 -->
I have had an unacknowledged letter from you longer than is decorous.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_16_2 -->
But I have shown you ere this that epistolary decorum is a virtue I have ceased to pretend to.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_16 -->
你的一封信我一直没有回，拖得有失礼数了。不过，我以前就让你看出来了：书信上的礼数已经是我不再假装具有的美德。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_16 -->
<!-- USE:U002_17 -->
## unacknowledged /ˌʌnəkˈnɑlɪdʒd/ adj. · 未获答谢的
`用于好意、服务或礼物等，表示没有得到感谢或答礼，与消息未获收件确认分开理解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_17 USE:U002_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未获答谢的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/38776/pg38776-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_17 USE:U002_17 -->
I have, as usual, endless unacknowledged benefits to thank you for after too many days.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_17_2 -->
The last is your letter of the end of March, full of interesting substance as always.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_17 -->
像往常一样，拖了太多天以后，我又有数不清尚未答谢的好意要感谢你。最近的一项就是你三月底的来信，和往常一样满是有意思的内容。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_17 -->
<!-- USE:U002_18 -->
## unacknowledged /ˌʌnəkˈnɑlɪdʒd/ adj. · 尚未承认或供认的
`指事实、责任、过错或罪行尚未被当事人承认；用于罪行时可指未供认，不等于法律上已经判定无罪。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C002_18 USE:U002_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"尚未承认或供认的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/17763/pg17763-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_18 -->
The district attorney, the chief inspector and their companions arrived at Mr Roberts’ house.
<!-- SENTENCE:S002_18_2 USE:U002_18 -->
Seeing him absorbed in its new furnishings, more than one of them began to doubt whether a man burdened with unacknowledged guilt could take such pleasure in his surroundings.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C002_18 -->
地方检察官、警察总督察和同行者来到罗伯茨先生家。看他兴致勃勃地欣赏新家具，他们中不止一人开始怀疑：一个背负着未曾供认的罪责的人，会这样高兴地欣赏自己的居所吗？
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C002_18 -->

<!-- WORD:W003 -->
# advocate

**词源**：advocate 经古法语进入英语，追溯至拉丁语 advocatus，原为 advocare“召来作证或提供意见”的分词；ad- 表向，vocare 表呼唤。由“为别人被召来发声”联想倡导者和辩护者；后来名词也用作动词，两者在现代英语中末尾读音不同。

<!-- USE:U003_01 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkeɪt/ v. · 公开主张支持
`较正式，公开建议或以论据支持某项行动、政策或事业，直接接名词或动名词，advocate doing something；不是 advocate somebody to do 的一般劝说句式。recommend 偏建议，support 偏支持，champion 强调积极维护，encourage 可侧重促成或鼓励。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_01 USE:U003_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"公开主张支持","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6473/pg6473-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_01 USE:U003_01 -->
Phrase the debate proposition so that the affirmative side is the one to advocate a change in existing conditions or belief.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_01_2 -->
That side must provide evidence and reasoning before it can demand an answer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_01 -->
拟定辩题时，应让正方成为主张改变现有状况或信念的一方。这一方必须先提出证据和推理，才能要求对方作答。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_01 -->
<!-- USE:U003_02 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkeɪt/ v. · 为人或主张辩护
`及物动词，用论辩维护、抗辩或为某方辩护；不必在法庭上，也不只是提出一项新政策。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_02 USE:U003_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"为人或主张辩护","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/722/pg722-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_02 -->
The biographer praises James Otis for refusing to win cases by petty tricks.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_02_2 USE:U003_02 -->
He undertook to advocate no cause that he did not truly and fully believe in.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_02 -->
传记作者称赞詹姆斯·奥蒂斯不肯靠小伎俩打赢官司。他不会接手为自己并不真正、完全信服的诉求辩护。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_02 -->
<!-- USE:U003_03 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkət/ n. · 公开支持某主张的人
`较正式的可数名词，指支持并为某项政策、行动等发声的人，常用 an advocate of…，strong/leading advocate。名词末尾读/ət/，不同于动词/eɪt/；supporter、proponent 偏支持者或倡议者，champion 更突出积极捍卫。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_03 USE:U003_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"公开支持某主张的人","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6473/pg6473-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_03 -->
Avoid an appearance of superiority or a desire for personal glory.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_03_2 USE:U003_03 -->
The arguer should remember that the cause is greater than its advocate.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_03 -->
要避免显得高人一等，或一心追求个人荣耀。论辩者应记得：所维护的事业比倡导者本人更重要。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_03 -->
<!-- USE:U003_04 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkət/ n. · 为某群体利益发声的人
`为另一人或某群体争取利益、陈述需求的代表或支持者，常用 an advocate for…；群体服务义在美式英语中常见，不能一律理解为持律师资格者。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_04 USE:U003_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"为某群体利益发声的人","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/care-act-2014-supporting-implementation/care-and-support-statutory-guidance"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_04 USE:U003_04 -->
England’s published Care Act guidance expects independent advocates to decide how best to support and represent the people they are helping, always having regard to their wellbeing and interests, including their views, beliefs and wishes.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_04 -->
英格兰已公布的《照护法》指导文件提出，独立倡导代表应决定如何最好地支持和代表受助者，并始终顾及受助者的福祉与利益，包括他们的看法、信念和意愿。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_04 -->
<!-- USE:U003_05 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkət/ n. · 出庭代理的律师
`法律语境，代表一方在法庭陈述、论辩的专业律师，可为控方或辩方；苏格兰的 advocate 特指其制度中的出庭律师，不能机械套成各国相同的职业资格。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_05 USE:U003_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"法庭辩护律师","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/98/pg98-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_05 USE:U003_05 -->
At the Bar, Stryver had once been thought to lack a skill essential to the advocate: extracting the heart of a case from a heap of statements.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_05 -->
在律师界，斯特莱弗一度被认为缺少出庭律师必备的一项本领：从堆积的陈述中抓住案件要点。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_05 -->
<!-- USE:U003_06 -->
## advocacy /ˈædvəkəsi/ n. · 对事业或政策的倡导
`较正式，公开支持或以论据主张某项事业、政策的行为，常用 advocacy of…；可说 somebody’s advocacy，重点是倡导活动而非倡导者本人。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_06 USE:U003_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"对事业或政策的倡导","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/11557/pg11557-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_06 USE:U003_06 -->
Morley challenges the advocacy of a dual doctrine in which a minority keeps what it believes true while leaving the majority with false beliefs.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_06_2 -->
He argues that error is not useful in itself, while treating the timing of reform as a separate question.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_06 -->
莫利质疑一种双重教义的倡导：少数人保留自己认为真实的见解，却让多数人继续持有错误信念。他主张，错误本身没有益处，但应把何时改革视为另一个问题。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_06 -->
<!-- USE:U003_07 -->
## advocacy /ˈædvəkəsi/ n. · 法庭论辩与代理工作
`指 advocate 的出庭论辩或代理职能，尤其律师在法庭处理案件、提出论辩的工作或方式，通常不可数；可涉及控方或辩方，也可依语境指为他人陈述利益的代理职能。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_07 USE:U003_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"法庭辩论与代理工作","sourceUrls":["https://www.cps.gov.uk/publication/cps-briefing-principles"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_07 USE:U003_07 -->
The Crown Prosecution Service requires both in-house and external advocates to deliver high-quality advocacy.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_07_2 -->
Effective advocates present the prosecution case clearly, concisely and persuasively, and probe and challenge the defence case.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_07 -->
英国皇家检察署要求内部和外聘的出庭律师都提供高质量的法庭论辩服务。称职的出庭律师要清晰、简明、有说服力地陈述控方案情，并深入审查和质疑辩方的论证。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_07 -->
<!-- USE:U003_08 -->
## advocacy /ˈædvəkəsi/ n. · 影响公共决策的倡议活动
`美式英语中常作名词定语，如 advocacy group/organization，指试图影响政府或其他当局决策的团体所做的倡议活动；定语位置不使 advocacy 变成形容词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_08 USE:U003_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"影响公共决策的倡议活动","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/consultations/office-for-students-fee-charging-model"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_08 USE:U003_08 -->
In its May 2026 consultation on fees charged to higher education providers in England, the Department for Education said the proposals might also interest student and advocacy groups.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_08_2 -->
It welcomed considered feedback to help shape any changes to the Office for Students’ fee structure.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_08 -->
英国教育部在2026年5月就英格兰高等教育机构缴纳的费用开展咨询时表示，学生团体和倡议团体也可能对这些提案感兴趣。教育部欢迎经过认真考虑的反馈，以帮助形成对高等教育监管机构Office for Students收费结构的任何调整。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_08 -->
<!-- USE:U003_09 -->
## advocateship /ˈædvəkətʃɪp/ n. · 倡导或辩护者的职位身份
`-ship 表身份、职位或职能，指担任 advocate 的身份或工作；具体是法律职位还是一般辩护职能由语境决定，不等于抽象的支持意见。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_09 USE:U003_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"倡导或辩护者的职位身份","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/18775/pg18775-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_09 -->
In 1830, Jeffrey became Lord Advocate for Scotland when the Whigs came to power.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_09_2 USE:U003_09 -->
Yet, the reviewer reports, citing Lord Cockburn, Jeffrey would have avoided the advocateship if he could.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_09_3 -->
He accepted it only from a feeling of duty to his party.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_09 -->
1830年，辉格党上台后，杰弗里出任苏格兰总检察长。可是，书评作者援引科克本勋爵的说法称，杰弗里如果能够推辞，本会避开这一职位。他接受任命，仅仅是出于对自己政党的责任感。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_09 -->
<!-- USE:U003_10 -->
## devil’s advocate /ˈdɛvəlz ˈædvəkət/ n. · 为检验观点而唱反调的人
`在讨论中故意提出不同或反对的意见，以检验论点、推动讨论；本人未必认同反方观点。常说 play devil’s advocate，不指恶意反对一切的人。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_10 USE:U003_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"为检验观点而唱反调的人","sourceUrls":["https://assets.publishing.service.gov.uk/media/6970f2c7011505255b2d430d/mergers_guidance_cma_jurisdiction_procedure_1.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_10 USE:U003_10 -->
For more complex phase 1 merger cases, the CMA’s December 2025 guidance assigns a staff member outside the case team to act as a devil’s advocate, critically examining the recommendation for or against a phase 2 investigation.
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_10_2 -->
The challenger also attends the issues meeting wherever possible.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_10 -->
对于竞争问题较复杂的第一阶段并购案件，英国竞争与市场管理局2025年12月的指导文件指定一名不属于案件团队的工作人员来扮演“故意唱反调者”，批判性地审查团队关于是否转入第二阶段调查的建议。这位质疑者还会尽可能参加问题讨论会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_10 -->
<!-- USE:U003_11 -->
## devil’s advocate /ˈdɛvəlz ˈædvəkət/ n. · 历史上的列圣审查员
`天主教历史用语，指受命对拟议的宣福、封圣等提出反证或质疑的人；这是制度中的审查角色，不是把普通讨论者认作宗教官员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C003_11 USE:U003_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"历史上的列圣审查员","sourceUrls":["https://www.newadvent.org/cathen/01168b.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S003_11 USE:U003_11 -->
In the 1907 Catholic Encyclopedia account, the official popularly called the devil’s advocate had to prepare written arguments against proposed beatification or canonization and insist that objections were considered.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C003_11 -->
在1907年《天主教百科全书》的记述中，俗称“列圣审查员”的官员必须准备反对拟议宣福或封圣的书面论据，并坚持要求审查各种异议。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C003_11 -->

<!-- WORD:W004 -->
# arbitrary

**词源**：arbitrary 追溯到拉丁语 arbitrarius，与 arbiter“裁断者、仲裁人”相关。现代 arbitrary 常批评决定只凭个人意志、缺少客观原则；同源关系不表示它具有“公正仲裁”的褒义。

<!-- USE:U004_01 -->
## arbitrary /ˈɑrbətrɛri/ adj. · 缺乏原则的任意决定
`决定、规则、选择等没有明确原则、计划或体系，凭个人意见或随意选择，常带不赞成，尤其暗示不公平。技术讨论也可强调选择不受特定理由支配，是否贬斥须看语境，不等于经过统计设计的随机抽样。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C004_01 USE:U004_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"缺乏原则的任意决定","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/40983/pg40983-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_01 USE:U004_01 -->
Changes of opinion may look arbitrary to a casual observer, the anonymous writer argues.
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_01_2 -->
Someone who traces the associations influencing the mind, however, can see them as natural rather than without cause.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C004_01 -->
这位匿名作者认为，在粗略观察者看来，意见的变化可能显得随意。但如果追溯影响头脑的联想，就能把这些变化理解为自然发生，而非毫无缘由。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C004_01 -->
<!-- USE:U004_02 -->
## arbitrary /ˈɑrbətrɛri/ adj. · 专断而不受制约的
`用于权力、统治或行为，指不受法律或合理限制而由个人意志支配，具有专制、暴虐意味；比单纯“随便挑一个”更强。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C004_02 USE:U004_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"专断而不受制约的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/7370/pg7370-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_02 USE:U004_02 -->
In Locke’s account of the state of nature, power over an offender is not absolute or arbitrary power to act on anger or personal will.
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_02_2 -->
Punishment must be proportionate to the offence and directed to reparation and restraint.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C004_02 -->
在洛克对自然状态的论述中，对违法者的权力并不是可以任凭怒气或个人意志行事的绝对、专断权力。惩罚须与过错相称，并以补偿和防止再犯为目的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C004_02 -->
<!-- USE:U004_03 -->
## arbitrarily /ˌɑrbəˈtrɛrəli/ adv. · 任意或专断地
`修饰选择、设定、拘押等行为，表示缺少原则、根据或制约；可表示随意选定，也可强调专横不公，依动作判断，不把否定句 were not chosen arbitrarily 读成随机选择。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C004_03 USE:U004_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"任意或专断地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/7370/pg7370-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_03 USE:U004_03 -->
Locke rejects the idea that a legislature may do whatever it wishes and dispose of subjects’ estates arbitrarily, taking property at pleasure.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C004_03 -->
洛克反对这样的想法：立法权可以为所欲为，任意处置臣民的财产，随意拿走其中任何部分。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C004_03 -->
<!-- USE:U004_04 -->
## arbitrariness /ˈɑrbətrɛrinəs/ n. · 任意性与专断
`不可数，缺乏原则或依据的性质，也可指权力不受合理制约的专断；不是“仲裁”的 arbitration。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C004_04 USE:U004_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"任意性与专断","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/organisations/government-legal-department/about/complaints-procedure"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_04 USE:U004_04 -->
The Government Legal Department’s complaints procedure cites the 1966 Crossman list of possible maladministration, including bias, neglect, delay and arbitrariness.
<!-- SENTENCE:S004_04_2 -->
The list gives examples of what the Ombudsman might find, rather than deciding any individual complaint.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C004_04 -->
政府法律部门的投诉程序援引1966年的克罗斯曼清单，列举可能构成行政失当的情形，包括偏见、疏忽、拖延和专断。清单只是给出监察专员可能作此认定的例子，并不替任何具体投诉作出决定。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C004_04 -->

<!-- WORD:W005 -->
# assume

**词源**：assume 源于拉丁语 assumere“拿起、接过”，由表示“向”的 ad- 与 sumere“取”构成。可用“接过来”串联接任职责、采用姿态和暂时接受一个前提；assumption 的宗教义保留“被接上去”的历史联系，但现代各义不能互换。

<!-- USE:U005_01 -->
## assume /əˈsum/ v. · 未经证实便认为
`把某事视为真实，有时会错。可接 that 从句、assume somebody/something to be…，被动 be assumed to…，或答话 assume so；没有证据的预设不同于从证据推出结论。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_01 USE:U005_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未经证实便认为","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6473/pg6473-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_01 USE:U005_01 -->
It is never safe to assume that the hearer or reader of an argument will struggle through obscure reasoning.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_01_2 -->
Make the route to your conclusion clear.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_01 -->
绝不能想当然地以为，听众或读者会费力穿过含糊的推理。要把通向结论的路径讲清楚。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_01 -->
<!-- USE:U005_02 -->
## assume /əˈsum/ v. · 承担职务责任
`开始承担某项职务、角色、权力或责任，直接接 responsibility、office、control 等；强调接过职责，不自动暗示非法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_02 USE:U005_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"承担职务责任","sourceUrls":["https://www.orr.gov.uk/search-news/paul-smith-appointed-orr-chief-executive-officer"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_02 USE:U005_02 -->
In its 29 September 2026 announcement, the Office of Rail and Road said Paul Smith would assume office as chief executive on 4 January 2027.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_02_2 -->
Feras Alshaker would continue as interim chief executive until then.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_02 -->
英国铁路与公路办公室在2026年9月29日的公告中说，保罗·史密斯将于2027年1月4日出任首席执行官。在此之前，费拉斯·阿尔沙克将继续担任临时首席执行官。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_02 -->
<!-- USE:U005_03 -->
## assume /əˈsum/ v. · 开始呈现某种性质
`事物开始具有某种形态、性质或重要性，接 an aspect/form/importance 等；不含事物在有意假装的意思。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_03 USE:U005_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"开始呈现某种性质","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/12299/pg12299-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_03 -->
A torsion test twists a wooden shaft by turning its ends in opposite directions or holding one end fixed and turning the other.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_03_2 USE:U005_03 -->
All the fibres except those on the axis tend to assume the form of helices.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_03 -->
扭转试验通过把木轴两端向相反方向转动，或固定一端、转动另一端，来使木轴扭转。除轴线处的纤维外，所有纤维都倾向于呈现螺旋线状。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_03 -->
<!-- USE:U005_04 -->
## assume /əˈsum/ v. · 故意摆出或假装
`有意识地装出某种表情、语气、态度，或假装具有某状态，接 an air、a tone、ignorance 等；区别于事物自然呈现性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_04 USE:U005_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"故意摆出或假装","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/41020/pg41020-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_04 -->
The narrator found the woman’s superior manner disgusting.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_04_2 USE:U005_04 -->
When an air of deep pity for the child’s ignorance was assumed on top of it, it became unbearable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_04 -->
叙述者觉得那个女人高高在上的态度令人厌恶。当她又在这种态度之上装出一副深深同情孩子无知的样子时，就更加令人无法忍受了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_04 -->
<!-- USE:U005_05 -->
## assume /əˈsum/ v. · 妄自攫取权力资格
`把本不属于自己的权力、功劳或资格据为己有，常用 assume something to oneself；含僭越、冒称或篡夺意味，不能混同正当接任。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_05 USE:U005_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"妄自攫取权力资格","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/7370/pg7370-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_05 USE:U005_05 -->
The legislature cannot assume to itself a power to rule by improvised, arbitrary decrees, Locke argues.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_05_2 -->
It is bound to administer justice through established, publicly declared laws.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_05 -->
洛克认为，立法机关不能妄自取得以临时、专断法令统治的权力，而应受已确立、已公布法律的约束来施行司法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_05 -->
<!-- USE:U005_06 -->
## let’s assume /lɛts əˈsum/ v.; let us assume /lɛt əs əˈsum/ v. · 设定一种假想前提
`讨论中先把某情况暂作前提，再推演后果，后接从句；不是声称它已经真实发生。let’s 较口语，let us 可较正式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_06 USE:U005_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"设定一种假想前提","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_06 -->
Let us suppose that the salaried manager of a hat factory sets up his own business.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_06_2 USE:U005_06 -->
Let us assume that he has no money but can borrow enough to equip and operate it.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_06_3 -->
This artificial case separates the business organiser’s work from ownership of land and capital.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_06 -->
设想一位领薪的帽厂经理创办自己的企业。先假定他没有资金，但能借到足够的钱来置办设备并经营企业。这个人为构造的案例把企业组织者的工作与土地、资本的所有权区分开来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_06 -->
<!-- USE:U005_07 -->
## assumable /əˈsuməbəl/ adj. · 可由他人承接的
`指职务、义务或贷款等可以由另一方接过来承担。可作定语，如 an assumable mortgage，也可作表语并用 by 引出承接者；贷款语境说的是由新借款人接续原贷款，不是新发放一笔贷款。可承接不等于已经转移，也不表示任何人都无需满足相关条件便可承接。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_07 USE:U005_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可由他人承接的","sourceUrls":["https://www.consumerfinance.gov/rules-policy/regulations/1026/37/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_07 USE:U005_07 -->
The CFPB’s Loan Estimate interpretation recognises that a lender may not know whether a loan will be assumable on its original terms if the property is later sold or transferred.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_07_2 -->
Where assumption is conditional or uncertain, the disclosure must say that a third party may be allowed to take over the loan under certain conditions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_07 -->
美国消费者金融保护局对贷款估算披露的解释指出，贷款人在披露时，可能无法判断：相关房产将来出售或转让时，该笔贷款是否可由他人按原条款承接。当承接取决于特定条件或尚不确定时，披露应说明：在特定条件下，可能允许第三方承接该贷款。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_07 -->
<!-- USE:U005_08 -->
## assumed /əˈsumd/ adj. · 被假定为真的
`过去分词作定语，指被预先当作真的条件、数值或事实，未必已验证。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_08 USE:U005_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"被假定为真的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/45954/old/45954-h/45954-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_08 -->
Sumner agrees that the towns of Massachusetts have been schools of freedom.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_08_2 USE:U005_08 -->
But he refuses to attribute their influence to the assumed fact that every town had been entitled to a legislative representative: at the Revolution, that was true of most towns, not all.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_08 -->
萨姆纳同意，马萨诸塞州的各城镇曾是自由的学校。但他不肯把它们的影响归因于这样一个被当成事实的前提：每个城镇都有权选派一名立法机关代表；在美国革命时期，这对多数城镇成立，却并非所有城镇都如此。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_08 -->
<!-- USE:U005_09 -->
## assumed /əˈsumd/ adj. · 装出来或借用的
`指故意采用而非本来的表情、态度或身份等；assumed confidence 是装出的自信，assumed name 是化名，不是猜测的名字。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_09 USE:U005_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"装出来或借用的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_09 USE:U005_09 -->
With assumed tranquillity Darcy replied that he had done everything in his power to separate his friend from Elizabeth’s sister, and rejoiced in his success.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_09 -->
达西装作镇静地回答说，他已经尽其所能让朋友与伊丽莎白的姐姐分开，并为自己成功做到这一点而高兴。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_09 -->
<!-- USE:U005_10 -->
## assumed name /əˈsumd neɪm/ n. · 使用的化名
`可数，实际采用而非本名的名字，常用 under an assumed name；不自带合法或违法判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_10 USE:U005_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使用的化名","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/67550/pg67550-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_10 USE:U005_10 -->
"I didn’t murder anybody, nor loot a bank, nor commit any felony whatever to send me on the tramp under an assumed name, Steve," he said.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_10_2 -->
He admitted that the explanation might sound like a confession of weakness.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_10 -->
“史蒂夫，我没杀人，没抢银行，也没犯过什么重罪，以致必须用化名四处流浪，”他说。他承认，这样解释听来可能像是在承认自己的软弱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_10 -->
<!-- USE:U005_11 -->
## assumedly /əˈsumɪdli/ adv. · 按假定来看
`以被假定的方式或依据假设而言，表示推断前提而非确定事实；不把它当作肯定证据。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_11 USE:U005_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"按假定来看","sourceUrls":["https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/psychology/articles/10.3389/fpsyg.2013.00951/full"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_11 -->
In the introduction to their 2013 study, Maltese and colleagues say there is only indirect evidence for the role of interpretation in helping behavior.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_11_2 USE:U005_11 -->
Assumedly, people use others’ reactions to judge whether an ambiguous situation requires intervention.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_11_3 -->
Seeing others do nothing may therefore make the situation seem less critical.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_11 -->
马尔泰塞及其同事在2013年研究的引言中说，关于情境解释在助人行为中所起的作用，当时只有间接证据。按其推测，人们会借助他人的反应，判断一个含混的情境是否需要干预。因此，看到别人没有行动，可能让人觉得情况没那么紧迫。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_11 -->
<!-- USE:U005_12 -->
## assuming /əˈsumɪŋ/ conj. · 假如所设前提成立
`assuming 或 assuming that 引出条件，暂且假定某事为真后谈后果，与分词表示“正在认为”或形容词“傲慢的”有别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_12 USE:U005_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"假如所设前提成立","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/98/pg98-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_12 USE:U005_12 -->
"Assuming for a moment that he was overworked, would it show itself in some return of this disorder?" the persistent man of business asked.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_12 -->
“姑且假定他劳累过度，这会不会表现为这种病症的某种复发？”那位坚持追问的商人问道。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_12 -->
<!-- USE:U005_13 -->
## assuming /əˈsumɪŋ/ adj. · 自以为是而僭越的
`形容人过分想当然，摆出傲慢、冒昧或自居有权的态度；不是连词“假如”，也不等于有根据的自信。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_13 USE:U005_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"自以为是而僭越的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/20203/pg20203-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_13 USE:U005_13 -->
An assuming manner of speaking seldom fails to disgust people and tends to create opposition, Franklin warns.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_13_2 -->
If you state your views dogmatically, you may provoke contradiction rather than an open-minded hearing.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_13 -->
富兰克林告诫说，自以为是的说话方式几乎总会让人生厌，并容易招致反对。若你教条地陈述自己的观点，可能激起反驳，而得不到坦诚的聆听。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_13 -->
<!-- USE:U005_14 -->
## assumption /əˈsʌmpʃən/ n. · 未经证实的假设
`可指假定这一行为；表示据此接受的一项命题或前提时可数，常用 make an assumption、an assumption that…、on the assumption that…、an assumption on which…。不同于有证据推导的 inference；guess/conjecture 偏猜测，supposition 偏假定，presumption 可含预先推定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_14 USE:U005_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未经证实的假设","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6473/pg6473-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_14 USE:U005_14 -->
If someone calls football brutal without first showing that it is, and then uses its brutality as the reason to abolish it, the assumption has been used as proof.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_14 -->
如果有人未经证明就把美式橄榄球称为残酷的运动，再以其残酷性为理由要求废除它，就把假设当成了证据。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_14 -->
<!-- USE:U005_15 -->
## assumption /əˈsʌmpʃən/ n. · 职务权力的承担
`通常不可数，assumption of power/office/responsibility 表示开始执掌、接任或承担；不是对权力作一项猜测。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_15 USE:U005_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"职务权力的承担","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/fiji-country-of-concern/fiji-country-of-concern-latest-update-30-september-2014"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_15 USE:U005_15 -->
In its September 2014 update on Fiji, the Foreign Office reported that the Commonwealth Ministerial Action Group recognised credible elections and the assumption of office by a democratically elected government.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_15_2 -->
The group decided to lift Fiji’s suspension and restore its full Commonwealth membership.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_15 -->
英国外交部在2014年9月关于斐济的更新中报告说，英联邦部长级行动小组认可了可信的选举和民选政府的就职。该小组决定解除对斐济的暂停资格措施，恢复其完整的英联邦成员资格。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_15 -->
<!-- USE:U005_16 -->
## assumption /əˈsʌmpʃən/ n. · 傲慢自负
`表示过度自居、僭越的态度；不能把每个 assumptions 译成傲慢，须看是否在评论人的姿态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_16 USE:U005_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"傲慢自负","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/77270/pg77270-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_16 USE:U005_16 -->
Recalling an autumn 1819 visit to Abbotsford, the writer says Scott talked openly of his early career without the slightest egotistical assumption or literary vanity.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_16_2 -->
He spoke kindly of men who had been his companions or rivals.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_16 -->
作者回忆1819年秋天拜访阿伯茨福德庄园时说，司各特坦率地谈起自己的早年经历，却丝毫没有自负矜夸或文学名家的虚荣。他还友善地谈起曾经的同伴或对手。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_16 -->
<!-- USE:U005_17 -->
## Assumption /əˈsʌmpʃən/ n. · 圣母升天
`天主教教义中圣母马利亚身体被接入天国；专名大写，讲述的是该宗教教义，不是一般论证中的假设。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_17 USE:U005_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"圣母升天","sourceUrls":["https://www.newadvent.org/cathen/02006b.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_17 USE:U005_17 -->
The historical Catholic article describes two objects of the feast: Mary’s departure from this life and the assumption of her body into heaven.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_17 -->
这篇历史上的天主教文章说，这个节日有两个纪念对象：马利亚离世，以及她的身体被接入天国。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_17 -->
<!-- USE:U005_18 -->
## Assumption /əˈsʌmpʃən/ n. · 八月十五日圣母升天节
`纪念圣母升天的节日，日期为八月十五日；指节日而非事件本身，常用 the feast of the Assumption。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_18 USE:U005_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"八月十五日圣母升天节","sourceUrls":["https://www.newadvent.org/cathen/02006b.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_18 USE:U005_18 -->
The early twentieth-century Catholic article identifies 15 August as the Feast of the Assumption of the Blessed Virgin Mary.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_18 -->
这篇二十世纪初的天主教文章把8月15日标为圣母马利亚升天节。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_18 -->
<!-- USE:U005_19 -->
## assumptive /əˈsʌmptɪv/ adj. · 被当作理所当然的
`指被预设、未经进一步证明就接受的内容；与形容人的傲慢义有别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_19 USE:U005_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"被当作理所当然的","sourceUrls":["https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/psychology/articles/10.3389/fpsyg.2018.02346/full"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_19 USE:U005_19 -->
In this account of posttraumatic growth, core beliefs form the structure of a person’s assumptive world: basic ideas about the universe, relationships with others and one’s place within them.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_19_2 -->
A traumatic event may challenge those beliefs and prompt a rebuilding of that world.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_19 -->
在这套关于创伤后成长的理论解释中，核心信念构成了一个人所视为当然的世界：关于宇宙、与他人的关系及自身在其中位置的基本观念。创伤事件可能冲击这些信念，促使人重新建构这一世界。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_19 -->
<!-- USE:U005_20 -->
## assumptive /əˈsʌmptɪv/ adj. · 傲慢自居的
`形容人或态度自负、冒昧，过分自居有权；并非中性的“依据假设”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_20 USE:U005_20 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"傲慢自居的","sourceUrls":["https://naija247news.com/the-little-biafran-mosquito-perched-delicately-on-nigerias-scrotum-by-kennedy-emetulu/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_20 USE:U005_20 -->
Writing in 2015 about then Nigerian President Muhammadu Buhari, Kennedy Emetulu stated: “His political instinct is provincial, retrogressive, assumptive and dictatorial.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_20 -->
2015年，肯尼迪·埃梅图卢在评论当时的尼日利亚总统穆罕默杜·布哈里时写道：“他的政治直觉狭隘、倒退、自以为是而且专断。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_20 -->
<!-- USE:U005_21 -->
## reassume /ˌriəˈsum/ v. · 再次承担或采取
`再次接任职务、承担责任，或重新采取某种姿态、形式；re- 表再次，不是重新推测。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_21 USE:U005_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"再次承担或采取","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/14013/pg14013-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_21 USE:U005_21 -->
Almoran, appearing as Hamet, left Almeida’s apartment so that he might reassume his own figure.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_21_2 -->
Osmyn, however, still mistook him for Hamet and intercepted him again.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_21 -->
阿尔莫兰当时呈现着哈米特的模样；他离开阿尔梅达的房间，打算恢复自己的本来外形。然而，奥斯明仍把他当作哈米特，又一次拦住了他。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_21 -->
<!-- USE:U005_22 -->
## reassumption /ˌriəˈsʌmpʃən/ n. · 再次接任承担
`再次承担职务、责任等的行为，常用 reassumption of；对应 reassume 的再次接过，不是未经证明的 assumption。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_22 USE:U005_22 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"再次接任承担","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/55536/pg55536-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_22 USE:U005_22 -->
In Cannon’s regimental history, news of Bonaparte’s return to France and re-assumption of the imperial dignity was followed by preparations for war.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_22_2 -->
Six troops of the Inniskilling Dragoons were selected for foreign service.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_22 -->
在坎农的团史记述中，波拿巴重返法国、重新登上皇位的消息传来后，战争准备随即开始。因尼斯基林龙骑兵团选出了六个骑兵连前往海外服役。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_22 -->
<!-- USE:U005_23 -->
## unassuming /ˌʌnəˈsumɪŋ/ adj. · 谦逊而不摆架子的
`形容人或其举止安静谦逊、不自夸、不装腔作势，通常带赞许；不是说他没有任何假设，也不必表示缺乏能力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_23 USE:U005_23 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"谦逊而不摆架子的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_23 USE:U005_23 -->
Miss Darcy’s manners were perfectly unassuming and gentle.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_23_2 -->
Elizabeth had expected an observer as acute and self-possessed as Mr. Darcy and was relieved to find such different feelings.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_23 -->
达西小姐的举止十分谦逊温柔。伊丽莎白本来以为会遇到一个像达西先生那样敏锐、镇定自若的观察者，发现对方感情如此不同，便松了一口气。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_23 -->
<!-- USE:U005_24 -->
## unassumingly /ˌʌnəˈsumɪŋli/ adv. · 谦逊低调地
`以不摆架子、不夸耀自己的方式行事。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_24 USE:U005_24 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"谦逊低调地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/107/pg107-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_24 -->
Gabriel Oak’s height and breadth could have made him imposing, but his modest bearing reduced that effect.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_24_2 USE:U005_24 -->
He walked unassumingly, with a slight bend that was distinct from stooping.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_24 -->
加布里埃尔·奥克身材高大宽阔，本可以显得很有威势，却因谦逊的姿态而减弱了这种感觉。他走路低调谦逊，身体略微前倾，但并不弓肩。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_24 -->
<!-- USE:U005_25 -->
## unassumingness /ˌʌnəˈsumɪŋnəs/ n. · 谦逊不张扬的品质
`不可数，指人的谦逊、自然、不自居重要的品质，不等于能力平庸或不敢发言。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_25 USE:U005_25 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"谦逊不张扬的品质","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/12340/pg12340-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_25 USE:U005_25 -->
In private life Sir John Herschel displayed a simplicity of manner in harmony with the general unassumingness of his character.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_25_2 -->
He was free from ostentation and as ready to talk with a child as with men of science.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_25 -->
在私人生活中，约翰·赫歇尔爵士举止朴实，与他整体谦逊的性格相称。他不炫耀，既乐于同孩子交谈，也乐于同科学家谈话。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_25 -->
<!-- USE:U005_26 -->
## assumable /əˈsuməbəl/ adj. · 可假定为真的
`指某个命题或情况可以被暂当作真来讨论或据以行动，并不保证它已经证实。可作表语，如 be logically assumable，评价某种假定在逻辑上是否成立；与 assumed“已经被假定的”不同，-able 强调能否这样假定。这里接受的是论证前提，不能用承接贷款或义务的意义理解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C005_26 USE:U005_26 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可假定为真的","sourceUrls":["https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/education/articles/10.3389/feduc.2024.1466631/full"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_26 -->
In a review of teachers’ doctoral studies, links between research and practice—reported mainly in Dutch studies—seemed to run from research to practice.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_26_2 USE:U005_26 -->
Citing Bakx and colleagues, the reviewer says it is assumable that this one-way pattern reflects unequal status between the two settings.
<!-- SENTENCE:S005_26_3 -->
Yet one teacher’s classroom experience had also inspired doctoral research, suggesting a two-way relationship.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C005_26 -->
一篇关于教师攻读博士的综述发现，主要来自荷兰研究的材料显示，研究与实践之间的联系似乎多由研究流向实践。综述作者援引巴克斯及其同事的观点说，可以假定，这种单向模式反映了两个场域之间不平等的地位。不过，一位教师的课堂经验也曾启发博士研究，提示二者的联系可能是双向的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C005_26 -->

<!-- WORD:W006 -->
# coherent

**词源**：coherent、coherence、cohesion 与 cohere 同源，联系到拉丁语 cohaerere“黏在一起”，由 co-“共同”与 haerere“黏附”构成。物理结合引出思想连贯、群体凝聚等意义；波的“相干”则有专门的相位条件，不能只靠词根推断。

<!-- USE:U006_01 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 思路与内容连贯一致
`用于论点、计划、政策、写作等，表示各部分相互配合，逻辑清楚、前后一致，容易理解；不能仅因句子语法正确就称整个论证 coherent。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_01 USE:U006_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"思路与内容连贯一致","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6473/pg6473-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_01 -->
Do not fire scattered answers at isolated points.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_01_2 USE:U006_01 -->
Show how each rebuttal relates to the main proposition: coherent refutation makes a debate more effective.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_01 -->
不要对孤立的点零散反击。要说明每一项反驳怎样关联主命题：连贯的反驳能提高辩论的效果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_01 -->
<!-- USE:U006_02 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 能清楚有条理地说话
`用于人，表示能够清楚、连贯地表达想法，常作表语；受惊或昏迷后恢复时也可描述说话能力，不是只评价观点是否正确。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_02 USE:U006_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"能清楚有条理地说话","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_02_2 -->
"My dearest sister, you have received my hurried letter.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_02 USE:U006_02 -->
I hope this one is clearer, but my head is so bewildered that I cannot promise to be coherent.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_02_3 -->
I have bad news, and it cannot wait."
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_02 -->
“我最亲爱的妹妹，你已经收到我那封匆忙写就的信了。我希望这一封能更清楚，可我头脑如此混乱，不能保证自己能说得有条理。我有坏消息，不能再拖了。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_02 -->
<!-- USE:U006_03 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 黏合成一体的
`用于物体或组成部分，表示相互黏着、结合而保持整体；不是逻辑连贯或波的相干。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_03 USE:U006_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黏合成一体的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/52824/pg52824-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_03 USE:U006_03 -->
In his 1913 account of bread-making, T. B. Wood reports that gluten placed in pure water and acid solutions of different strengths could range from tiny dispersed particles to a tough coherent mass almost like rubber.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_03_2 -->
He adds that the acid concentration in wheat itself was never high enough to produce this last state.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_03 -->
T. B. 伍德在1913年出版的制面包著作中记述，把面筋放入纯水和不同浓度的酸溶液中，它可以呈现从细小分散颗粒到几乎像橡胶一样坚韧、黏合成整块的各种状态。他又指出，小麦本身的酸浓度从未高到足以使面筋达到后一种状态。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_03 -->
<!-- USE:U006_04 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 波保持固定相位关系的
`物理学中，波之间的相位关系保持稳定，称为相干；不是仅指强、亮或方向相同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_04 USE:U006_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"波保持固定相位关系的","sourceUrls":["https://science.nasa.gov/learn/basics-of-space-flight/chapter10-1/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_04 -->
To track a spacecraft precisely, the downlink must be extremely stable.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_04_2 USE:U006_04 -->
NASA’s account explains the solution: have the spacecraft generate a downlink that is coherent to the uplink it receives.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_04 -->
要精确跟踪航天器，下行链路必须极其稳定。NASA 的说明给出了解决办法：让航天器生成与其收到的上行链路相干的下行链路。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_04 -->
<!-- USE:U006_05 -->
## cohere /koʊˈhɪr/ v. · 黏合连结
`不及物，部分黏在一起、保持结合，可以描述物理材料；不能直接把它当及物“粘某物”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_05 USE:U006_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黏合连结","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/34350/pg34350-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_05 USE:U006_05 -->
Describing sandstone, Lyell writes that its grains often cohere without any visible cement.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_05_2 -->
More commonly, he adds, small amounts of siliceous or calcareous matter, iron, or clay bind them together.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_05 -->
莱尔描述砂岩时写道，构成砂岩的颗粒常常黏合在一起，却看不出可见的胶结物。不过，他又补充说，更常见的是少量含硅或钙质物、铁或黏土把这些颗粒结合在一起。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_05 -->
<!-- USE:U006_06 -->
## cohere /koʊˈhɪr/ v. · 结合成统一整体
`不及物，作品的元素、群体等相互配合而形成整体，可用 cohere as a whole；不必有物理黏着。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_06 USE:U006_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"结合成统一整体","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/35341/pg35341-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_06 USE:U006_06 -->
MacClintock argues that, to be of lasting use in work or play, children’s fancies must connect with one another and cohere into a somewhat orderly whole.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_06_2 -->
Literature can help: children encounter complete, ordered works in which significant details do not remain fragmentary or unattached.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_06 -->
麦克林托克认为，儿童的想象只有彼此连接、组成一个多少有些条理的整体，才能在工作或游戏中长久发挥作用。文学能够提供帮助：儿童在其中接触到完整而有序的作品，有意义的细节不会零碎散落、彼此脱节。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_06 -->
<!-- USE:U006_07 -->
## cohere /koʊˈhɪr/ v. · 逻辑一致相吻合
`推理、思想等合乎逻辑、相互一致；cohere with… 表与另一观点或事实吻合，不仅是空间上放在一起。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_07 USE:U006_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"逻辑一致相吻合","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/5827/pg5827-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_07 USE:U006_07 -->
In his discussion of a theory of truth, Russell reports that failure to cohere with our other beliefs is treated as a mark of falsehood.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_07_2 -->
He objects that a novelist could invent a past that fits everything we know yet differs from the real past: a coherent system need not be true.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_07 -->
罗素讨论一种真理理论时指出，依该理论，某个信念不能与我们的其他信念相吻合，被看作它错误的标志。但他提出反驳：小说家可能虚构一段与我们已知的一切都吻合、却不同于真实历史的过去，因此自洽的信念体系未必就是真实的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_07 -->
<!-- USE:U006_08 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 思路内容的连贯性
`指思想、论证、政策或作品各部分衔接成整体的连贯一致性，通常不可数；coherency 为同义形式，不能把名词仅解释为形容词的形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_08 USE:U006_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"思路内容的连贯性","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6473/pg6473-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_08 USE:U006_08 -->
A common fault in student debates is lack of coherence: a speaker answers a miscellaneous set of points without distinguishing main ideas from subordinate ones.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_08 -->
学生辩论中的一个常见问题是缺乏连贯性：发言者回答了一堆杂乱的论点，却未区分主次。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_08 -->
<!-- USE:U006_09 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 清楚表达的能力状态
`用于人的言语表达，表示思路和说话清晰、可理解的状态；与本人是否清醒相关但不是意识水平的同义词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_09 USE:U006_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"清楚表达的能力状态","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/28006/pg28006-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_09 -->
Miss Wodehouse said that her attempt to help had only made matters worse.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_09_2 USE:U006_09 -->
Mr Wentworth drew a chair close and held her hand in a brotherly grasp, trying to restore her to coherence so that she could explain what was wrong.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_09 -->
伍德豪斯小姐说，自己本想帮忙，却反而把事情弄得更糟。温特沃思先生把椅子拉近，像兄长般握住她的手，想让她恢复清楚表达的能力，说出究竟出了什么事。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_09 -->
<!-- USE:U006_10 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 黏合为一体的状态
`指物质各部分结合、保持整体的状态；与社会凝聚或逻辑连贯依对象辨别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_10 USE:U006_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黏合为一体的状态","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10616/pg10616-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_10 USE:U006_10 -->
Locke contrasts the necessary relation between a triangle and its angles with the coherence and continuity of the parts of matter, whose deeper connections he says our ideas do not reveal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_10 -->
洛克把三角形与其角之间的必然关系，同物质各部分的结合与连续性作对照；他认为，我们的观念并未揭示后者更深层的联系。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_10 -->
<!-- USE:U006_11 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 波的相干性
`物理学中，波之间保持稳定相位关系的性质；不是“信息一致性”的泛泛比喻。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_11 USE:U006_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"波的相干性","sourceUrls":["https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/physics/articles/10.3389/fphy.2022.924508/full"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_11 -->
Angelsky and colleagues caution that an interference pattern reveals underlying wave parameters through its visibility.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_11_2 USE:U006_11 -->
To interpret that pattern consistently, an experimenter must also know the polarization properties and degree of coherence of the waves being superposed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_11 -->
安格尔斯基及其同事提醒，干涉图样借助条纹可见度提供有关波的内在参数的信息。但要对图样作出一致的解释，实验人员还必须了解叠加波的偏振特性和相干程度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_11 -->
<!-- USE:U006_12 -->
## coherently /koʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv. · 清楚连贯地
`修饰表达、组织或论证，表示说得清楚、各部分合乎逻辑地衔接；用于人时强调表达可理解，用于作品时强调组织一致。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_12 USE:U006_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"清楚连贯地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/98/pg98-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_12 -->
He formed the speech with his lips many times before he could utter it.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_12_2 USE:U006_12 -->
When spoken words finally came, they came coherently, though slowly.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_12 -->
他的嘴唇反复做出这番话的口形，才终于能把它说出口。话语终于出口时，虽然缓慢，却是连贯的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_12 -->
<!-- USE:U006_13 -->
## coherently /koʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv. · 黏合成整体地
`描述物质或部分彼此黏着、整体结合的方式；须有实际结合对象，不能以一般合作替代。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_13 USE:U006_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黏合成整体地","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US5304342"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_13 -->
In their 1994 patent, H. Tracy Hall Jr. and David R. Hall describe one form of a layered composite built on a supporting base.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_13_2 USE:U006_13 -->
After sintering, each layer is coherently bonded to its neighbour, and the first layer is bonded to the base.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_13 -->
在1994年的专利中，H. Tracy Hall Jr. 和 David R. Hall 描述了一种在支撑基体上构建的层状复合材料。烧结后，各层与相邻层牢固地黏合成整体，第一层则与基体黏合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_13 -->
<!-- USE:U006_14 -->
## coherently /koʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv. · 以相干方式
`物理学用法，波以保持稳定相位关系的方式叠加、传播或发生作用；不表示普通意义的“讲得有条理”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_14 USE:U006_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以相干方式","sourceUrls":["https://science.nasa.gov/science-research/science-enabling-technology/technology-highlights/new-instrument-used-antarctic-ice-sheet-to-probe-extreme-universe/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_14 -->
NASA’s May 2026 account of the Antarctic balloon mission PUEO describes its interferometric trigger.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_14_2 USE:U006_14 -->
It coherently summed signals from multiple antennas in real time, lowering the detection threshold so the instrument could register weaker signals.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_14 -->
NASA在2026年5月对南极高空气球任务PUEO的介绍中，描述了它的干涉式触发系统。该系统实时地把多个天线的信号按相干方式叠加，降低探测阈值，使仪器能够记录更微弱的信号。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_14 -->
<!-- USE:U006_15 -->
## cohesion /koʊˈhiʒən/ n. · 群体凝聚力
`通常不可数，社会、组织、群体成员能够结合成为整体的状态或力量，可用 cohesion within/of…；不是要求每个人意见完全相同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_15 USE:U006_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"群体凝聚力","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/common-ground-award-2026-to-2027-prospectus"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_15 USE:U006_15 -->
The Common Ground Award 2026 to 2027 was designed to reward organisations promoting social cohesion in England, with capital funding for facilities and equipment that bring people from different backgrounds together.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_15_2 -->
Its application window closed on 12 October 2026.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_15 -->
2026至2027年度“共同基础奖”旨在奖励英格兰促进社会凝聚的组织，以资本性资金支持能让不同背景的人相聚的设施和设备。该奖项的申请窗口已于2026年10月12日关闭。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_15 -->
<!-- USE:U006_16 -->
## cohesion /koʊˈhiʒən/ n. · 黏合与聚合性
`指部分黏在一起的行为、状态或倾向；依对象可说材料黏合性，不只限于群体团结。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_16 USE:U006_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黏合与聚合性","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/73975/pg73975-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_16 USE:U006_16 -->
Describing how to separate colloidal clay, Hilgard says that gentle kneading of wet clay or soaking it in hot water can loosen the particles’ adhesion or cohesion.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_16_2 -->
Boiling works more quickly, but he warns against continuing it beyond the point of disintegration: prolonged boiling can make the clay permanently less dispersible.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_16 -->
希尔加德描述分离胶态黏土的方法时指出，轻柔地揉捏湿黏土，或将其放在热水中浸泡，可以松开颗粒间的黏附或黏聚。煮沸的作用更快，但他提醒，不要在材料已解体后继续煮：长时间煮沸可能使黏土的分散能力永久降低。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_16 -->
<!-- USE:U006_17 -->
## cohesion /koʊˈhiʒən/ n. · 同种物质分子的内聚
`物理学中，同一物质分子相互吸引而黏聚的性质；区别于不同物质之间的黏附 adhesion。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_17 USE:U006_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"同种物质分子的内聚","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2018/06/stemonstrations_surface-tension.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_17 -->
NASA’s surface-tension activity asks students to add water drops to a penny until the water spills over.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_17_2 USE:U006_17 -->
The background explains how cohesion—the attraction between like molecules—helps water hold together: the slightly positive hydrogen end of one water molecule is attracted to the slightly negative oxygen end of another.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_17 -->
NASA的表面张力活动要求学生往一枚一美分硬币上滴水，直到水溢出。背景说明解释了内聚力——同类分子之间的吸引——怎样帮助水聚在一起：一个水分子带微正电的氢端，会受到另一个水分子带微负电的氧端吸引。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_17 -->
<!-- USE:U006_18 -->
## cohesive /koʊˈhisɪv/ adj. · 成员协同而凝聚的
`用于群体或由若干部分构成的事物，表示各部分配合成为整体；重点是结合力，不完全等同于 coherent 的逻辑易懂。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_18 USE:U006_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"成员协同而凝聚的","sourceUrls":["https://www.army.mil/article/247552/21_14_building_cohesive_teams"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_18 USE:U006_18 -->
The Army handbook offers leaders methods for building cohesive teams and indicators for measuring team performance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_18 -->
这本陆军手册向领导者提供建设有凝聚力团队的方法，以及衡量团队表现的指标。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_18 -->
<!-- USE:U006_19 -->
## cohesive /koʊˈhisɪv/ adj. · 具有黏聚性的
`用于材料或分子作用等，表示能使部分黏聚、保持结合；物理语境中可指同种物质之间的内聚力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_19 USE:U006_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"具有黏聚性的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/52824/pg52824-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_19 -->
Wood uses clay to illustrate how the properties of colloidal materials can change.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_19_2 USE:U006_19 -->
In his 1913 account, he explains that lime improves the texture of clay-rich soils by making the clay lose its sticky, cohesive nature.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_19 -->
伍德用黏土说明胶体材料的性质会怎样改变。他在1913年的著作中解释，石灰使黏土失去黏稠、易黏聚的特性，从而改善富含黏土的土壤的质地。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_19 -->
<!-- USE:U006_20 -->
## cohesively /koʊˈhisɪvli/ adv. · 协同成整体地
`表示群体或组成部分以彼此配合、形成整体的方式行动或组织。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_20 USE:U006_20 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"协同成整体地","sourceUrls":["https://files.ofsted.gov.uk/v1/file/50148941"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_20 USE:U006_20 -->
Ofsted’s February 2020 report on Seacroft Grange Primary School found that the curriculum was not cohesively planned across all subjects; plans for art and design, and for design and technology, were less developed.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_20_2 -->
It called for plans in every subject that made the knowledge and skills expected at the end of each key stage clear, along with staff training to deliver them.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_20 -->
Ofsted在2020年2月对西克罗夫特格兰奇小学的报告中指出，课程尚未在所有学科中得到整体协调的规划，艺术与设计、设计与技术等学科的规划较不完善。报告要求每个学科都制定课程计划，明确学生在各关键学段结束时应具备的知识和技能，并培训教职员工，使他们能够落实这些计划。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_20 -->
<!-- USE:U006_21 -->
## cohesively /koʊˈhisɪvli/ adv. · 黏聚结合地
`用于物质，表示以自身部分相互黏聚的方式结合、受力或破坏；须依实际材料过程理解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_21 USE:U006_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黏聚结合地","sourceUrls":["https://link.springer.com/article/10.1186/s40563-018-0116-1"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_21 USE:U006_21 -->
In Barbosa and colleagues’ comparison of four types of bonded joints, most failures occurred cohesively within the adhesive layer.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_21_2 -->
There were exceptions: with the adhesive Araldite 2015, the stepped-lap and double-lap joints with a 50 mm overlap failed in the bonded components as they underwent plastic deformation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_21 -->
在巴尔博萨及其同事对四类胶接接头的比较中，大多数破坏以内聚破坏的方式发生在胶层内部。但也有例外：使用胶黏剂Araldite 2015、搭接长度为50毫米的阶梯搭接和双搭接接头，其被粘接构件因发生塑性变形而破坏。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_21 -->
<!-- USE:U006_22 -->
## cohesiveness /koʊˈhisɪvnəs/ n. · 群体或作品的凝聚性
`指人、组织或组成部分相互配合而成为整体的性质，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_22 USE:U006_22 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"群体或作品的凝聚性","sourceUrls":["https://www.army.mil/article/247552/21_14_building_cohesive_teams"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_22 USE:U006_22 -->
Cohesiveness is critical to high performing teams.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_22_2 -->
Each member needs to understand their role and responsibilities and to know that teammates are doing their duties too.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_22 -->
凝聚力对表现出色的团队至关重要。每个成员都需要理解自己的角色和责任，并知道队友也在履行各自的职责。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_22 -->
<!-- USE:U006_23 -->
## cohesiveness /koʊˈhisɪvnəs/ n. · 材料的黏聚性
`指材料自身部分相互结合、保持整体的性质，与群体的社会凝聚性分开。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_23 USE:U006_23 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"材料的黏聚性","sourceUrls":["https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/earth-science/articles/10.3389/feart.2023.1140038/full"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_23 -->
In their tests of high-strength manufactured-sand concrete, Zhou and colleagues kept the water and water-reducer dosages unchanged as they increased the mud-powder content.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_23_2 USE:U006_23 -->
The fresh mix’s cohesiveness increased as its methylene-blue (MB) value rose from 0.85 to 2.00, but flowability worsened and water retention remained largely unchanged.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_23 -->
周及其同事在高强机制砂混凝土试验中增加泥粉含量，同时保持用水量和减水剂用量不变。随着亚甲蓝（MB）值从0.85升到2.00，新拌混凝土的黏聚性增强，但流动性变差，保水性则基本不变。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_23 -->
<!-- USE:U006_24 -->
## incoherent /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 说话混乱难懂的
`用于人或其言语，因惊恐、病情等语无伦次、难以理解；可用 incoherent with fear，不必表示说话者有意隐瞒。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_24 USE:U006_24 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"说话混乱难懂的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/5387/pg5387-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_24 USE:U006_24 -->
After the host set punch-bowls in every available corner, several guests returned home more or less insensible, while others were quite incoherent.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_24_2 -->
The host himself had become a victim of his own good cheer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_24 -->
主人在各个能摆放的角落都设了宾治酒碗；几位客人回家时已不同程度地醉得神志不清，另一些人则完全语无伦次。主人自己最终也没能逃过这番尽兴饮宴。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_24 -->
<!-- USE:U006_25 -->
## incoherent /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 逻辑混乱不一致的
`用于论点、政策、目标或说话内容，表示各部分缺少逻辑或相互不配合，评论政策时常带不赞成。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_25 USE:U006_25 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"逻辑混乱不一致的","sourceUrls":["https://hansard.parliament.uk/Lords/2025-07-01/debates/EF615FD6-E20B-4374-816A-2329943D65DA/UKWeaponsSystems"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_25 USE:U006_25 -->
In a Lords debate on 1 July 2025, Defence Minister Lord Coaker called the Scottish National Party’s position on nuclear weapons ‘incoherent’.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_25_2 -->
He argued that it accepted NATO’s nuclear umbrella and the security it provided while rejecting the weapons needed to deliver it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_25 -->
在英国上议院2025年7月1日的一场辩论中，国防部国务大臣科克勋爵称苏格兰民族党在核武器问题上的立场“缺乏逻辑一致性”。他认为，该党一方面接受北约的核保护伞及其带来的安全，另一方面却反对实现这种保护所需的核武器。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_25 -->
<!-- USE:U006_26 -->
## incoherent /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/ adj. · 波的相位关系不稳定的
`物理学中，不存在确定、稳定相位关系的波称非相干；不等于完全没有波或波很弱。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_26 USE:U006_26 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"波的相位关系不稳定的","sourceUrls":["https://www.ebi.ac.uk/europepmc/webservices/rest/PMC12069550/fullTextXML"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_26 USE:U006_26 -->
Sohn and Yang describe what happens when a spatial light modulator is illuminated with incoherent light from a large-area LED.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_26_2 -->
The light arrives over a broad range of angles, allowing a wide viewing region, but this ordinary arrangement cannot optically reconstruct arbitrary three-dimensional scenes.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_26 -->
索恩和杨描述了用大面积LED发出的非相干光照射空间光调制器时的情况。光从很宽的角度范围入射，从而形成较宽的可视区域；但这种常规布置本身不能在光学上重建任意三维场景。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_26 -->
<!-- USE:U006_27 -->
## incoherence /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; incoherency /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 语无伦次的状态
`指说话混乱、表达无法理解的状态；通常作性质名词，incoherency 也可数，复数 incoherencies 可指具体不连贯之处。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_27 USE:U006_27 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"语无伦次的状态","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/43975/pg43975-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_27 -->
‘You take the thing very seriously,’ the man protested after a public exchange with Apache Kid.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_27_2 USE:U006_27 -->
He started to add that he had only asked a question, but his voice grumbled off into incoherence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_27 -->
在众人面前与阿帕奇小子一番交锋后，那人抗议道：“你把这件事看得太认真了。”他又想说自己不过是问了个问题，但声音渐渐变成含混不清的嘟囔。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_27 -->
<!-- USE:U006_28 -->
## incoherence /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; incoherency /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 缺乏逻辑一致性
`指论证、政策或内容各部分不吻合；也可用可数 incoherencies 指具体混乱或不一致之处，不把所有复数都当多人说话。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_28 USE:U006_28 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"缺乏逻辑一致性","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10616/pg10616-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_28 -->
Locke argues that formal syllogisms do not teach the mind to reason.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_28_2 USE:U006_28 -->
The mind, he says, can perceive the coherence or incoherence of its ideas without those repeated formal steps.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_28 -->
洛克认为，形式三段论并不教会头脑如何推理。他说，头脑无须反复走那些形式步骤，也能察觉自己的观念是否连贯。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_28 -->
<!-- USE:U006_29 -->
## incoherence /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; incoherency /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənsi/ n. · 波的非相干性
`物理学中的稳定相位关系缺失，属于波的性质；与语篇不连贯分开理解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_29 USE:U006_29 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"波的非相干性","sourceUrls":["https://www.ebi.ac.uk/europepmc/webservices/rest/PMC12069550/fullTextXML"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_29 USE:U006_29 -->
In their 2025 study, Sohn and Yang demonstrate a hologram method that deliberately uses spatial incoherence.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_29_2 -->
Their CRIS method optimizes the incoherent sum of coherent light components so that the combined intensity resembles the desired three-dimensional scene.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_29_3 -->
In their proof-of-concept experiment, the viewing region expanded 32-fold, compared with a theoretical 1000-fold expansion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_29 -->
索恩和杨在2025年的研究中展示了一种有意利用空间非相干性的全息成像方法。他们的CRIS方法优化多个相干光分量的非相干叠加，使合成后的光强分布呈现所需的三维场景。在概念验证实验中，可视区域达到原来的32倍，而理论上可扩展到1000倍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_29 -->
<!-- USE:U006_30 -->
## incoherently /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv. · 语无伦次地
`修饰说话、喃喃等表达行为，表示话语混乱、难以理解；也可修饰论证，说明缺乏逻辑衔接。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_30 USE:U006_30 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"语无伦次地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/53551/pg53551-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_30 USE:U006_30 -->
Sarita called him a true friend, yet cried, "No, no, I cannot, I cannot!" wildly and half incoherently.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_30_2 -->
She was tempted by his offer of help but said she had resisted it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_30 -->
萨丽塔称他是位真诚的朋友，却激动而有些语无伦次地喊道：“不，不，我不能，我不能！”他的帮助提议使她动心，但她说自己已经克制住了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C006_30 -->
<!-- USE:U006_31 -->
## incoherently /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv. · 以非相干方式
`物理语境，波以没有稳定相位关系的方式相加或作用；不是行为不理智。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C006_31 USE:U006_31 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以非相干方式","sourceUrls":["https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/physics/articles/10.3389/fphy.2022.1055401/full"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_31 -->
Hyde describes how an experiment could test his simulations of twisted space-time beams in atmospheric turbulence.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_31_2 -->
A Fourier transform pulse shaper can synthesize individual beam realizations.
<!-- SENTENCE:S006_31_3 USE:U006_31 -->
Partial coherence manifests by summing many independent realizations incoherently.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C006_31 -->
