按照斯威夫特的邮政抗争史，邮政大臣的否认激起了更多批评。在作者看来，随后出现的新证据详尽地证明，阿诺德·莫利否认事实的做法过于仓促。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_08 -->
<!-- USE:U014_09 -->
## elaborately /ɪˈlæbərətli/ adv. · 装饰得繁复精巧地
`修饰穿戴、装饰等动作，突出细致图样或装饰手法；它描述做法而不是评价穿戴者的性格。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_09 USE:U014_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/68145/68145-h/68145-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_09_01 USE:U014_09 -->
The calumet was not itself a pipe, but an elaborately decorated shaft pierced like a pipe stem.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_09_02 -->
A pipe bowl was not necessarily attached to it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_09 -->
卡柳梅特本身并不是烟斗，而是一根装饰精细繁复、像烟斗杆一样穿孔的长杆。它并不一定接有烟斗钵。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_09 -->
<!-- USE:U014_10 -->
## elaborateness /ɪˈlæbərətnəs/ n. · 繁复精细程度
`通常不可数，指事物设计、安排或装饰的复杂精细性质；可讨论一项安排过于繁复，也可欣赏工艺，不是“详细说明”这一动作。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_10 USE:U014_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/54476/pg54476-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_10_01 USE:U014_10 -->
A customer's letterhead should not be treated with great elaborateness unless the customer expressly requests it and is willing to pay for it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_10 -->
客户的信笺抬头不应制作得十分繁复，除非客户明确要求这样处理，并且愿意支付相应费用。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_10 -->
<!-- USE:U014_11 -->
## elaboration /ɪˌlæbəˈreɪʃən/ n. · 详细发展与制作
`指把计划、理论或成果详细发展、精心形成的过程，常用 elaboration of；谈过程通常不可数，不能与省力的简单执行混为一谈。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_11 USE:U014_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/35772/pg35772-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R11_01 USE:U014_11 -->
In his 1920 history, Hardwick describes Dalton’s atomic theory as an elaboration of Lavoisier’s principle that total weight is conserved in chemical reactions.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R11_02 -->
In his account, Dalton drew on both Lavoisier’s findings and other chemists’ experimental results to explain how substances combine in fixed proportions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_11 -->
哈德威克在1920年的历史论述中，把道尔顿的原子论看作对拉瓦锡“化学反应中总重量保持不变”这一原理的详细发展。按他的记述，道尔顿结合拉瓦锡的发现和其他化学家的实验结果，解释了物质为何按固定比例化合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_11 -->
<!-- USE:U014_12 -->
## elaboration /ɪˌlæbəˈreɪʃən/ n. · 进一步阐释或细节
`指补充详细解释的过程，也可指展开的细节；对已知陈述作进一步说明，不必改变原观点。without further elaboration 表示不再详述。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_12 USE:U014_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5780548/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R12_01 -->
Postman defined elaboration as adding to the information presented.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R12_02 USE:U014_12 -->
Weinstein and colleagues describe Hirshman’s account as an elaboration on that definition: it specifies a conscious process of associating information to be remembered with other information in memory.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_12 -->
波斯特曼把elaboration界定为对所呈现信息的增补。温斯坦等人将赫希曼的论述看作对这一定义的进一步阐释：它具体指出，这是有意识地将待记信息与记忆中其他信息联系起来的过程。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_12 -->
<!-- USE:U014_13 -->
## elaborative /ɪˈlæbərətɪv/ adj. · 起展开加工作用的
`形容涉及详细展开、加工或说明的活动、方法；常置于名词前，重点在展开作用，不能只因外观华丽就套用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_13 USE:U014_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5780548/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R13_01 USE:U014_13 -->
In elaborative interrogation, students ask how and why questions about the concepts they are studying.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R13_02 -->
They then try to answer from their materials or, eventually, from memory.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R13_03 -->
Weinstein and colleagues stress checking those answers against the materials or with a teacher, because poor content generated this way can actually harm learning.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_13 -->
在“展开加工式提问”中，学生针对正在学习的概念提出“怎样”和“为什么”的问题，继而尝试借助材料、最终凭记忆来回答。温斯坦等人强调，要用材料或请教师核对答案，因为用这种方式生成的劣质内容反而可能妨碍学习。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_13 -->
<!-- USE:U014_14 -->
## elaborator /ɪˈlæbəreɪtər/ n. · 详细发展或阐述者
`可数名词，指把方案、论述等详细展开的人或起这种作用的事物；of 后可交代所展开的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_14 USE:U014_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/58498/58498-h/58498-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_14_01 USE:U014_14 -->
Eudoxus was the discoverer and elaborator of the theory of proportion set out in Book V of Euclid's Elements.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_14_02 -->
The theory applies to magnitudes whether they are commensurable or incommensurable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_14 -->
欧多克索斯发现并详细发展了欧几里得《几何原本》第五卷所载的比例理论。这套理论既适用于可公度的量，也适用于不可公度的量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_14 -->
<!-- USE:U014_15 -->
## over-elaborate /ˌoʊvərɪˈlæbərət/ adj. · 繁复或详尽过头的
`形容制作、安排或说明超出实际需要，带有“过度”的评价；不能与中性的 elaborate 自动等同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_15 USE:U014_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/51700/pg51700-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_15_01 USE:U014_15 -->
A Valentine card need not be gaudy or over-elaborate to be attractive.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_15_02 -->
A few simple motifs, well placed and well coloured, can produce a pleasing result.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_15 -->
情人节贺卡不必花哨或繁复过头才有吸引力。几个简单的图案，只要位置安排得当、配色合适，就能产生悦目的效果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_15 -->
<!-- USE:U014_16 -->
## over-elaborate /ˌoʊvərɪˈlæbəreɪt/ v. · 说明得过于详细
`及物动词，接被详说的对象，也可在宾语已知时独立使用；问题在细节过量，而不是任何进一步解释都有问题。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_16 USE:U014_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/3234/pg3234.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R16_01 USE:U014_16 -->
Pliny contrasts a speaker who has over-elaborated his subject with one who has failed to do it justice.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R16_02 -->
Both are at fault, he argues, though if forced to choose a style he would prefer fluent, flowing oratory of generous scope.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_16 -->
小普林尼把将论题铺陈过头的演说者，与未能充分发挥的演说者相比较。他认为两者都有问题；不过，若必须选择一种风格，他仍偏爱流畅、充沛而展开充分的演说。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_16 -->
<!-- USE:U014_17 -->
## over-elaborately /ˌoʊvərɪˈlæbərətli/ adv. · 过分繁复地
`修饰制作、安排、阐说等方式，指出细密程度超出需要；程度判断依具体目的而定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_17 USE:U014_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/3091/3091-h/3091-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R17_01 USE:U014_17 -->
Torres, a character in Verne’s The Giant Raft, is dressed like a backwoodsman, not over-elaborately.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R17_02 -->
His clothes show long, rough wear: coarse wool trousers, heavy boots and a faded yellowish poncho.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_17 -->
凡尔纳《大筏》中的人物托雷斯，穿得像个林野居民，并不花哨。他的衣服经过长久而粗重的穿用：粗羊毛长裤、厚重靴子，以及一件褪色的淡黄色斗篷。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_17 -->
<!-- USE:U014_18 -->
## over-elaborateness /ˌoʊvərɪˈlæbərətnəs/ n. · 过度繁复的性质
`通常不可数，指设计、安排、表达过分复杂或精细这一性质；重点是状态与程度。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_18 USE:U014_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/17680/pg17680-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_18_01 -->
On entering the Randolph house, a visitor might at first have thought it empty.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_18_02 USE:U014_18 -->
Instead of the over-elaborateness of heavily coloured, gold-laden interiors, its decoration was austere; closer inspection revealed luxurious details.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_18 -->
走进伦道夫家的房子时，来客乍看之下可能觉得里面空荡荡的。这里的装饰朴素克制，没有那种色彩浓重、堆满金饰的过分繁复；细看之下，才会发现细节处的奢华。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_18 -->
<!-- USE:U014_19 -->
## over-elaboration /ˌoʊvərɪˌlæbəˈreɪʃən/ n. · 过度展开或阐述
`通常不可数，指发展、解释时增加了过多细节；与 over-elaborateness 相比，更着眼于展开过程或产生的冗长内容。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C014_19 USE:U014_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/63382/pg63382-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R19_01 USE:U014_19 -->
Gosse admires the careful construction of four of Zola’s stories, yet finds it too mechanically perfect.
<!-- SENTENCE:S014_R19_02 -->
Their over-elaboration leaves him sceptical and cold: their endings draw the threads together too neatly.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C014_19 -->
戈斯赞赏左拉四篇故事构思之精细，却又觉得它们过于机械地完美。过度铺陈使他心存疑虑、感受冷淡：故事结尾把所有线索收拢得太过整齐。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C014_19 -->

<!-- WORD:W015 -->
# entail

**词源**：原先与把地产限定给某一继承序列有关，来自英法语中“切分、限定”的词系，与 tailor 的远源相连。后来发展成“使某人不可避免地承担”，进而有今天的“必然涉及”义，不要用动物的 tail 作历史词源。

<!-- USE:U015_01 -->
## entail /ɪnˈteɪl/ v. · 必然涉及或带来
`正式及物动词，某事使另一事成为不可避免的组成部分、要求或后果；可接名词、doing，也可接 somebody doing。比普通 involve 更强调随之而来的必要联系，不只是事情偶然同时发生。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_01 USE:U015_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/treasury-approvals-process-for-programmes-and-projects/treasury-approvals-process-for-projects-and-programmes"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_01_01 USE:U015_01 -->
In the preparatory phase of the Treasury approval process, this stage entails research and development of the initial proposal before formal initiation of the project.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_01 -->
在英国财政部审批流程的准备阶段，这一步需要研究和开发最初的提案，随后才正式启动项目。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_01 -->
<!-- USE:U015_02 -->
## entail /ɪnˈteɪl/ v. · 使财产按限嗣继承
`法律、历史制度用法，把地产等限定给特定家族继承序列，使受领者不能随意处分给其他人；可说 entail an estate on 某继承人。不是一切普通遗赠的统称。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_02 USE:U015_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_02_01 USE:U015_02 -->
Mr Bennet's estate was entailed, in default of male heirs, on a distant relation.
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_02_02 -->
This was unfortunate for his daughters.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_02 -->
班纳特先生的地产规定，在没有男性继承人的情况下，由一位远亲继承。这对他的女儿们很不利。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_02 -->
<!-- USE:U015_03 -->
## entail /ɪnˈteɪl/ v. · 不可转让地赋予
`把某物永久地系于某对象，通常用 entail something on somebody；重点是不可任意脱离或转让，可能由限嗣继承的概念引申，不等同于普通的一次赠送。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_03 USE:U015_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/42657/pg42657-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_R03_01 USE:U015_03 -->
For Spurgeon, believers’ loved ones are God’s loans, not something entailed upon them.
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_R03_02 -->
He urges readers to place these loved ones in God’s care and learn to surrender what they hold dearest.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_03 -->
在司布真看来，信徒所爱的人是上帝暂时借与的，并非不可撤回地永久交给他们的。他劝读者把这些至亲交托上帝照看，学会将最珍爱的事物交还给上帝。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_03 -->
<!-- USE:U015_04 -->
## entail /ɪnˈteɪl/ n. · 限嗣继承地产
`法律用法，可数名词，指受到限定继承安排约束的不动产本身；与表示继承规则的抽象义区别开。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_04 USE:U015_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/31377/pg31377-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_R04_01 USE:U015_04 -->
In Hoffmann’s story, the baron leaves the festivities to attend to his duties as lord of the entail.
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_R04_02 -->
He reviews the estate’s revenues, hears proposals for improvements and listens to his tenants’ complaints.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_04 -->
在霍夫曼的故事里，男爵离开宾客的欢宴，去履行他作为限嗣继承地产主的职责。他审阅地产收入，听取改进建议，也听取佃户的申诉。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_04 -->
<!-- USE:U015_05 -->
## entail /ɪnˈteɪl/ n. · 限嗣继承安排
`法律用法，指特定地产的继承顺序、限嗣继承制度或约束；break the entail 表示解除这种限制，不能理解成拆毁地产。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_05 USE:U015_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/32198/pg32198-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_05_01 USE:U015_05 -->
The entail on Heatherington Hall had been broken long before.
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_05_02 -->
Yet no Fallowfield had tried to sell the estate until the present lord was nearly ruined by unsuccessful stock-market speculation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_05 -->
希瑟灵顿庄园的限嗣继承约束早已解除。然而，直到现任法洛菲尔德勋爵因股票投机失利而几乎破产，家族才有人试图出售庄园。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_05 -->
<!-- USE:U015_06 -->
## entailment /ɪnˈteɪlmənt/ n. · 必然涉及或蕴涵
`指某事必然带来另一事的关系或结果；在推理中可表示前者成立就要求后者成立的蕴涵。of、between 等结构须按所述关系理解，不等于统计相关。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_06 USE:U015_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-rdf12-semantics-20260924/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_06_01 USE:U015_06 -->
According to W3C’s September 2026 draft on RDF semantics, making more assumptions about the meanings of internationalized resource identifiers (IRIs) in an RDF graph generally allows more entailments to follow.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_06 -->
按照W3C在2026年9月发布的RDF语义草案，一般来说，对RDF图中国际化资源标识符（IRI）的意义作出的假定越多，就能从中推出越多蕴涵结论。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_06 -->
<!-- USE:U015_07 -->
## entailment /ɪnˈteɪlmənt/ n. · 设定限嗣继承
`法律语境中指施加限定继承的行为、状态或安排，与普通后果义区别在财产处分制度；不是所有遗产继承都具有这种约束。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_07 USE:U015_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/17972/pg17972-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_07_01 USE:U015_07 -->
In Crosse's account of nineteenth-century Hungary, an act of entailment would require special permission from the sovereign and government.
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_07_02 -->
The estate would then be placed under a special court, without whose permission neither sale nor mortgage would be allowed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_07 -->
按照克罗斯对十九世纪匈牙利的记述，若要设定限嗣继承，需要得到君主和政府的特别许可。地产随后会交由一个特别法庭管辖；没有该法庭的许可，就既不能出售，也不能抵押。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_07 -->
<!-- USE:U015_08 -->
## disentail /ˌdɪsɪnˈteɪl/ v. · 解除限定继承
`法律及物动词，解除某地产的限嗣继承限制，使其不再受该继承序列约束；宾语是财产，解除限制不等于否认其所有权。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C015_08 USE:U015_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/44533/44533-h/44533-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_08_01 USE:U015_08 -->
Under the Scottish entail bill discussed by the writer in 1848, an heir in possession born after the date of the act could disentail the estate in the prescribed manner.
<!-- SENTENCE:S015_08_02 -->
An heir in possession born before that date could do so only with the consent of the next heir, who had to be born after that date and capable of contracting.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C015_08 -->
按照作者在1848年讨论的苏格兰限嗣继承法案，占有该限定继承地产、且在法令日期之后出生的继承人，可以依规定方式解除地产的限定继承。在该日期之前出生的占有地产者，只有得到下一顺位继承人的同意，才能这样做；后者须在该日期之后出生，并具有订约能力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C015_08 -->

<!-- WORD:W016 -->
# feasible

**词源**：经法语来自 faire（做、制造）一系，远源为拉丁语 facere。基本联系是“做得成”，而不是仅仅“似乎会发生”；feasibility 表示做得成的条件或程度。

<!-- USE:U016_01 -->
## feasible /ˈfiːzəbəl/ adj. · 实际做得成的
`指计划、行动或制造等在实际条件下可以完成，有时还带有容易、便利实施的意思；常用 it is feasible to do、whether something is feasible。它谈可实行性，不保证方案一定会被采用或一定成功。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_01 USE:U016_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/guidance/assessment-process-and-criteria"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_01_01 USE:U016_01 -->
For this UK government funding assessment, consider whether the proposal is scientifically, technically and practically feasible.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_01_02 -->
Assess how innovative the idea is and how it compares with possible alternatives.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_01 -->
在这项英国政府资助评审中，请考虑提案在科学、技术和实际操作上是否可行。评估这个想法的创新程度，以及它与其他可能方案相比如何。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_01 -->
<!-- USE:U016_02 -->
## feasible /ˈfiːzəbəl/ adj. · 可能发生的
`也见用来表示 likely、probable，但这一用法存在规范争议；谈“可能会下雨”等发生概率时，通常选 likely 或 possible 更清楚，避免与“可实行”混淆。此处保留辨认这一用法，不把争议写成语法绝对禁令。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_02 USE:U016_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6382/pg6382-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_R02_01 USE:U016_02 -->
In Bat Wing, Knox suggests that Colonel Menendez was Mrs Camber’s lover; Harley calls the idea feasible.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_R02_02 -->
Yet Harley later insists that he will not speculate about the crime without a solid foundation of fact.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_02 -->
在《蝠翼》中，诺克斯推测梅嫩德斯上校曾是坎伯夫人的情人；哈利说这个想法说得通。然而，哈利稍后强调，没有坚实的事实基础，他不会对罪案作推测。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_02 -->
<!-- USE:U016_03 -->
## feasibly /ˈfiːzəbli/ adv. · 在实际可行条件下
`表示某行动以可实施的方式、在可做到的范围内进行；也可修饰情态动词说明现实可能性。应区分行动能否实现与单纯猜测其概率。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_03 USE:U016_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/28178/28178-h/28178-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_03_01 -->
If a commander cannot accomplish the assigned task, he should look for ways to advance it.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_03_02 USE:U016_03 -->
If even that is impossible, he should contribute as far as he feasibly and acceptably can to the wider purpose of the mission.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_03 -->
指挥员若无法完成分配的任务，应寻找仍能推进任务的办法。如果连这一点也做不到，就应在实际可行、后果可接受的范围内，为任务更广的目的作出贡献。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_03 -->
<!-- USE:U016_04 -->
## feasibility /ˌfiːzəˈbɪləti/ n. · 可行性及其程度
`通常不可数，指某行动、项目可实行的状态或程度；常用 feasibility of doing，研究条件是否允许，不等于已经实施。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_04 USE:U016_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/guidance/further-education-estates-planning/strategic-estate-planning"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_04_01 USE:U016_04 -->
When comparing options for a college estate, you may set aside options with clearly evidenced problems of technical feasibility, deliverability or affordability.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_04_02 -->
Then develop the evidence needed to evaluate the remaining options.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_04 -->
比较学院校舍方案时，可以排除那些已有清楚证据显示在技术可行性、实施交付或费用承受能力上存在问题的选项。然后，应继续建立评估其余选项所需的证据基础。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_04 -->
<!-- USE:U016_05 -->
## feasibility study /ˌfiːzəˈbɪləti ˌstʌdi/ n. · 项目可行性研究
`可数名词，在决定实施前研究一个拟议项目是否实际可行；可涉及技术、资源、成本等实际条件，但词本身不保证评估全面或结果为肯定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_05 USE:U016_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/treasury-approvals-process-for-programmes-and-projects/treasury-approvals-process-for-projects-and-programmes"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_05_01 USE:U016_05 -->
Before a new project expected to join the Government Major Projects Portfolio proceeds to detailed business-case work, it must undergo an initial feasibility study.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_05_02 -->
The study should test whether the proposed project is viable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_05 -->
一个预计纳入英国政府重大项目组合的新项目，在开展详细商业论证之前，必须先进行初步可行性研究。这项研究应检验拟议项目是否可行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_05 -->
<!-- USE:U016_06 -->
## infeasible /ɪnˈfiːzəbəl/ adj.; unfeasible /ʌnˈfiːzəbəl/ adj. · 实际行不通的
`指计划或行动在相关条件下无法实施、不切实际；两种否定形式均可表达此义。可以是资金、技术、天气等条件造成，不必理解为在任何世界中逻辑上都不可能。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_06 USE:U016_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://developers.google.com/optimization/cp/cp_solver"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_R06_01 USE:U016_06 -->
Google’s CP-SAT guide distinguishes a problem proved infeasible from a solver that has simply stopped without finding a solution.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_R06_02 -->
A time or memory limit may leave its status unknown.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_06 -->
Google的CP-SAT指南区分了两种情况：问题被证明没有可行解，以及求解器只是停了下来、没找到解。时间或内存限制可能使其状态仍为未知。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_06 -->
<!-- USE:U016_07 -->
## infeasibility /ɪnˌfiːzəˈbɪləti/ n.; unfeasibility /ʌnˌfiːzəˈbɪləti/ n. · 不可行性
`通常不可数，指某方案实际行不通这一状态或程度；of 后可指出方案或行动。判断须有具体条件，不等于主观不喜欢。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_07 USE:U016_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://developers.google.com/optimization/math_opt"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_R07_01 -->
Google’s February 2026 MathOpt documentation describes models as independent of the solver.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_R07_02 USE:U016_07 -->
It lists infeasibility analysis as available only with Gurobi, which requires a licence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_07 -->
Google在2026年2月更新的MathOpt文档说明，模型的定义独立于所用的求解器。该文档将不可行性分析列为仅在使用Gurobi时提供的功能，而Gurobi需要许可证。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_07 -->
<!-- USE:U016_08 -->
## unfeasibly /ʌnˈfiːzəbli/ adv. · 达到难以实行的程度
`表示做法或要求在现实中不可行，常修饰高、贵、复杂等程度；可说明规模或要求已超出可实施条件，不是仅仅不方便。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C016_08 USE:U016_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://journals.plos.org/plosmedicine/article?id=10.1371/journal.pmed.0020249"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_08R_01 USE:U016_08 -->
In a 2005 analysis of preclinical HIV-prevention trials, Regoes and colleagues found that repeated low-dose virus challenges need not require unfeasibly large numbers of animals.
<!-- SENTENCE:S016_08R_02 -->
Using statistical power analysis to compare trial designs, they proposed repeated low-dose challenges as a promising alternative to high-dose challenges.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C016_08 -->
雷戈斯及同事在2005年对临床前HIV预防试验的分析中发现：反复进行低剂量病毒攻击试验，未必需要多到难以实行的实验动物数量。他们运用统计效能分析比较试验设计，提出反复低剂量攻击是一种有希望替代高剂量攻击的方案。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C016_08 -->

<!-- WORD:W017 -->
# implicit

**词源**：经法语与拉丁语 implicitus（卷在里面、包含其中）相关，是 implicare 一系的形式。可借“含在其中而未展开”理解未明说与内含义；“完全信任”和数学义仍须单独掌握。

<!-- USE:U017_01 -->
## implicit /ɪmˈplɪsɪt/ adj. · 未明说而暗含的
`没有直接讲明，但由措辞、行动或语境传达出来；如 implicit warning。与 explicit（明确说出）相对，不能仅因读者猜到了就断言说话者一定有此暗示。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_01 USE:U017_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_01_01 -->
Ryan considers a tax that would take future increases in land value.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_01_02 -->
Buyers would have paid less, he argues, if they had known the state would introduce this tax.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_01_03 USE:U017_01 -->
Passing it therefore violates an implicit promise and obliges the state to compensate owners for the resulting losses.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_01 -->
瑞安讨论了一种征走土地未来增值的税。他认为，买家如果当初知道国家会开征这种税，就不会支付那么高的地价。因此，开征该税违背了一项未明说的承诺，使国家有义务补偿所有者由此遭受的损失。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_01 -->
<!-- USE:U017_02 -->
## implicit /ɪmˈplɪsɪt/ adj. · 内在包含的
`正式用法，某成分或后果本就包含在一件事中，常用 implicit in；未必是有人刻意隐瞒或暗示，而是由事物性质带出。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_02 USE:U017_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_02_01 -->
Ryan argues that Adam Smith's belief in free competition would logically lead him to regard competitive wages as just.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_02_02 USE:U017_02 -->
That doctrine is implicit in Smith's writings, although his labour theory of value suggests that the worker should receive the whole product.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_02_03 -->
Smith, however, restricted the latter conclusion to primitive and pre-capitalist societies.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_02 -->
瑞安认为，亚当·斯密对自由竞争的信念，在逻辑上会使他把竞争形成的工资视为公正。这一学说内含于斯密的著作中，尽管他的劳动价值论又意味着劳动者应取得全部劳动产品。不过，斯密把后一个结论限定在原始社会和前资本主义社会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_02 -->
<!-- USE:U017_03 -->
## implicit /ɪmˈplɪsɪt/ adj. · 毫无保留的
`常置于 belief、faith、trust、obedience 等名词前，表示完全、不怀疑或不加保留；此义不是“未说出口的信任”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_03 USE:U017_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/45917/45917-h/45917-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_03_01 USE:U017_03 -->
In his historical account of cultivated plants, de Candolle does not place implicit faith in the claim that Cucurbita maxima is native to the banks of the Niger: it rests on one traveller's assertion.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_03_02 -->
He nevertheless believes that the species originated in the Old World and was brought to America by Europeans.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_03 -->
在其栽培植物历史论述中，德康多尔并不完全相信笋瓜原产于尼日尔河畔的说法：这一说法只依据一位旅行者的断言。尽管如此，他仍认为，这个物种起源于旧大陆，后来由欧洲人带到美洲。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_03 -->
<!-- USE:U017_04 -->
## implicit /ɪmˈplɪsɪt/ adj. · 隐式表达的
`数学用法，函数关系没有把因变量直接单独表示为自变量的显式表达式，而以变量之间的方程等给出；implicit function、implicit equation 须按实际对象理解，不表示这个关系含糊不精确。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_04 USE:U017_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/33283/pg33283-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_04_01 USE:U017_04 -->
In implicit form, a function is stated through a relation between variables.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_04_02 -->
An explicit form instead gives one variable directly in terms of the others.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_04 -->
隐式形式通过变量之间的关系来表述函数。显式形式则直接用其他变量表示其中一个变量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_04 -->
<!-- USE:U017_05 -->
## implicitly /ɪmˈplɪsɪtli/ adv. · 以未明说的方式
`表示没有直接说出，却由行动、结论或表述传达某意义；如 implicitly criticize。也可指某内容内含在论证中，不能把未说出的一切自动算作作者承诺。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_05 USE:U017_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/5983/5983-h/5983-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_05_01 USE:U017_05 -->
In the Roman rule described here, an appointment of a guardian for 'children' implicitly includes grandsons; an appointment only for 'sons' does not.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_05 -->
在这里所述的罗马法规则中，为“子女”指定监护人，暗含孙子也在范围之内；如果只为“儿子”指定监护人，则不包括孙子。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_05 -->
<!-- USE:U017_06 -->
## implicitly /ɪmˈplɪsɪtli/ adv. · 完全不加怀疑地
`与 trust、believe、obey 等结合，指完全信任或不加保留地服从；常在动词后，与“暗中相信”不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_06 USE:U017_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/14013/pg14013-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_06_01 USE:U017_06 -->
'To trust another implicitly,' says Hamet, 'is to give up a prerogative which it is both our duty and our interest to keep.'
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_06 -->
“毫无保留地信任另一个人，”哈米特说，“就是放弃一项我们既有义务、也有切身利益去保留的自主权。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_06 -->
<!-- USE:U017_07 -->
## implicitly /ɪmˈplɪsɪtli/ adv. · 以隐式关系给出
`数学语境中表示函数由含多个变量的关系式间接确定，而非显式解出；常用 defined implicitly，不等于不完整定义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_07 USE:U017_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/33283/pg33283-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_07_01 USE:U017_07 -->
An equation relating two variables can contain implicitly the means of expressing one in terms of the other, without showing that expression directly.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_07 -->
一个联系两个变量的方程，可以在没有直接写出表达式的情况下，隐含着用一个变量表示另一个变量的方法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_07 -->
<!-- USE:U017_08 -->
## implicitness /ɪmˈplɪsɪtnəs/ n. · 隐含不明说的性质
`通常不可数，指表达或信息没有明说、需要由所含关系理解的性质或程度；不应把这种隐含性自动评价为故弄玄虚。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_08 USE:U017_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s10339-023-01171-z"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_08R_01 USE:U017_08 -->
In their account of linguistic persuasion, Mannaioli and colleagues describe “implicitness of content”: both vague expressions and implicatures leave the receiver to infer part of the message.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_08R_02 -->
They argue that people may be less inclined to scrutinize conclusions they feel they have reached themselves.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_08 -->
曼纳约利及同事在讨论语言说服机制时，说明了“内容的隐含性”：模糊表达与会话含意都会让接收者自行推断一部分信息。他们认为，人们对于感觉是自己推得的结论，可能较少仔细审查。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_08 -->
<!-- USE:U017_09 -->
## implicitness /ɪmˈplɪsɪtnəs/ n. · 无保留的程度
`在信任、服从等语境指完全、不怀疑的性质；of 后交代具体态度。应与信息未明说的“隐含性”分别理解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C017_09 USE:U017_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_09_01 -->
Darcy's explanation restored Elizabeth's good opinion of Bingley.
<!-- SENTENCE:S017_09_02 USE:U017_09 -->
Bingley's affection seemed sincere, and his conduct blameless—unless the implicitness of his confidence in his friend counted as a fault.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C017_09 -->
达西的解释使伊丽莎白恢复了对宾利的好感。宾利的感情似乎真诚，行为也无可指责——除非他对朋友那种毫无保留的信任也算过错。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C017_09 -->

<!-- WORD:W018 -->
# intervene

**词源**：与拉丁语 intervenire（来到两者之间）相关：inter- 表“之间”，venire 表“来”。英语动词是在 intervention 等形式的历史关系中形成的；“来到中间”可联系介入、插话及时间空间相隔。

<!-- USE:U018_01 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 介入并改变局面
`不及物动词，参与某局面以防止、调整或改变其进程，常用 intervene in 或 between；既可指警察制止冲突，也可指国家干预，是否正当由语境判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_01 USE:U018_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/75896/pg75896-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_01_01 USE:U018_01 -->
Inspector Béchoux knew Barnett only from the hours they spent together when Barnett intervened in one of his cases.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_01_02 -->
Barnett's private life, his past, and even his identity remained a mystery to him.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_01 -->
贝舒探长对巴内特的了解，仅来自巴内特介入他的案件时两人相处的那些时光。巴内特的私生活、过去，甚至真实身份，对他来说仍然是谜。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_01 -->
<!-- USE:U018_02 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 插话加入谈话
`在别人的交谈中插入自己的话，可直接叙述 intervene，也可接引语；重点是进入话轮，不一定粗鲁或强制打断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_02 USE:U018_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/45954/old/45954-h/45954-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_02_01 -->
Explaining his ruling, the presiding officer says that the Senator from Massachusetts lost the floor after appealing the Chair's ruling.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_02_02 USE:U018_02 -->
The Senator from Louisiana then intervened with another motion, to lay the appeal on the table.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_02 -->
会议主持人解释裁定时说，马萨诸塞州参议员对主席裁定提出异议后，失去了发言权。随后，路易斯安那州参议员插话提出另一项动议，要求把这一异议搁置。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_02 -->
<!-- USE:U018_03 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 意外情况打乱进程
`某事件突然发生，阻止、延迟或改变原有安排；如天气介入并打乱计划。主体是发生的事件，不要求有人主动干预。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_03 USE:U018_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/75557/pg75557-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R03_01 USE:U018_03 -->
In Merely the Patient, Harper says a microbe intervened and upset the doctors’ predictions of a normal recovery.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R03_02 -->
The records described him as improving, but he could not eat and felt he was becoming a physical wreck.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_03 -->
在《不过是病人》中，哈珀说，一种病菌突然出现，打乱了医生对他正常康复的预计。记录说他正在好转，但他无法进食，觉得自己的身体状况正日益恶化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_03 -->
<!-- USE:U018_04 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 发生在两事之间
`某事件发生于两个时间点或事件之间，常用 between；重点在时间位置，并不一定造成阻碍。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_04 USE:U018_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/72981/pg72981-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_04_01 USE:U018_04 -->
Russell argues that an elaborate chain of physical and physiological causation intervenes between speaking and hearing oneself speak.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_04 -->
罗素指出，从说出话语到听见自己说话，中间经过了一连串复杂的物理和生理因果过程。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_04 -->
<!-- USE:U018_05 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 位于两物之间
`不及物用法，某物、区域处在两物中间，常用 between；这里是空间关系，不是采取行动调停。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_05 USE:U018_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/21239/pg21239-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R05_01 -->
Six ladders rise against a cliff, as if someone had tried to escape from the valley.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R05_02 USE:U018_05 -->
But a long gap still intervenes between the highest ladder and the top, and overhanging rock prevents another ladder from being added.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_05 -->
六架梯子沿着峭壁层层架起，仿佛曾有人想借此逃出山谷。但是，最高一架梯子与崖顶之间仍隔着一大段空隙；上方突出的岩石使人无法再架上一架。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_05 -->
<!-- USE:U018_06 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 以第三方身份参加诉讼
`法律用法，原非该案当事人的第三方依法加入诉讼程序；不是法官一般地插话。具体申请条件须由相应司法制度决定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_06 USE:U018_06,U018_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.insb.uscourts.gov/book/export/html/1384"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_06_01 USE:U018_06,U018_07 -->
At the US Bankruptcy Court for the Southern District of Indiana, a party other than a trustee seeking intervenor status must file a motion to intervene.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_06_02 -->
The motion may be filed only in an adversary proceeding.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_06 -->
在美国印第安纳州南区破产法院，除受托人以外，希望取得诉讼参加人身份的一方，必须提出参加诉讼动议。这项动议只能在对抗性诉讼程序中提出。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_06 -->
<!-- USE:U018_07 -->
## intervener /ˌɪntərˈviːnər/ n.; intervenor /ˌɪntərˈviːnər/ n. · 介入者或诉讼参加人
`可数名词，指介入某事的人或主体；在诉讼语境尤其指申请或获准以第三方身份参加者。两种词形均与 intervene 有关，不能把它当成已经胜诉的身份。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C018_06 USE:U018_07 -->
<!-- USE:U018_08 -->
## intervenient /ˌɪntərˈviːniənt/ adj. · 介于中间或介入的
`形容发生、处于两者之间或介入其间的事物；意义随时间、空间或作用关系确定，不自动含“干涉有害”的评价。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_08 USE:U018_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/74630/pg74630-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R08_01 USE:U018_08 -->
Massive slopes rise around the riders, with silver gleams of mountain torrents in the deep intervenient ravines.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R08_02 -->
The horsemen turn, and the massive shoulder of the mountain hides a great peak from view.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_08 -->
骑行者周围，巨大的山坡耸立，隔在其间的深谷里闪出山涧的银光。骑手们转弯，山体隆起的一侧便遮住了一座高峰。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_08 -->
<!-- USE:U018_09 -->
## intervening /ˌɪntərˈviːnɪŋ/ adj. · 两事之间的时间
`通常位于名词前，指两个事件或时间点之间的一段时期，如 intervening years；不是“正在被干预的年份”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_09 USE:U018_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/36197/pg36197-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_09_01 -->
Between the Council of Nicaea in 325 and the Gregorian calendar reform in 1582, 1,257 years passed.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_09_02 USE:U018_09 -->
During the intervening years, errors in the Julian calendar complicated the calculation of Easter.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_09 -->
从325年的尼西亚会议到1582年的格里高利历改革，相隔1257年。在这段期间，儒略历的误差使复活节日期的计算变得复杂。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_09 -->
<!-- USE:U018_10 -->
## intervening /ˌɪntərˈviːnɪŋ/ adj. · 位于两物之间的
`通常位于名词前，形容隔在两物之间的区域或物体，如 intervening space；不要求它真的阻挡通行。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_10 USE:U018_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/56302/old/56302-h/56302-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_10_01 USE:U018_10 -->
In an electric arc lamp, current crosses the intervening space between two slightly separated carbon points and produces a brilliant light.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_10 -->
在碳弧灯中，电流穿过两根略微分开的碳棒尖端之间的空隙，产生明亮的光。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_10 -->
<!-- USE:U018_11 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n. · 介入行为或措施
`可数或不可数，指介入某局面、插入进程的行动或一次措施；intervention in 后交代局面。可用于医疗等措施，但是否有效需要独立证据。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_11 USE:U018_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/consultations/uk-clinical-guidelines-for-alcohol-treatment/uk-clinical-guidelines-for-alcohol-treatment-core-elements-of-alcohol-treatment"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_11_01 USE:U018_11 -->
The draft UK alcohol-treatment guidelines ask services to tailor their interventions to the diverse needs of the local population, especially people facing severe and multiple disadvantages.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_11 -->
英国酒精使用问题治疗指南草案要求服务机构根据当地人群的不同需要调整干预措施，尤其要照顾面临严重、多重不利处境的人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_11 -->
<!-- USE:U018_12 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n. · 对他方事务的干预
`可数或不可数，尤指国家介入另一方事务，可说 military intervention；是否受邀、是否合法、是否武力介入须保留上下文，词本身不作统一裁决。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_12 USE:U018_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/41046/old/41046-h/41046-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_12_01 -->
In 1830, the provisional Belgian government invited the Great Powers to intervene in its struggle with the Dutch.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_12_02 USE:U018_12 -->
Their intervention helped secure the formation of a separate Kingdom of Belgium.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_12 -->
1830年，比利时临时政府邀请列强介入它与荷兰方面的斗争。这次干预帮助促成了独立的比利时王国。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_12 -->
<!-- USE:U018_13 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n. · 调停斡旋
`指第三方介入以缓和冲突、促成解决的调停行为或一次调停；不等于裁判已经强制决定结果。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_13 USE:U018_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.eeoc.gov/federal-sector/mediation"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_13_01 USE:U018_13 -->
In mediation, an acceptable, impartial and neutral third party makes an intervention to help the disputing parties reach a mutually acceptable solution.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_13_02 -->
The mediator has no decision-making authority.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_13 -->
在调停中，一位各方可以接受、公正且中立的第三方介入，帮助争议各方达成彼此能够接受的解决方案。调停者没有作出裁决的权限。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_13 -->
<!-- USE:U018_14 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n. · 亲友劝助干预会
`可数名词，亲友针对某人严重问题共同指出其需要帮助的一次干预；不是所有随口建议都称为 an intervention，也不保证对方因此接受治疗。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_14 USE:U018_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/GOVPUB-HE20_400-PURL-gpo153429/pdf/GOVPUB-HE20_400-PURL-gpo153429.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_14R_01 USE:U018_14 -->
The 2020 revision of SAMHSA’s family-therapy guide removed the Johnson Intervention, an approach involving a family session in which relatives confront the person with a substance use problem.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_14R_02 -->
The guide warns that the approach may do more harm than good: many families find it confrontational and blaming, and most do not go through with that session.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_14 -->
美国药物滥用与精神健康服务管理局2020年修订的家庭治疗指南删去了“约翰逊干预”这一方式；在这种家庭会谈中，亲属会当面质询有物质使用问题的家人。指南警告说，这种方式可能弊大于利：许多家庭觉得它对抗性强、带有责备意味，多数家庭没有真正进行这场会谈。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_14 -->
<!-- USE:U018_15 -->
## interventionism /ˌɪntərˈvenʃənɪzəm/ n. · 干预主义
`通常不可数，支持主动介入事务、由国家等采取干预的主张或政策取向；须看经济、外交等具体领域，不等于每一种干预行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_15 USE:U018_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://hansard.parliament.uk/Commons/1986-07-07/debates/92637e48-6f3c-467c-b9eb-fe4446af3aed/FutureOfManufacturingIndustry"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R15_01 USE:U018_15 -->
In a 1986 debate, Mr Butcher asked rhetorically whether the government’s industrial approach amounted to interventionism.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R15_02 -->
He distinguished it from detailed national planning and sector targets, arguing that support should work with market forces.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_15 -->
1986年一次辩论中，布彻先生反问，政府这套产业政策思路是否算干预主义。他将其与详细的全国规划和行业目标区分开来，主张提供支持时应顺应市场力量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_15 -->
<!-- USE:U018_16 -->
## interventionist /ˌɪntərˈvenʃənɪst/ adj. · 倾向主动干预的
`形容机构、国家的政策或立场愿意介入并非直接关乎自身的局面或事务；常见于新闻政经表述，可指工业政策等，不只限军事。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_16 USE:U018_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/news/business-secretary-speech-on-industrial-strategy-at-policy-exchange"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_16_01 USE:U018_16 -->
In a speech published on 26 October 2011, the UK's Business Secretary recalled seeing the mistakes of heavily interventionist government in the late 1970s: it had picked 'winners' that often became expensive 'lame ducks'.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_16_02 -->
At the time of the speech, he was calling for and designing a new industrial policy.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_16_03 -->
He argued that it should work with markets without being passive.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_16 -->
在2011年10月26日发布的一篇演说中，英国商务大臣回忆了20世纪70年代末高度干预型政府所犯的错误：它挑选的“赢家”，往往最后成了代价高昂、难以维持的“跛脚鸭”。演说发表时，他正在倡议并设计一种新的产业政策。他主张，这种政策应当顺着市场的力量发挥作用，同时避免消极无为。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_16 -->
<!-- USE:U018_17 -->
## interventionist /ˌɪntərˈvenʃənɪst/ n. · 干预主义支持者
`可数名词，支持干预政策的人；支持的领域和方式须由上下文确定，不自动等于主战者。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_17 USE:U018_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/29850/pg29850-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R17_01 USE:U018_17 -->
Writing about Mexico in 1913, Lord and Bryan describe a split among interventionists.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R17_02 -->
Some wanted action against its ruler, Huerta, while others wanted to support him; economic interests complicated the situation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_17 -->
洛德和布赖恩谈到1913年的墨西哥时，描述了干预主义支持者内部的分歧：有些人想采取行动反对当时的统治者韦尔塔，另一些人则想支持他；经济利益又使局面更为复杂。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_17 -->
<!-- USE:U018_18 -->
## non-intervention /ˌnɑːnɪntərˈvenʃən/ n.; nonintervention /ˌnɑːnɪntərˈvenʃən/ n. · 不干涉原则或实践
`不可数名词，不介入他方事务、争端或冲突，也不帮助其中一方的政策或实践，尤其用于国家间事务；美式也写作 nonintervention，不因去掉连字符而另立意义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_18 USE:U018_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/41759/41759-h/41759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_18_01 USE:U018_18 -->
A review of some nineteenth-century cases shows that non-intervention was increasingly professed, while practice remained strongly influenced by political expediency.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_18 -->
回顾19世纪的一些案例可以看到，不干涉原则越来越常被公开宣扬，但实际做法仍深受政治权宜考量的影响。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_18 -->
<!-- USE:U018_19 -->
## non-interventionist /ˌnɑːnɪntərˈvenʃənɪst/ adj. · 奉行不干涉的
`形容不介入他方事务的政策或立场；不自动表示对任何公共问题都毫无行动。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_19 USE:U018_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/speeches/a-new-approach-to-trade-and-industry"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R19_01 USE:U018_19 -->
In his 2016 speech, Sajid Javid described his approach to industry as non-interventionist but highly engaged.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R19_02 -->
Rather than pick winning sectors, he proposed listening to businesses across the economy and removing barriers to growth.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_19 -->
在2016年的这篇演说中，赛义德·贾维德把自己的产业政策思路称为“不干预，但积极参与”。他提出，不去挑选获胜的产业，而应听取各行各业企业的意见，并排除增长障碍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_19 -->
<!-- USE:U018_20 -->
## non-interventionist /ˌnɑːnɪntərˈvenʃənɪst/ n. · 不干涉主义者
`可数名词，支持不干涉原则的人；其适用领域依讨论对象确定，不能直接等同于反对一切国际交往。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_20 USE:U018_20 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6456/pg6456.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R20_01 -->
Lippmann examines a campaign statement about Mexico.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R20_02 USE:U018_20 -->
Its talk of friendship appeals to non-interventionists, he argues, while its promise of a new, firm policy addresses interventionists.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_20 -->
李普曼分析了一段有关墨西哥的竞选声明。他认为，其中谈论友谊的话迎合不干涉主义者，而有关新政策和坚定态度的承诺则面向干预主义者。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_20 -->
<!-- USE:U018_21 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈviːn/ v. · 外在因素介入
`某个外来的因素或事物进入既有过程并参与影响结果；不及物用法。这里关注因果过程中的新增因素，不必含突然发生或使原计划中断的意思。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_21 USE:U018_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/72981/pg72981-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_21_01 USE:U018_21 -->
In his account of causal laws, Russell argues that a law can tell us what would follow an event if no other factors intervene.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_21_02 USE:U018_21 -->
In practice, he says, other factors usually do intervene, and the actual result reflects their combined effects.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_21 -->
罗素在论述因果规律时指出，一条规律可以告诉我们，在没有其他因素介入时，一个事件会引出怎样的后续结果。他说，实际上其他因素通常也会介入，真正的结果反映了它们的共同作用。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_21 -->
<!-- USE:U018_22 -->
## intervenient /ˌɪntərˈviːniənt/ n. · 介入其间者
`可数名词，指介入某事的人；在法律文件中可指参加诉讼、主张自身利益的第三方。此处是名词，不是修饰circumstances等名词的形容词用法，也不表示法院必定支持其请求。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C018_22 USE:U018_22 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/38460/38460-h/38460-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R22_01 -->
After being turned away from Alice Arden’s house, Longcluse considers asking Lady May to plead for him.
<!-- SENTENCE:S018_R22_02 USE:U018_22 -->
Instead, he decides to write directly to Alice, without an intervenient, though he struggles to compose a satisfactory letter.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C018_22 -->
被挡在艾丽斯·阿登家门外后，朗克卢斯考虑请梅夫人为自己说情。最终，他决定不经中间人，直接写信给艾丽斯，却又苦于写不出一封满意的信。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C018_22 -->

<!-- WORD:W019 -->
# justify

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 justificare，与 justus（正当的）和 facere（做）相关。基本联系是“表明或使之正当”；神学称义与印刷齐行已经形成专门意义，不能从普通辩护义直接猜出。

<!-- USE:U019_01 -->
## justify /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪ/ v. · 证明行为或主张有理
`及物动词，给出足够理由、证据，表明人、行为、决定、要求或论断正当、合理、必要或正确；也可作辩护、洗清责难。justify doing 接行为，不用 justify to do 代替；有理由不等于对方一定接受。近义表达中，warrant侧重情势足以支持行动，excuse侧重为过错开脱，vindicate侧重证明原判断或当事人正确；这里的justify可以是给行为提供正当论据。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_01 USE:U019_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_01_01 -->
One defence of private landownership claims that the state cannot abolish it because it is an individual right derived from nature.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_01_02 USE:U019_01 -->
Ryan argues that those premises do not justify the conclusion: the institution's moral basis depends on whether it serves human welfare.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_01 -->
为私人土地所有权辩护的一种说法是：它属于源于自然的个人权利，所以国家无权废除。瑞安认为，这些前提不足以支持该结论：这一制度的道德基础取决于它是否服务于人的福祉。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_01 -->
<!-- USE:U019_02 -->
## justify /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪ/ v. · 构成正当理由
`情势、证据等本身足以支持某行为、决定或判断；常见 be justified by 或 circumstance justifies action。区别于主动陈述理由：这里说的是依据是否足够。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_02 USE:U019_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/28178/28178-h/28178-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_02_01 USE:U019_02 -->
In this account of military decision-making, the circumstances may justify even a very great loss if the alternative is unacceptable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_02 -->
在这段关于军事决策的论述中，如果另一种选择无法接受，那么具体情势可能足以构成承受极大损失的理由。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_02 -->
<!-- USE:U019_03 -->
## justify /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪ/ v. · 调整字行使两端齐行
`印刷、计算机排版用法，通过调整字词间距等使字行均匀填满规定宽度；通常指两端对齐。它不同于只把整行靠左或靠右。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_03 USE:U019_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/directwrite/justification--kerning--and-spacing"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_03_01 USE:U019_03 -->
From Windows 8 onwards, DirectWrite provides methods that let an application justify text.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_03_02 -->
A custom layout can use JustifyGlyphAdvances to adjust glyph advances to fit the line width.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_03 -->
从Windows 8开始，DirectWrite提供了让应用程序对文字进行两端对齐的方法。自定义布局可以使用JustifyGlyphAdvances，调整字形的前进宽度，使其适合字行宽度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_03 -->
<!-- USE:U019_04 -->
## justify /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪ/ v. · 宣告某人称义
`神学用法，宣告某人在上帝面前为义；宾语为人，常见被动 justified。不能简单译成替某人的一切行为找借口，具体教义依原文本。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_04 USE:U019_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/6038/pg6038-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_04_01 USE:U019_04 -->
Evans teaches that a person is justified through faith in Jesus Christ rather than through works of the law.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_04 -->
埃文斯教导说，人借着对耶稣基督的信而称义，而不是靠遵行律法的行为。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_04 -->
<!-- USE:U019_05 -->
## justifiable /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪəbəl/ adj. · 有充分理由可辩护的
`行为、处境、感情或想法由于有好理由而可接受、可认为正当；可用 justifiable on the grounds of 指明依据。强调能够被合理辩护，不是已经被法院判定合法的通用标签。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_05 USE:U019_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/28178/28178-h/28178-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_05_01 USE:U019_05 -->
Movement that offers no advantage is scarcely justifiable, even if it causes no material loss.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_05_02 -->
Moving merely for the sake of moving is not a profitable military operation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_05 -->
不能带来任何优势的机动，即使不造成物质损失，也几乎没有正当理由。仅仅为了移动而移动，并不是有益的军事行动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_05 -->
<!-- USE:U019_06 -->
## justifiably /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪəbli/ adv. · 有充分理由地
`表示某种反应、判断、感受有正当理由，如 justifiably proud；修饰动作或形容词，不等于当事人自称有理由。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_06 USE:U019_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/28178/28178-h/28178-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_06_01 USE:U019_06 -->
In a chain of command, responsibility and authority cannot justifiably be separated.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_06_02 -->
Authority must be properly proportioned to responsibility.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_06 -->
在指挥链中，责任与权限不能被合理地割裂开来。权限必须与责任恰当相称。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_06 -->
<!-- USE:U019_07 -->
## justifiability /ˌdʒʌstəfaɪəˈbɪləti/ n.; justifiableness /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪəbəlnəs/ n. · 可合理辩护的性质
`通常不可数，指某行为、政策或判断能够被充足理由支持的性质；两种词形均指可辩护性，不把争论中的可辩护性写成已经证实的事实。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_07 USE:U019_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/57877/57877-h/57877-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_07_01 -->
Around 1890, Raikes became less hostile to trade unionism in the Post Office.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_07_02 USE:U019_07 -->
His change may have owed as much to expediency as to a conviction of its justifiableness.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_07 -->
大约在1890年，雷克斯对邮政部门工会运动的敌意有所缓和。他的转变出于权宜考虑的程度，可能不亚于出于对这种运动正当性的认可。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_07 -->
<!-- USE:U019_08 -->
## justifiable homicide /ˌdʒʌstəfaɪəbəl ˈhɑːməsaɪd/ n. · 法律认定正当的杀人
`法律名词，指被视为合法且不负刑事罪责的致人死亡行为；某些制度历史上包括执行死刑等情况。具体范围随法域与条件变化，不能只因动机可理解就断言属于此类。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_08 USE:U019_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://app.leg.wa.gov/RCW/default.aspx?cite=9A.16.046"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_08_01 -->
Washington State's RCW 9A.16.046 covers peace officers charged with a crime for actions taken on duty or otherwise within their authority.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_08_02 USE:U019_08 -->
If such an officer is found not guilty, or the charges are dismissed, by reason of justifiable homicide under RCW 9A.16.040, the state must reimburse reasonable defence costs.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_08_03 -->
This reimbursement is not an independent cause of action.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_08 -->
华盛顿州RCW 9A.16.046适用于因在职务期间或以其他方式在权限范围内采取的行动而被控犯罪的警员。如果这类警员因行为属于RCW 9A.16.040规定的正当杀人而被判无罪，或因此撤销指控，州政府必须偿付合理辩护费用。这种偿付本身并不构成独立的诉讼请求依据。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_08 -->
<!-- USE:U019_09 -->
## justification /ˌdʒʌstəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n. · 足以支持的理由或论证
`可数或不可数，指证明行为、观点等合理的理由、解释或论证过程；常用 justification for，a justification 指一项理由，without justification 表示没有正当依据。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_09 USE:U019_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_09_01 -->
Ryan considers losses that new land taxes might impose on owners and says that compensating every such loss would be impracticable.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_09_02 USE:U019_09 -->
The justification for a law imposing these losses, if one exists, must therefore rest on social considerations.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_09 -->
瑞安讨论了新土地税可能给所有者造成的损失，并认为补偿所有这类损失并不现实。因此，一项造成这些损失的法律，其正当理由——如果确有这样的理由——必须建立在社会层面的考虑上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_09 -->
<!-- USE:U019_10 -->
## justification /ˌdʒʌstəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n. · 字行两端对齐
`排版中调整字行使其均匀占满规定宽度的过程或效果；不是一般意义上的内容论证，区别于单侧 alignment。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_10 USE:U019_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/directwrite/justification--kerning--and-spacing"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_10_01 USE:U019_10 -->
Justification aligns text to fill the width of a column.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_10_02 USE:U019_10 -->
Depending on the script, it can increase the advances between characters or glyph clusters, or add justification characters.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_10 -->
两端对齐会调整文字排列，使其填满一栏的宽度。根据文字系统的不同，可以增大字符或字形簇之间的前进宽度，也可以添加用于齐行的字符。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_10 -->
<!-- USE:U019_11 -->
## justification /ˌdʒʌstəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n. · 称义
`神学中宣告人为义的行为或状态；与普通“提供理由”及排版“齐行”分别理解，不能根据现代日常义改写教义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_11 USE:U019_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/64185/64185-h/64185-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_11_01 USE:U019_11 -->
In the catechism presented by Ridgley, justification is an act of God's free grace: sinners are pardoned and counted righteous in God's sight.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_11_02 -->
It rests on Christ's obedience and satisfaction, received through faith alone, rather than on anything the sinners have done.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_11 -->
在里奇利呈现的教理问答中，称义是上帝白白施恩的行为：罪人获得赦免，并被上帝视为义人。它建立在基督的顺服与赎罪之上，单借信心领受，而不是依靠罪人所做的任何事。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_11 -->
<!-- USE:U019_12 -->
## justificatory /ˈdʒʌstəfəˌkeɪtəri/ adj. · 起辩护论证作用的
`形容为某行为、观点等提供正当理由的论述或材料；说明其作用，不保证理由实际充分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_12 USE:U019_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/27289/27289-h/27289-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_R12_01 USE:U019_12 -->
Sloane calls the Spanish king’s ministers’ accounts justificatory writings: they claimed Napoleon’s envoy had encouraged the king to meet the Emperor.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_R12_02 -->
He also stresses that there is no conclusive evidence of what the envoy actually said or promised.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_12 -->
斯隆把西班牙国王的大臣们所写的记述称为辩护性文字：他们声称，拿破仑的特使曾鼓动国王前去与皇帝会面。斯隆同时强调，对于特使究竟说过什么、作过什么承诺，并没有确凿证据。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_12 -->
<!-- USE:U019_13 -->
## justifier /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪər/ n. · 提供正当依据者
`可数名词，指提出论证、为某行为或主张提供正当依据的人或事物；不一定是律师，也不保证其论据确实充分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_13 USE:U019_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/E9284ADBF253563A7C7AE3DC3D26CFB9/S1742360024000601a.pdf/defense_of_doxastic_defeaters.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_13R_01 USE:U019_13 -->
Tiozzo examines whether beliefs lacking evidence can nevertheless act as justifiers for other beliefs.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_13R_02 -->
His defence concerns coherence among a person’s beliefs; he distinguishes that from having evidence that makes a belief reasonable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_13 -->
蒂奥佐考察了这样一个问题：缺少证据的信念，能否仍为其他信念提供正当依据。他的辩护涉及一个人的各项信念能否彼此一致；他把这种一致性与拥有使信念合理的证据区分开来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_13 -->
<!-- USE:U019_14 -->
## justified /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪd/ adj. · 合理而有根据的
`形容决定、行为、观点本身有充分理由、正当可接受；常用 fully、wholly 等修饰。与 justifiable 相近，这里侧重有理的状态或判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_14 USE:U019_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/28178/28178-h/28178-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_14_01 USE:U019_14 -->
A leader should distinguish overbearing arrogance, born of ignorance, from the pride that comes from justified self-respect.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_14 -->
领导者应当区分出于无知而盛气凌人的傲慢，与基于合理自尊的自豪。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_14 -->
<!-- USE:U019_15 -->
## justified /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪd/ adj. · 做某事有正当理由的
`以人为主语，用 be justified in doing，表示该人做某事有充分依据或理由；in 后接动名词，不用不定式直接替代。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_15 USE:U019_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_15_01 USE:U019_15 -->
On Ryan's account, a person is justified in appropriating particular property only by some title to it.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_15_02 -->
A title is original when the property was previously ownerless, and derived when it comes from a previous owner.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_15 -->
按照瑞安的论述，一个人只有具有某种权利根据，才有正当理由取得某项财产。若该财产此前无主，这种根据就是原始的；若财产来自前任所有者，这种根据就是继受的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_15 -->
<!-- USE:U019_16 -->
## left-justify /ˌleft ˈdʒʌstəfaɪ/ v. · 使文字左对齐
`排版及物动词，使各行起点距页面或栏左边缘相同，右边缘可参差；常见 left-justified，也可在对象已知时不明说宾语。单元格数据同样可设此对齐。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_16 USE:U019_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://docs.python.org/3/builtins/stdtypes.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_16_01 USE:U019_16 -->
Python's str.ljust pads a string on the right, using spaces by default, so that it is left justified within the requested width.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_16_02 -->
If that width is no greater than the original string's length, the original string is returned unchanged.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_16 -->
Python的str.ljust在字符串右侧填充字符，默认使用空格，使字符串在指定宽度内左对齐。如果该宽度不大于原字符串的长度，就原样返回原字符串。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_16 -->
<!-- USE:U019_17 -->
## right-justify /ˌraɪt ˈdʒʌstəfaɪ/ v. · 使文字右对齐
`排版及物动词，使各行终点距页面或栏右边缘相同，左边缘可以参差；不是把字符顺序倒过来。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_17 USE:U019_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://docs.python.org/3/builtins/stdtypes.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_17_01 USE:U019_17 -->
Python's str.rjust pads a string on the left, using spaces by default, so that it is right justified within the requested width.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_17_02 -->
If that width is no greater than the original string's length, the original string is returned unchanged.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_17 -->
Python的str.rjust在字符串左侧填充字符，默认使用空格，使字符串在指定宽度内右对齐。如果该宽度不大于原字符串的长度，就原样返回原字符串。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_17 -->
<!-- USE:U019_18 -->
## self-justification /ˌself ˌdʒʌstəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n. · 为自己辩白
`为本人、自己的行为等给出理由或开脱；通常不可数，可用于对自我辩护的评价，不保证这种解释诚实或充分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_18 USE:U019_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/47394/pg47394-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_R18_01 USE:U019_18 -->
When Polly says their visitor is not Billy Kellogg, Rosalind answers as if in self-justification: he arrived using that name and seemed to know their friends.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_R18_02 -->
Polly admits she had pretended to recognise him because she did not want to make a scene.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_18 -->
波莉说来客并非比利·凯洛格，罗莎琳德便仿佛在为自己辩白：那人是以这个名字登船的，看起来也认识她们的朋友。波莉承认，为了不当场闹起来，她曾假装认出他。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_18 -->
<!-- USE:U019_19 -->
## self-justifying /ˌself ˈdʒʌstəfaɪɪŋ/ adj. · 为自身辩护的
`形容言论、态度等旨在证明自己或自己的行动有理；重点是辩护指向自己，不是理由已经成立。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_19 USE:U019_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4607781/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_19R_01 USE:U019_19 -->
Patterson and colleagues discuss self-justifying rationalization in explanations that shift blame or make excuses.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_19R_02 -->
Someone may sincerely find an explanation credible partly because it reduces their responsibility; the authors do not suggest that such motives let people believe absolutely anything about themselves.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_19 -->
帕特森及同事讨论了在推卸责任或找借口的解释中，替自己辩护的合理化倾向。一个人可能真心认为某个解释可信，而其中一部分原因是这个解释减轻了自己的责任；作者并不认为，这类动机能让人对自己相信任何事情。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_19 -->
<!-- USE:U019_20 -->
## unjustifiable /ʌnˈdʒʌstəfaɪəbəl/ adj. · 无正当理由可辩护的
`形容行为、尤其伤害他人的行为没有好理由、无法合理开脱，常带明确否定评价；不能因为某决定有争议就自动作此断言。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_20 USE:U019_20 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://assets.publishing.service.gov.uk/media/672a413a40f7da695c921b9a/190320_AnnexA_ListOfMinistersInterests.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_20R_01 USE:U019_20 -->
The Cabinet Office’s March 2019 introduction calls publishing every private interest and financial arrangement of ministers and their close families an unjustifiable intrusion.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_20R_02 -->
It lists interests that are, or might reasonably be seen as, directly relevant to a minister’s public duties.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_20 -->
英国内阁办公室在2019年3月的名单引言中指出，若公布大臣及其近亲属所有私人利益与财务安排，就会构成无正当理由的隐私侵扰。因此，这份名单只列实际与大臣公务直接相关，或可合理地被视为直接相关的利益。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_20 -->
<!-- USE:U019_21 -->
## unjustifiably /ʌnˈdʒʌstəfaɪəbli/ adv. · 没有正当依据地
`修饰行动、判断或程度，表示缺乏足够理由，因而不公平或不合理；不是只说明当事人没有公开解释。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_21 USE:U019_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/52264/pg52264-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_21_01 USE:U019_21 -->
A writer quoted in a letter defending William Palmer argued that Lord Campbell had unjustifiably suggested to the jury that Professor Taylor might not have used the proper chemical tests.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_21 -->
在一封为威廉·帕尔默辩护的信中，一位被引述的作者认为，坎贝尔勋爵向陪审团暗示泰勒教授可能没有使用恰当的化学检验方法，这种做法毫无正当依据。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_21 -->
<!-- USE:U019_22 -->
## unjustified /ˌʌnˈdʒʌstəfaɪd/ adj. · 缺乏根据或未获证明的
`形容信念、指控、行为等没有足够理由、未被证明正当或正确；可强调证据不足，也可作不合理评价。与 unjustifiable 相比，不必都含“根本不可能辩护”的同等强度。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_22 USE:U019_22 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/E9284ADBF253563A7C7AE3DC3D26CFB9/S1742360024000601a.pdf/defense_of_doxastic_defeaters.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_22R_01 USE:U019_22 -->
Tiozzo gives an example of an unjustified belief: someone distrusts an informant simply because the informant looks suspicious, without evidence for that judgement.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_22R_02 -->
That belief may fit with the person’s other beliefs, but this is a question of coherence rather than evidential support.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_22 -->
蒂奥佐举了一个缺乏依据的信念的例子：一个人仅仅因为提供消息者看上去可疑，就不信任对方，却没有证据支持这个判断。这种信念可能与此人的其他信念相合，但这涉及的是彼此是否一致，而不是是否有证据支持。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_22 -->
<!-- USE:U019_23 -->
## justifier /ˈdʒʌstəfaɪər/ n. · 使人称义者
`神学语境中指宣告人为义者，尤其用于上帝；它对应 justify 的称义义，不是指上帝替不正当行为寻找借口。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C019_23 USE:U019_23 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/64185/64185-h/64185-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_23_01 -->
Ridgley argues that Christ's obedience and suffering satisfy divine justice.
<!-- SENTENCE:S019_23_02 USE:U019_23 -->
On that basis, God appears both just and the justifier of the person who believes in Jesus.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C019_23 -->
里奇利主张，基督的顺服与受苦满足了神圣公义。在这个基础上，上帝既显为公义，又是使信耶稣之人称义的那一位。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C019_23 -->

<!-- WORD:W020 -->
# perspective

**词源**：来自中世纪拉丁语有关视学的词，与 perspicere（看透、仔细看）相关，per- 表“穿过”，后部与“看”有关。绘画义的形成还受意大利语 prospettiva（透视法）影响；由视觉中的远近关系，又发展到衡量事情相对重要性的看法。

<!-- USE:U020_01 -->
## perspective /pərˈspektɪv/ n. · 看待问题的角度
`可数名词，指受经验、信念等影响的一种看法或观察角度；常用 perspective on 某事、from the perspective of 某人，也可有 historical perspective 等，并不表示唯一正确的结论。近义表达中，outlook偏总体看法，attitude偏态度，angle偏切入问题的角度；实际选词须看想强调的是哪一层。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_01 USE:U020_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/guidance/further-education-estates-planning/strategic-estate-planning"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_01_01 -->
Check with curriculum staff that they have been consulted and involved in creating the estates strategy.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_01_02 USE:U020_01 -->
You need to be sure that their perspective has been heard.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_01 -->
应向课程教学人员核实：他们是否已获得征询，并参与了校舍规划策略的制定。你需要确认，他们的看法确实得到了倾听。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_01 -->
<!-- USE:U020_02 -->
## in perspective /ɪn pərˈspektɪv/ adv.; into perspective /ˈɪntuː pərˈspektɪv/ adv. · 放在整体中恰当衡量
`把某事与其他事情联系起来，正确判断其相对重要性；keep something in perspective 表示保持这种判断，put/get something into perspective 表示使之获得。这里表达判断方式，不是绘画位置。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_02 USE:U020_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/16225/pg16225-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_02_01 USE:U020_02 -->
Only after some time has passed, and we have worked through practical points at leisure, can we see the music-training course in perspective and appreciate how much it has covered.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_02 -->
只有过了一段时间，并有机会从容推敲一些实际问题之后，我们才能从整体上恰当地看待这门音乐训练课程，认识到它究竟覆盖了多少内容。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_02 -->
<!-- USE:U020_03 -->
## out of perspective /aʊt əv pərˈspektɪv/ adv. · 对相对重要性判断失当
`把某事的重要性放错比例，常指夸大或忽略整体关系；get something out of perspective 不只意味着换了一个主观看法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_03 USE:U020_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://hansard.parliament.uk/Commons/1987-01-29/debates/f46ac87b-3292-4347-a25a-171389114b46/PublicExpendtiture"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_03R_01 USE:U020_03 -->
In a 1987 parliamentary exchange, Mr Campbell-Savours warned that a moratorium on regional development grants would cost jobs and prevent firms from expanding.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_03R_02 -->
Mr MacGregor replied that he had the issue out of perspective: total grant and assistance spending was due to rise, and the change would spread payments across the year rather than stop them for two months.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_03 -->
在1987年的一场议会问答中，坎贝尔-萨沃斯先生警告说，暂停支付地区发展补助会使人失业，并妨碍企业扩张。麦格雷戈先生回答说，对方对事情轻重的判断失了比例：补助与援助支出的总额计划增加，而这项变动是把付款分散到全年，并非停止付款两个月。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_03 -->
<!-- USE:U020_04 -->
## perspective /pərˈspektɪv/ n. · 透视画法
`不可数名词，在二维平面表现立体物体的相对位置、大小、远近，使画面呈现空间关系的画法；不仅是把远处画小这一条技巧。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_04 USE:U020_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/20165/pg20165-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_04_01 USE:U020_04 -->
We have explained the theory of perspective; now we must apply it to drawing figures and the objects we wish to depict.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_04 -->
我们已经解释了透视法的理论；现在必须把它用于绘制图形，以及我们想要描画的各种物体。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_04 -->
<!-- USE:U020_05 -->
## perspective /pərˈspektɪv/ n. · 透视图
`可数名词，指按透视法绘出的具体图画，与表示技法的不可数义区别；a perspective 可是一幅供观察空间关系的图。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_05 USE:U020_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/75563/pg75563-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_05R_01 USE:U020_05 -->
Windoes offers a perspective of a cedar chest as a model, rather than a design students must copy exactly.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_05R_02 -->
He encourages them to combine details from the examples and add their own ideas to create an original design.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_05 -->
温多斯提供了一幅雪松木箱的透视图，作为学生参考的范样，而不是必须照搬的设计。他鼓励学生结合各个示例中的细节，再加入自己的构思，形成原创设计。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_05 -->
<!-- USE:U020_06 -->
## perspective /pərˈspektɪv/ n. · 视觉中的远近位置关系
`通常不可数，指可见物体之间呈现出来的相对位置、距离、大小等关系或效果；真实观察中也有这种关系，不限于画纸。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_06 USE:U020_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/72981/pg72981-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_06_01 USE:U020_06 -->
In Russell’s example, people viewing the same scene from different positions receive stimuli that differ in perspective, light and shade, and apparent size.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_06_02 -->
Photographs taken from those positions would reproduce the differences, which also affect the observers’ reactions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_06 -->
罗素举例说，人们从不同位置观看同一场景时，到达各自眼睛的刺激在透视关系、明暗和表观大小等方面会有所不同。从这些位置拍摄的照片会再现这些差别，而这些差别也会影响观察者的反应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_06 -->
<!-- USE:U020_07 -->
## perspective /pərˈspektɪv/ n. · 景观与展望景象
`可指从某处展开的地理景观，也可指想象中的景象或前景；重点是展现在眼前或心中的一幅景象，区别于表达论点的“观点”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_07 USE:U020_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/41150/pg41150-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_07R_01 USE:U020_07 -->
After a seemingly endless forest of pines, Beckford’s party descended a long avenue, with perspectives opening on either side.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_07R_02 -->
Beyond it lay a sunlit valley of grazing herds, a winding rivulet and cultivated slopes, enclosed by hills and rocky heights.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_07 -->
穿过一片仿佛无边无际的松林后，贝克福德一行沿着一条长长的林荫道下行，两侧景观不断展开。林荫道外是一片洒满阳光的山谷：牲畜在吃草，小河蜿蜒而过，开垦的坡地由山丘和嶙峋高地环抱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_07 -->
<!-- USE:U020_08 -->
## perspective /pərˈspektɪv/ adj. · 采用透视法的
`形容按透视法表现的图画、绘制方式等；置于名词前时是形容词义，不要与名词表示观察角度混为一谈。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_08 USE:U020_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/20165/pg20165-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_08_01 USE:U020_08 -->
The two perspective lines converge at the point of sight and represent parallels.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_08_02 -->
Cross-lines drawn between them, parallel to the base, represent equal widths.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_08 -->
这两条透视线在画面的视心处汇聚，表现的是一组平行线。画在它们之间、且与基线平行的横线，表示相等的实际宽度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_08 -->
<!-- USE:U020_09 -->
## in perspective /ɪn pərˈspektɪv/ adv.; out of perspective /aʊt əv pərˈspektɪv/ adv. · 符合或不合透视比例地
`描述物体被绘制或观看时是否符合透视规则；in 表示符合，out of 表示不符。与衡量事情轻重的比喻义对象不同，这里谈视觉空间比例。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_09 USE:U020_09 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/20165/pg20165-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_09_01 USE:U020_09 -->
To draw the hexagon in perspective we must first find the rectangle in which it is inscribed, according to the view we take of it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_09 -->
要按透视法画出六边形，首先必须根据所取的观察角度，找出它所内接的矩形。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_09 -->
<!-- USE:U020_10 -->
## perspectival /pərˈspektɪvəl/ adj. · 与透视或观察视角有关的
`形容透视关系或观察角度相关的性质，依实际对象判断是视觉空间还是思考视角；不自动表示主观偏见。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_10 USE:U020_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://bop.unibe.ch/JEMR/article/download/JEMR.13.2.15/11067"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_10R_01 USE:U020_10 -->
Crucq’s pilot eye-tracking study asks how people look at perspectival paintings.
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_10R_02 -->
Do converging lines guide the gaze towards the vanishing point, or do important figures and objects placed there attract attention first?
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_10 -->
克鲁克的试点眼动研究探讨人们如何观看采用透视法的绘画：是汇聚的线条把视线引向消失点，还是那里重要的人物和物体首先吸引了注意？
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_10 -->
<!-- USE:U020_11 -->
## perspectively /pərˈspektɪvli/ adv. · 按透视方式地
`表示以透视关系来观看或表现；修饰绘制、排列等方式，不是普通“将来地”或 prospective 的副词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C020_11 USE:U020_11 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/20165/pg20165-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S020_11_01 USE:U020_11 -->
Horizontals in the same plane which are drawn to the same point on the horizon are perspectively parallel to each other.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C020_11 -->
同一平面内、画向地平线上同一个点的各条水平线，在透视表现中对应于相互平行的线。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C020_11 -->

<!-- WORD:W021 -->
# plausible

**词源**：来自拉丁语 plausibilis，原指“值得鼓掌赞许的”，与表示“鼓掌”的 plaudere 有关。由“让人接受”发展到“听来可信、似乎合理”；听来可信并不保证实际为真。

<!-- USE:U021_01 -->
## plausible /ˈplɔzəbəl/ adj. · 似乎合理可信的
`说法、解释或论点听起来合情理、有可能为真，但说话者并未因此保证它已经证实。a plausible explanation 是“说得通的解释”，不能直接等同于有证据的结论。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_01 USE:U021_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"似乎合理可信的","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/universe/nasa-study-shows-disks-dont-need-planets-to-make-patterns/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_01a -->
Rings in dusty disks around young stars can suggest orbiting planets.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_01b -->
But a 2013 NASA study found in simulations that interactions between gas and dust could form such patterns under suitable conditions, without planets.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_01c USE:U021_01 -->
The researchers concluded that photoelectric instability provides a simple and plausible explanation for many features in debris disks.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_01 -->
年轻恒星周围尘埃盘中的环状结构，可能暗示有行星在运行。但NASA在2013年的一项研究通过模拟发现，在适当条件下，气体与尘埃的相互作用也可能在没有行星的情况下形成这类图案。研究者认为，光电不稳定性为碎屑盘中的许多结构提供了一个简单而说得通的解释。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_01 -->
<!-- USE:U021_02 -->
## plausible /ˈplɔzəbəl/ adj. · 说得像真的、善于取信的
`形容人表现得真诚、诚实，说话很有说服力，实际上可能在欺骗。重点在令人信以为真的外表；用它形容人时要留意这种潜在的负面意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_02 USE:U021_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"说得像真的、善于取信的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/105/pg105-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_02a USE:U021_02 -->
Mrs Smith describes Mrs Clay as a clever, insinuating woman, poor and plausible, whose situation and manner lead people to suspect that she means to become Lady Elliot.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_02 -->
史密斯太太形容克莱太太是个聪明而善于讨好人的女人，既贫穷又善于取信于人；她的处境和举止使人怀疑，她打算成为埃利奥特爵士夫人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_02 -->
<!-- USE:U021_03 -->
## plausibly /ˈplɔzəbli/ adv. · 说得通地、貌似可信地
`修饰解释、主张或其他行为，表示做得让人觉得合理、可信；也可限定一句判断只是有合理可能，而非已经证明。它不能把推测升级为事实。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_03 USE:U021_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"说得通地、貌似可信地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_03a USE:U021_03 -->
If a dramatist gives his dialogue the necessary concentration, yet keeps it plausibly near to the language of life, he has achieved style and may snap his fingers at the critics.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_03b -->
Style, in prose drama, is the sifting of common speech.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_03 -->
如果剧作家让对白足够凝练，同时又使它可信地贴近日常语言，他就已经形成了风格，大可不必在意评论家的指责。散文剧的风格，就是对普通言语的筛选提炼。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_03 -->
<!-- USE:U021_04 -->
## plausibility /ˌplɔzəˈbɪləti/ n. · 表面可信性、合理性
`指一个说法、情节或解释让人觉得说得通的程度，通常不可数；常说 the plausibility of a theory 或 question its plausibility。可相信的外观与真实性、证据强度仍须分开。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_04 USE:U021_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"表面可信性、合理性","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_04a USE:U021_04 -->
On stage, plausibility matters more than demonstrable probability.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_04b -->
In the rush of dramatic action, there is no time to calculate the chances of an event or prove experimentally that it can happen.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_04 -->
在舞台上，情节显得可信，比能够论证的发生概率更重要。戏剧动作迅速推进时，观众没有时间计算某件事发生的几率，也没有时间用实验证明它是否可能发生。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_04 -->
<!-- USE:U021_05 -->
## implausible /ɪmˈplɔzəbəl/ adj.; unplausible /ʌnˈplɔzəbəl/ adj. · 不像真的、难以信服的
`表示说法、解释或情节显得不合情理、不太可能为真。unplausible 同样表示 not plausible；这里教它的否定义，不把两种拼写当作两种不同程度，也不把“不大可信”误成逻辑上绝无可能。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_05 USE:U021_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"不像真的、难以信服的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/rpc-opinion-review-of-the-control-of-asbestos-regulations/rpc-opinion-first-post-implementation-review-control-of-asbestos-regulations"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_05a -->
An asbestos-regulation review compares continued controls with a scenario in which the regulations are withdrawn and businesses stop all the previously required measures.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_05b USE:U021_05 -->
It recognises that the latter scenario is implausible, but uses it to compare the costs of those measures.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_05c -->
It also considers businesses gradually stopping only some measures.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_05 -->
一份石棉监管评估将继续采取控制措施的情形，与撤销这些法规后企业停止所有原先规定措施的假设情形作比较。评估承认，后一种情形不太可能发生，但仍以此比较采取这些措施的成本。它还考虑了企业逐渐停止其中一部分措施的情形。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_05 -->
<!-- USE:U021_06 -->
## implausibly /ɪmˈplɔzəbli/ adv. · 令人难以相信地
`修饰发生方式、程度或整项判断，表示它显得不大可能或不合情理；并不自动断言事情没有发生。可用来指出故事发展得过分巧合，也可评论真实情况出人意料。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_06 USE:U021_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"令人难以相信地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/energy-performance-of-buildings-certificates-in-england-and-wales-change-note/energy-performance-of-buildings-certificates-in-england-and-wales-change-note"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_06a -->
The 2021 change note for energy-certificate statistics in England and Wales announced that the live tables would start in the fourth quarter of 2008 rather than the first.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_06b USE:U021_06 -->
Inconsistencies in the early data led to implausibly high CO2 emission rates and heating costs.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_06c -->
Removing those data would make the statistics and comparisons more reliable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_06 -->
2021年英格兰和威尔士建筑能源证书统计修订说明宣布，在线数据表的时间序列将改从2008年第四季度开始，而不再从第一季度开始。早期数据中的不一致导致了高得令人难以相信的二氧化碳排放率与供暖费用。剔除这些数据，将使统计和比较更加可靠。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_06 -->
<!-- USE:U021_07 -->
## implausibility /ɪmˌplɔzəˈbɪləti/ n. · 难以置信之处或性质
`指说法、情节等不合情理、难使人信服的性质；也可指其中具体不可信之处。讨论证据时，它是对可信程度的判断，并不是“已经证明为假”的同义词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C021_07 USE:U021_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"难以置信之处或性质","sourceUrls":["https://tribunalsdecisions.service.gov.uk/utiac/ui-2023-003948"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_07a USE:U021_07 -->
In its October 2024 decision, the Upper Tribunal stated that an assessment of implausibility must take into account relevant background country material.
<!-- SENTENCE:S021_07b -->
A story must not be rejected simply because it would seem unreasonable if it had happened in the United Kingdom.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C021_07 -->
在2024年10月的一份裁决中，英国上级裁判所指出，判断一段陈述是否不合情理，必须考虑有关国家背景的材料。不能仅仅因为同样的事情若发生在英国会显得不合理，就否定这段陈述。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C021_07 -->

<!-- WORD:W022 -->
# precedent

**词源**：经法语进入英语，源于拉丁语 praecedere“走在前面”。先发生的事可成为后来处理同类事件的参照，因此有“先例”；形容词则仍保留“在先”的意思。

<!-- USE:U022_01 -->
## precedent /ˈprɛsədənt/ n. · 可供援引的先例
`较正式地指以前的事例，尤其可作为后来同类案件的处理依据或再次行动的理由。可数时指一项先例；无冠词的 precedent 可泛指先例依据。常说 set a precedent、a precedent for something、without precedent；有先例不等于任何情况下都必须照办。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_01 USE:U022_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可供援引的先例","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/dao-0226-letter-novel-contentious-or-repercussive-spending/dao-0226-letter-novel-contentious-or-repercussive-spending--2"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_01a USE:U022_01 -->
Spending or announcements may set a precedent or create expectations of future spending within a department or across government.
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_01b -->
Accounting officers should consider whether even non-binding expectations could create pressure to take similar approaches elsewhere.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_01 -->
支出或公告可能在部门内部或整个政府范围内开创先例，或引发对未来支出的预期。会计责任官应当考虑：即使预期没有法律约束力，它是否仍会造成在其他地方采取类似做法的压力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_01 -->
<!-- USE:U022_02 -->
## precedent /prɪˈsidənt/ adj. · 在时间、次序等方面在先的
`表示时间、顺序或重要性上先于另一事物。法律中的 condition precedent 是先决条件，即须先满足、相应权利或义务才发生的条件；这里的 precedent 是形容词，读音可区别于名词“先例”，也有 /ˈprɛsədənt/ 的读法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_02 USE:U022_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"在时间、次序等方面在先的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_02a -->
In some forms of drama, truth of character is impaired or nullified unless the events are plausible.
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_02b USE:U022_02 -->
The plausibility of events is its indispensable condition precedent.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_02 -->
在某些戏剧形式中，如果情节事件不可信，人物的真实感便会受到削弱，甚至完全失效。情节事件可信，是这种人物真实感不可缺少的先决条件。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_02 -->
<!-- USE:U022_03 -->
## precedented /ˈprɛsədɛntɪd/ adj. · 有先例可援的
`表示事情以前有过同类先例，或做法得到先例支持。它与 unprecedented 的否定关系在于“有无前例”，并不自行说明做法好坏或先例是否仍适用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_03 USE:U022_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"有先例可援的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/9486/pg9486-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_03a USE:U022_03 -->
Mac says, with exaggerated gravity, “Doing evil for the sake of a prospective good is an immemorial custom, and well precedented.”
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_03b -->
“But seriously, Mac,” Clarian asks, “is it not admissible, now and then, to employ questionable means, ordinary ones failing?”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_03 -->
麦克故作一本正经地说：“为了预期的善而作恶，是自古就有的习惯，也大有先例可援。”“不过，麦克，说正经的，”克拉里安问，“通常的办法都不管用时，偶尔使用有问题的手段，难道不可以吗？”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_03 -->
<!-- USE:U022_04 -->
## precedently /ˈprisədəntli/ adv.; precedently /ˈprɛsədəntli/ adv. · 在先、先前地
`表示在时间或次序上先前发生。它对应形容词 precedent 的“在先”义，不能把它理解为“依照法律先例”；具体哪一件事在先，要由上下文交代。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_04 USE:U022_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"在先、先前地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/29583/pg29583-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_04a -->
Josephine has slipped open the sliding doors to listen to Richard Crawford and his cousin.
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_04b USE:U022_04 -->
What might have passed precedently, while she was looking after the chicken and the bread-and-butter, she has no means of divining.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_04 -->
约瑟芬悄悄把移门推开一条缝，偷听理查德·克劳福德和他一位堂表亲的谈话。至于刚才她张罗鸡肉和面包黄油时，两人谈过些什么，她却无从推知。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_04 -->
<!-- USE:U022_05 -->
## unprecedented /ʌnˈprɛsədɛntɪd/ adj. · 从未有过先例的
`表示此前没有发生过可相比的事情，或某种做法、局面是全新的。常见 in unprecedented times、an unprecedented event；判断的历史范围须由语境确定，不能把某地或某时期的首次说成全人类首次。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_05 USE:U022_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"从未有过先例的","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/missions/chandra/nasa-discovers-crash-of-extreme-stars-in-unexpected-site/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_05a -->
In March 2026, NASA reported a likely neutron-star collision in a tiny galaxy.
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_05b USE:U022_05 -->
This unprecedented location may help explain why some gamma-ray bursts do not appear to have host galaxies.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_05 -->
2026年3月，NASA报告说，一次中子星碰撞很可能发生在一个微小星系中。这种前所未见的位置，或许有助于解释为什么有些伽马射线暴似乎没有宿主星系。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_05 -->
<!-- USE:U022_06 -->
## unprecedented /ʌnˈprɛsədɛntɪd/ adj. · 规模或程度空前的
`用来强调质量、数量、规模等极大，常放名词前，如 unprecedented success。它可能是在赞扬或强调达到以往未有的程度，并非说这类事情本身第一次出现；引用宣传或评价时仍应保留是谁作出的判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_06 USE:U022_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"规模或程度空前的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/news/mdg-summit-outcomes-uk-hails-unprecedented-success"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_06a USE:U022_06 -->
Following the September 2010 UN Millennium Development Goals Summit, the UK government hailed its outcomes as an unprecedented success.
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_06b -->
It praised new global commitments to save women and children and to combat malaria, hunger and under-nutrition.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_06 -->
2010年9月联合国千年发展目标峰会之后，英国政府称赞峰会成果取得了空前成功。它赞扬了旨在拯救妇女和儿童生命、对抗疟疾、饥饿与营养不良的新一轮全球承诺。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_06 -->
<!-- USE:U022_07 -->
## unprecedentedly /ʌnˈprɛsədɛntɪdli/ adv. · 前所未有地
`修饰形容词或行动，表示程度、方式或规模此前未有，如 unprecedentedly high。范围和比较基准须由上下文确定；它不是单纯的 very，也不自动意味着有利。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C022_07 USE:U022_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"前所未有地","sourceUrls":["https://www.pmel.noaa.gov/news-and-media/highlights/cascade-extreme-events-transforming-bering-sea-challenging-communities"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S022_07a USE:U022_07 -->
Unprecedentedly low sea ice cover in the northern Bering and Chukchi seas during the winters of 2018 and 2019 triggered a cascade of impacts, transforming the regional marine ecosystem and adding to coastal communities’ difficulties.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C022_07 -->
2018年和2019年冬季，白令海北部与楚科奇海的海冰覆盖率降到前所未有的低水平，引发了一连串影响，改变了当地的海洋生态系统，也加重了沿海社区面临的困难。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C022_07 -->

<!-- WORD:W023 -->
# reconcile

**词源**：经古法语并直接源于拉丁语 reconciliare“使重新友好”：re- 表示“再”，conciliare 表示“使友好”。从使人和好，延伸到使要求、观点或事实彼此协调。

<!-- USE:U023_01 -->
## reconcile /ˈrɛkənsaɪl/ v. · 使看似矛盾的事物协调一致
`把不同的信念、事实或要求协调起来，使它们能够同时成立或兼顾；也可通过论证或实践说明它们并不矛盾。常用 reconcile A with B 或 reconcile A and B。不是忽略差异，更不是只选一边。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_01 USE:U023_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使看似矛盾的事物协调一致","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/105/pg105-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_01a USE:U023_01 -->
Anne says that she could never quite reconcile certain details of Mr Elliot’s earlier conduct towards her father and sister, and of the circumstances of his marriage, with his present behaviour.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_01b -->
It seemed to suggest a different sort of man.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_01 -->
安妮说，埃利奥特先生过去对她父亲和姐姐的态度，以及后来结婚的相关情形，总有些细节让她无法与他如今的行为协调起来。那似乎显示，他曾是另一种人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_01 -->
<!-- USE:U023_02 -->
## reconcile /ˈrɛkənsaɪl/ v. · 使疏远的人重归于好
