`表示使争吵、疏远的人或双方恢复友好，可说 reconcile A with B。be reconciled with somebody 表示双方重新和好；这里 with 指和好对象，不是勉强接受的处境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_02 USE:U023_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使疏远的人重归于好","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/147/pg147-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_02a -->
In 1776, Thomas Paine addressed advocates of reconciliation between Britain and the American colonies.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_02b -->
“Can you restore to us the time that is past?”
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_02c USE:U023_02 -->
“Neither can you reconcile Britain and America.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_02 -->
1776年，托马斯·潘恩向主张英国与美洲殖民地和解的人发出质问：“你们能把过去的时光还给我们吗？同样，你们也不能使英国与美洲重归于好。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_02 -->
<!-- USE:U023_03 -->
## reconcile /ˈrɛkənsaɪl/ v. · 调解争端、解决争吵
`以争吵或分歧为宾语，表示把冲突调解妥当。它与“使两人和好”有关，但着眼于被解决的争端；只让双方见面或暂时停谈，不足以说明已 reconcile the dispute。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_03 USE:U023_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"调解争端、解决争吵","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/compensation-scheme-for-group-litigation-order-case-postmasters/glo-compensation-scheme-guidance-and-principles"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_03a -->
Under the GLO Compensation Scheme, claims facilitators should consider the likely costs to both parties and to themselves.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_03b USE:U023_03 -->
Rather than take steps costing more than the financial gap between the parties, they should encourage the parties to reconcile their differences.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_03c -->
This criterion should be relaxed only if the case is likely to set a precedent for others.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_03 -->
按照GLO赔偿计划，索赔协调人员应考虑双方及其自身可能承担的费用。如果采取某种处理办法的费用会超过双方主张金额之间的差额，就应鼓励双方调解分歧。只有案件可能为其他案件开创先例时，才应放宽这一标准。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_03 -->
<!-- USE:U023_04 -->
## reconcile /ˈrɛkənsaɪl/ v. · 使自己接受不如意的现实
`常用 reconcile oneself to something/doing something，或 be reconciled to something，表示逐渐接受不愿面对的处境，并非因此喜欢它。to 是介词，后接名词或动名词；区别于 be reconciled with somebody“与某人和好”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_04 USE:U023_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使自己接受不如意的现实","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/105/pg105-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_04a USE:U023_04 -->
Anne tells Lady Russell, “Your feelings are less reconciled to Kellynch being rented out than mine.”
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_04b -->
“By remaining in the neighbourhood, I have become used to it.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_04 -->
安妮对拉塞尔夫人说：“对于凯林奇宅邸被租出去这件事，你还没有我这么能接受。我一直留在附近，已经逐渐习惯它了。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_04 -->
<!-- USE:U023_05 -->
## reconcilable /ˈrɛkənsaɪləbəl/ adj. · 能够相互协调的
`表示观点、事实、要求等可以兼容或调和，常用 reconcilable with。它说明存在协调的可能，不等于双方已经达成一致。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_07 USE:U023_05,U023_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可以调和或和解的可能性","sourceUrls":["https://api.parliament.uk/historic-hansard/commons/1980/nov/11/expenditure-which-authorities-may-make"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_07a USE:U023_07 -->
In the Commons debate of 11 November 1980, Mr Best questioned the reconcilability of local autonomy with the government’s need to control local government expenditure.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_07b USE:U023_05 -->
“It is an uneasy relationship, and I am not convinced that it is reconcilable,” he said.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_07c -->
He nevertheless welcomed the amendment as an attempt to achieve that.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_07 -->
在1980年11月11日的英国下议院辩论中，贝斯特议员质疑地方自主权能否与政府控制地方支出的需要相容。他说：“这是一种很难处理的关系，我并不确信二者能调和。”尽管如此，他仍欢迎这项修正案，认为它是在尝试实现这一点。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_07 -->
<!-- USE:U023_06 -->
## reconcilable /ˈrɛkənsaɪləbəl/ adj. · 能够和解的
`形容疏远的双方或冲突可以通过修复关系、调解而和好或解决。不要把“尚有和解可能”说成和解已经发生。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_06 USE:U023_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"能够和解的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/64185/64185-h/64185-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_06a USE:U023_06 -->
Ridgley distinguishes reconciliation from merely being reconcilable.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_06b USE:U023_06 -->
He points to scripture speaking of God having “reconciled us to himself by Jesus Christ”, not of “becoming reconcilable to us”.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_06 -->
里奇利区分了已达成的和解与仅仅可以和解。他指出，经文说的是上帝已借着耶稣基督“使我们与他自己和好”，而不是“变得可以与我们和解”。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_06 -->
<!-- USE:U023_07 -->
## reconcilability /ˌrɛkənsaɪləˈbɪləti/ n. · 可以调和或和解的可能性
`指两种要求、观点能够相容，或双方能够恢复和好的性质与可能性。通常作抽象名词；常在 reconcilability of A and B、reconcilability with B 中交代对象，须区别于已经取得的 reconciliation。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C023_07 USE:U023_07 -->
<!-- USE:U023_08 -->
## reconcilement /ˈrɛkənsaɪlmənt/ n. · 和解、调和或接受的达成
`由 reconcile 构成，指恢复友好、协调冲突事物或接受某种处境的过程或结果。具体对象可用 with、of 或 to 交代：与某人和解和对现实的接受，介词关系不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_08 USE:U023_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"和解、调和或接受的达成","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/147/pg147-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_08a USE:U023_08 -->
In his 1776 argument against reconciliation between Britain and the American colonies, Thomas Paine invokes Milton’s words: “never can true reconcilement grow where wounds of deadly hate have pierced so deep.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_08 -->
在1776年反对英国与美洲殖民地和解的论述中，托马斯·潘恩援引弥尔顿的话：“致命仇恨的创伤已刺入如此之深，真正的和解便无从生长。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_08 -->
<!-- USE:U023_09 -->
## reconciler /ˈrɛkənsaɪlər/ n. · 促成和解或协调的人
`指使双方和好、调解争端或使不同要求相容的人或事物。所协调的对象要明确；并不只限于正式职业调解员，也不是“被劝接受现实的人”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_09 USE:U023_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"促成和解或协调的人","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/20861/pg20861.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_09a -->
Think kindly once more of a separated friend, and soften your heart toward someone who has offended you.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_09b USE:U023_09 -->
This, the writer says, brings “the blessing of the Peace-maker, the blessing of the Reconciler”.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_09 -->
重新善意地看待一位疏远的朋友，对冒犯过你的人放柔心肠。作者说，这会带来“促成和平者的福分、促成和解者的福分”。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_09 -->
<!-- USE:U023_10 -->
## reconciliation /ˌrɛkənsɪliˈeɪʃən/ n. · 重归于好的过程或一次和解
`指争吵过的人、群体或国家恢复友好的过程，也可数地指一次和解。常用 reconciliation between A and B、with somebody 或 the reconciliation of the two sides；恢复交往和实际解决分歧不应无条件等同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_10 USE:U023_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"重归于好的过程或一次和解","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_10a -->
Lady Catherine sent an abusive reply to the letter announcing Darcy’s marriage arrangements, and communication between them stopped for a time.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_10b USE:U023_10 -->
Eventually, persuaded by Elizabeth, he overlooked the offence and sought a reconciliation; his aunt later visited them at Pemberley.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_10 -->
凯瑟琳夫人在回复宣布达西婚事安排的信时大肆辱骂，两人一度断绝了来往。后来，在伊丽莎白的劝说下，他不再计较冒犯，寻求和解；他的姨妈随后到彭伯里看望了他们。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_10 -->
<!-- USE:U023_11 -->
## reconciliation /ˌrɛkənsɪliˈeɪʃən/ n. · 使不同要求或事实相协调
`通常用单数，指找到办法，让看似相反的价值、事实或要求能够同时成立、相容。常说 the reconciliation of A and B 或 reconciliation with；对账时也可借此表达把记录差异核对到一致，重点是解释和协调差异。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_11 USE:U023_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使不同要求或事实相协调","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/government-functional-standard-govs-015-grants/9-annual-review-and-reconciliation-html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_11a USE:U023_11 -->
For general grants funded by the Exchequer, the financial reconciliation should confirm the amount paid and check actual expenditure against the budget and forecast.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_11b -->
It should also scrutinise invoices to check that recipients spent the money on eligible costs.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_11 -->
对于由英国国库出资的一般性拨款，财务核对应确认已支付的金额，并把实际支出同预算及预测相比较。它还应审查发票，核实收款方是否把资金用于符合条件的费用。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_11 -->
<!-- USE:U023_12 -->
## reconciliatory /ˌrɛkənˈsɪliətɔri/ adj. · 旨在促成和解的
`形容姿态、言辞或措施有助于缓和敌意、恢复友好。它说明行动的方向或作用，不保证对方接受或和解成功。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_12 USE:U023_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"旨在促成和解的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/17972/pg17972-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_12a USE:U023_12 -->
Crosse laments that the Hungarian poet Vörösmarty died in 1855, too soon to see the benefits that would come to his country from the reconciliatory policy of his dear friend Deák.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_12 -->
克罗斯惋惜地写道，匈牙利诗人沃勒什马尔蒂于1855年去世，未能等到挚友戴阿克的和解政策为祖国带来益处的那一天。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_12 -->
<!-- USE:U023_13 -->
## irreconcilable /ɪˌrɛkənˈsaɪləbəl/ adj. · 不能相容或调和的
`较正式地形容观点、要求或事实差异根本，不能同时接受或协调，常用 irreconcilable with。它不是普通“不一样”，而是无法使二者一致。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_13 USE:U023_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"不能相容或调和的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/33445/pg33445-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_13a -->
In Naval Warfare, diplomacy often avoids conflict by adjusting opposing policies through agreement and concession.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_13b USE:U023_13 -->
Sometimes it fails because the policies really are irreconcilable—or because passion, prejudice, ambition or misunderstanding makes nations regard them as such.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_13 -->
《海战》论述道，外交常通过协议和让步调整相冲突的政策，以避免冲突。有时它会失败，原因可能是政策确实无法调和，也可能是激情、偏见、野心或误解使各国把它们看作无法调和。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_13 -->
<!-- USE:U023_14 -->
## irreconcilable /ɪˌrɛkənˈsaɪləbəl/ adj. · 无法和解、势不两立的
`较正式地形容冲突严重到无法解决，或双方敌意深、不能重归于好。irreconcilable differences 常指不能调和的分歧；并非每次争吵都达到这一程度。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_14 USE:U023_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"无法和解、势不两立的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/national-space-innovation-programme-call-2/announcement-of-opportunity"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_14a -->
The 2025 announcement for Call 2 of the National Space Innovation Programme stated that due diligence would be conducted throughout the call process.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_14b USE:U023_14 -->
If it resulted in irreconcilable differences, the grant would not be placed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_14 -->
英国国家空间创新计划2025年第二轮征集公告指出，将在整个征集过程中开展尽职调查。如果调查导致出现无法解决的分歧，就不会授予拨款。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_14 -->
<!-- USE:U023_15 -->
## irreconcilable /ɪˌrɛkənˈsaɪləbəl/ n. · 拒绝妥协的反对者
`可数名词，指对某项政治措施等坚决反对、不愿妥协的人。复数 irreconcilables 可指这一派反对者；不能仅因某人持不同意见便称其为 irreconcilable。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_15 USE:U023_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"拒绝妥协的反对者","sourceUrls":["https://www.visitthecapitol.gov/artifact/senators-william-borah-henry-cabot-lodge-and-reed-smoot"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_15a USE:U023_15 -->
Some U.S. senators, known as “Irreconcilables,” opposed the Treaty of Versailles in any form.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_15b -->
The “Reservationists,” by contrast, wanted amendments before they would approve it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_15 -->
美国参议院有些议员被称为“坚决反对派”，他们反对任何形式的《凡尔赛条约》。相比之下，“保留意见派”则希望先作修订，再予批准。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_15 -->
<!-- USE:U023_16 -->
## irreconcilable /ɪˌrɛkənˈsaɪləbəl/ n. · 不能调和的事物或观点
`通常用复数 irreconcilables，指彼此无法一致、不能相容的项目、思想等。它指冲突的事物本身，区别于指顽固反对者的可数人称义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_16 USE:U023_16,U023_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"不能调和的事物或观点","sourceUrls":["https://api.parliament.uk/historic-hansard/lords/1969/nov/25/the-case-of-ex-pc-luckhurst"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_16a USE:U023_17 -->
In the 1969 Lords debate, the Earl of Cork and Orrery pressed the government about the apparent irreconcilability of one officer’s guilt and another’s innocence.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_16b USE:U023_16 -->
How, he asked, had the inquiry reconciled “the two apparent irreconcilables”?
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_16c -->
If new evidence had resolved the contradiction, he argued, it ought to be disclosed to the convicted man’s legal advisers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_16 -->
在1969年的英国上议院辩论中，科克和奥勒里伯爵追问，一名警员有罪而另一名无罪这两项判断之间表面上的不可相容之处。他问，调查究竟怎样调和了“这两项表面上无法相容的判断”？他认为，如果新的证据已经消除了矛盾，就应当将它披露给被定罪者的法律顾问。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_16 -->
<!-- USE:U023_17 -->
## irreconcilability /ɪˌrɛkənsaɪləˈbɪləti/ n.; irreconcilableness /ɪˌrɛkənˈsaɪləbəlnəs/ n. · 无法相容或和解的性质
`指思想、要求等不相容，或敌意、分歧无法消解的性质。需由 of 或 with 等结构说明哪两者冲突；两种名词形式不另造程度区别。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C023_16 USE:U023_17 -->
<!-- USE:U023_18 -->
## irreconcilably /ɪˌrɛkənˈsaɪləbli/ adv. · 无法调和地
`修饰分裂、对立或差异，表示其程度使双方不能达成一致或恢复和好，如 irreconcilably opposed。语义强于仅仅意见不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_18 USE:U023_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"无法调和地","sourceUrls":["https://api.parliament.uk/historic-hansard/commons/1996/apr/24/period-for-reflection-and-consideration"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_18a USE:U023_18 -->
In the 24 April 1996 Commons debate, Ms Armstrong argued that, if a marriage had irreconcilably broken down, legislation must make children’s needs paramount.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_18b -->
She called for effective mediation rather than simply extending the period under discussion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_18 -->
在1996年4月24日的英国下议院辩论中，阿姆斯特朗议员主张：如果婚姻已经破裂到无法挽回，立法就必须把儿童的需要放在首位。她呼吁提供有效调解，而不是单纯延长当时正在讨论的期限。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_18 -->
<!-- USE:U023_19 -->
## unreconciled /ˌʌnˈrɛkənsaɪld/ adj. · 尚未和好或解决的
`表示双方仍未恢复友好，或争端仍未解决。un- 在这里表示结果尚未实现；它不一定像 irreconcilable 那样断言今后也无法和解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_19 USE:U023_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"尚未和好或解决的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/35857/pg35857-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_19a -->
Mary tells of two sisters who quarrelled when young and then lived for forty years in the same room, never speaking.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_19b -->
“And didn’t they ever make up?” Nancy asks.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_19c USE:U023_19 -->
“No, they died unreconciled, the book said.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_19 -->
玛丽讲起一则她读来的故事：两姐妹年轻时争吵，此后同室住了四十年，却从不交谈。南茜问：“她们后来就没和好吗？”“没有，书上说，她们直到死都没有和解。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_19 -->
<!-- USE:U023_20 -->
## unreconciled /ˌʌnˈrɛkənsaɪld/ adj. · 尚未协调一致的
`指数据、账目、观点或要求仍有未解释、未调和的差异。它说明状态尚未处理到一致，不一定意味着本质上绝不相容。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_20 USE:U023_20 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"尚未协调一致的","sourceUrls":["https://publications.parliament.uk/pa/cm200506/cmselect/cmpubacc/1079/6042615.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_20a USE:U023_20 -->
The Home Office told the Public Accounts Committee that, by June 2005, a technical solution had explained £67 million of the bank-reconciliation difference, but an unreconciled difference of £3.035 million remained.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_20b -->
Reports could not be run retrospectively, hampering investigation.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_20c -->
The memorandum later reported that the bank account had been reconciled to within £3,500 by 14 December.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_20 -->
英国内政部向公共账目委员会说明，截至2005年6月，一项技术解决方案已解释了银行对账中6700万英镑的差额，但仍有303.5万英镑的差额尚未对平。报表无法追溯生成，妨碍了调查。这份备忘录随后报告，到12月14日，银行账户已经核对到只剩3500英镑差额。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_20 -->
<!-- USE:U023_21 -->
## unreconciled /ˌʌnˈrɛkənsaɪld/ adj. · 仍不甘心接受的
`常用 unreconciled to something，表示仍未接受某个令人不快的事实或结局。与 unreconciled with somebody“未与某人和好”分清；to 后接处境而非和好伙伴。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C023_21 USE:U023_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"仍不甘心接受的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/2834/pg2834-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_21a -->
Isabel has no doubt that Caspar Goodwood remains unhappy about not having married her.
<!-- SENTENCE:S023_21b USE:U023_21 -->
She finds the aggressive, uncompromising quality of his unreconciled state unattractive.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C023_21 -->
伊莎贝尔毫不怀疑，卡斯帕·古德伍德仍为没能娶到她而不快。他那种仍不肯接受现实的状态带着咄咄逼人、毫不妥协的意味，让她感到反感。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C023_21 -->

<!-- WORD:W024 -->
# resilient

**词源**：与拉丁语 resilire“弹回、跳回”有关，其中 re- 表示“回”，salire 表示“跳”。可以用受压后弹回的形象，联想到人或系统受到打击后恢复的能力。

<!-- USE:U024_01 -->
## resilient /rɪˈzɪliənt/ adj. · 受压后能回弹复原的
`形容材料经弯曲、拉伸、挤压或撞击后能够恢复原状、不易损坏，常作表语，也可放名词前。不是只说材料硬；能变形后复原才是关键。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C024_01 USE:U024_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"受压后能回弹复原的","sourceUrls":["https://spinoff.nasa.gov/Spinoff2017/cg_7.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_01a USE:U024_01 -->
NASA’s 2017 Spinoff account contrasts brittle, strong glass with resilient rubber that is not stiff.
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_01b -->
According to Zyvex’s president Lance Criscuolo, carbon-nanotube-infused resin makes carbon-fibre composites tougher without sacrificing stiffness, often increasing it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C024_01 -->
NASA于2017年发表的《技术衍生》报道把坚固却易碎的玻璃与富有弹性、却不刚硬的橡胶作比较。Zyvex公司总裁兰斯·克里斯库洛介绍说，掺入碳纳米管的树脂能提高碳纤维复合材料的韧性，同时不牺牲刚度，甚至常能提高刚度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C024_01 -->
<!-- USE:U024_02 -->
## resilient /rɪˈzɪliənt/ adj. · 受挫后能恢复的
`形容人、群体、经济或其他系统受到冲击后能较容易、较快地恢复、继续运作；常作表语，也可放在名词前。它不表示从未受损或没有悲伤，而强调恢复能力；可说 resilient to shocks，语境须交代哪类冲击。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C024_02 USE:U024_02,U024_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"受挫后能恢复的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/the-uk-government-resilience-framework/the-uk-government-resilience-framework-html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_02a USE:U024_02 -->
One pillar of the UK’s 2021 National Cyber Strategy focuses on “building a resilient and prosperous digital UK”.
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_02b USE:U024_04 -->
It aims to improve understanding of cyber risk, prevent and resist attacks more effectively, and strengthen national and organisational resilience to prepare for, respond to and recover from cyber attacks.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C024_02 -->
英国2021年《国家网络战略》的一项支柱着眼于“建设一个韧性强、繁荣的数字英国”。它旨在增进对网络风险的认识，更有效地预防和抵御攻击，并增强国家与组织的韧性，以做好应对网络攻击的准备、作出响应并从中恢复。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C024_02 -->
<!-- USE:U024_03 -->
## resilience /rɪˈzɪliəns/ n.; resiliency /rɪˈzɪliənsi/ n. · 材料回弹复原的能力
`指材料、肌肉等受压、受拉或变形后恢复的弹性与能力，通常不可数。resilience 与 resiliency 都可表达这种性质，不能单凭词尾把一个限定为材料、另一个限定为心理。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C024_03 USE:U024_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"材料回弹复原的能力","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/12299/pg12299-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_03a USE:U024_03 -->
Hickory is associated with great strength, toughness, and resilience, but some pieces are comparatively weak and unsuitable for the demanding uses for which good hickory is valued.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C024_03 -->
山核桃木以强度高、韧性好、弹性恢复力强著称，但有些木料相对较弱，并不适合优质山核桃木所胜任的那些要求严苛的用途。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C024_03 -->
<!-- USE:U024_04 -->
## resilience /rɪˈzɪliəns/ n.; resiliency /rɪˈzɪliənsi/ n. · 受冲击后的恢复力
`通常不可数，指人或系统经历打击、困难后恢复的能力，常说 the resilience of somebody/something 或 resilience to something。它不是意志坚强就不会受伤的保证，也不说明所有人恢复速度相同。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C024_02 USE:U024_04 -->
<!-- USE:U024_05 -->
## resiliently /rɪˈzɪliəntli/ adv. · 有弹性地回弹
`表示材料以能恢复形状的方式反应、弯曲或回弹。修饰动作时着眼于变形与复原的关系，不把 resiliently 简化为“坚硬地”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C024_05 USE:U024_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"有弹性地回弹","sourceUrls":["https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20080004838/downloads/20080004838.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_05a USE:U024_05 -->
The deployment-hinge patent describes a hinge driven from a closed to an open position and resiliently biased toward the open position.
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_05b -->
If the deployable part moves past that position, the cam compresses the spring, forcing the part back toward it; friction dissipates energy during this movement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C024_05 -->
这项展开铰链专利描述了一种从闭合位置驱动到展开位置、并由弹性回复力趋向展开位置的铰链。如果可展开部件越过该位置，凸轮就会压缩弹簧，迫使部件向展开位置返回；在这个运动过程中，摩擦会耗散能量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C024_05 -->
<!-- USE:U024_06 -->
## resiliently /rɪˈzɪliəntli/ adv. · 以能承受打击并恢复的方式
`修饰应对、恢复或继续行动，表示经历困难后仍能调整、恢复。应交代实际行动或反应，不能仅凭这一副词保证某种方法一定有效。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C024_06 USE:U024_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以能承受打击并恢复的方式","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/speeches/levelling-up-secretarys-speech-to-the-convention-of-the-north"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_06a -->
In his January 2023 speech to the Convention of the North, Michael Gove, then the Levelling Up Secretary, said recent economic challenges made the previous year’s White Paper more important as a guide to action.
<!-- SENTENCE:S024_06b USE:U024_06 -->
It laid out steps to improve the country’s economic performance “durably, resiliently and equitably”, he argued.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C024_06 -->
2023年1月，当时的英国地方均衡发展事务大臣迈克尔·戈夫在北方大会上表示，近期经济挑战使前一年发表的白皮书作为行动指南显得更加重要。他主张，白皮书列出了改善国家经济表现的步骤，要使这种改善“持久、经得住冲击并能恢复，而且公平”。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C024_06 -->

<!-- WORD:W025 -->
# scope

**词源**：表示范围、余地的 scope 经意大利语 scopo“目标、目的”，来自希腊语 skopos“靶子、目标”，与“观察、瞄准”相关。仪器简称 scope 则来自以 -scope 结尾的名称；两种来源关系不等于所有意义可以互换。

<!-- USE:U025_01 -->
## scope /skoʊp/ n. · 可发挥或行动的余地
`不可数，指做某事、发挥能力的机会或自由。常用 scope for something、scope to do something；give somebody scope 是给某人施展空间。它不一定指实际物理空间。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C025_01 USE:U025_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"可发挥或行动的余地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_01a -->
Archer asks whether Othello and Lear are colossally simple rather than complex as character studies.
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_01b USE:U025_01 -->
They are extremely vivid, he argues, and the violence of their actions affords scope for the most vehement self-expression.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C025_01 -->
阿彻提出一个问题：如果把奥赛罗与李尔看作人物研究，他们是否与其说复杂，不如说极为单纯？他认为，这些人物极其鲜明，而他们激烈的行动给最强烈的自我表达提供了施展余地。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C025_01 -->
<!-- USE:U025_02 -->
## scope /skoʊp/ n. · 工作或研究所涵盖的范围
`通常用单数，指活动、议题或作品覆盖的整个领域，常说 the scope of something、within/beyond the scope of。扩大 scope 是扩大所涉及的内容；它与具体任务最终是否完成不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C025_02 USE:U025_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"工作或研究所涵盖的范围","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42773/42773-h/42773-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_02a USE:U025_02 -->
Literature might be taken to include everything written or printed, but I wish to confine my examination to works that can properly be brought within the scope of literature as one of the fine arts.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C025_02 -->
文学本可以被理解为所有写下或印出的文字，但我希望把这次考察限定在那些确实属于作为美的艺术之一的文学范围内的作品。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C025_02 -->
<!-- USE:U025_03 -->
## scope /skoʊp/ n. · 思想、观察或眼界的广度
`指智力活动、观察、见识能涉及的宽广程度，如 limited in scope。着眼于能看见、想到或理解多广，不单是某份文件列出的工作边界。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C025_03 USE:U025_03 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"思想、观察或眼界的广度","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"],"speakers":{"S025_03a":"Mr Collins to Elizabeth"}} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_03a USE:U025_03 -->
“My dear Miss Elizabeth, I have the highest opinion in the world of your excellent judgment in all matters within the scope of your understanding, but permit me to say that there must be a wide difference between the established forms of ceremony amongst the laity and those which regulate the clergy.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C025_03 -->
“亲爱的伊丽莎白小姐，对于您理解力所及的一切事情，我都极其敬重您的出色判断；不过，请容我指出，普通信徒之间既定的礼仪形式，与约束神职人员的礼仪，必然大不相同。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C025_03 -->
<!-- USE:U025_04 -->
## scope /skoʊp/ n. · 抛锚时放出的锚缆长度
`航海用语，指船舶锚泊时已放出的锚缆长度；在锚泊操作中也会通过缆长与水深的关系讨论放缆量。读技术指示时必须辨清具体量和单位，不能把它当作一般“范围”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C025_04 USE:U025_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"抛锚时放出的锚缆长度","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/8647/8647-h/8647-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_04a -->
“Anchor, sir—bear a hand and anchor, as soon as possible,” Wallingford calls after piloting the ship through the reef.
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_04b -->
He hears an anchor plunge into the water and waits to see the result.
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_04c USE:U025_04 -->
The ship takes her scope of cable freely, and then he sees that she has been brought to a stop.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C025_04 -->
引着船穿过礁间水道后，沃林福德喊道：“先生，抛锚——快搭把手，尽快抛锚！”他听见一只锚重重落水，便停着观察结果。船顺畅地把锚缆拉放到所需长度，随后他看见船停住了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C025_04 -->
<!-- USE:U025_05 -->
## scope /skoʊp/ n. · 目的、意图
`古义，指所追求的目的、意图或终点。阅读旧文时可结合“某人意在达到什么”理解，不把它机械译为现代常见的“研究范围”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C025_05 USE:U025_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"目的、意图","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/64185/64185-h/64185-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_05a USE:U025_05 -->
Discussing prohibited worship, Ridgley says that the commandment’s general scope and design, in its prohibitions, is to forbid false worship in the heart, actions or gestures.
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_05b -->
In his account, people should follow God’s revealed will rather than their own imaginations.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C025_05 -->
在讨论被禁止的敬拜方式时，里奇利说，这条诫命就其禁令而言，总的目的与意图是禁止内心、行为或姿态中的虚假敬拜。按他的阐释，人们应遵循上帝启示的旨意，而不是自己的想象。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C025_05 -->
<!-- USE:U025_06 -->
## scope /skoʊp/ n. · 望远镜等仪器的口语简称
`口语中可简称 telescope、microscope 或其他以 -scope 结尾的观察仪器。具体是哪一种须看情境，不能见到 scope 就一律译成“望远镜”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C025_06 USE:U025_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"望远镜等仪器的口语简称","sourceUrls":["https://science.nasa.gov/skywatching/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_06a USE:U025_06 -->
NASA’s skywatching guide notes that telescopes range from automated desktop models controlled by an app to large, open-frame scopes you can build yourself.
<!-- SENTENCE:S025_06b -->
They may be pointed manually or driven by motorised mounts with computerised tracking.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C025_06 -->
NASA的观星指南指出，望远镜有多种形式，从通过应用程序控制的自动化桌面型号，到可以自行搭建的大型开放框架式望远镜都有。它们可以手动指向目标，也可以由带有计算机跟踪功能的电动支架驱动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C025_06 -->

<!-- WORD:W026 -->
# sustain

**词源**：经古法语 sustenir，来自拉丁语 sustinere“托住、支撑、承受”，与 tenere“握住、保持”有关。把“撑住”作为联系，可以理解维持活动、支持生命，也可以理解承受伤害或压力；这些现代用法仍须分别辨认。

<!-- USE:U026_01 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 使活动或声音持续
`及物，表示使行动、努力、增长、声音等在一段时间内继续或保持，不中断。可说 sustain growth、sustain an effort 或 sustain a note；强调保持的过程，不只是在某个瞬间启动。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_01 USE:U026_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使活动或声音持续","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_01a USE:U026_01 -->
The happy playwright, Archer suggests, finds a theme that lets him sustain and increase the tension to the very close without needing a bloody or a tame final act.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_01b -->
Such themes occur, but they are not common; he expects readers to find it surprisingly hard to recall many examples.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_01 -->
阿彻认为，幸运的剧作家能找到这样的题材：既不必以流血收场，也不必让最后一幕平淡无味，却能一直维持并加强紧张感直到落幕。这类题材确实存在，却不常见；他预料，读者会发现，要想起许多例子竟出乎意料地困难。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_01 -->
<!-- USE:U026_02 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 遭受损失、伤害或失败
`较正式，及物，表示遭受 damage、injury、loss 或 defeat。sustain injuries 是受到伤害，不是“维持伤口”；过去分词可后置，如 injuries sustained in an accident。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_02 USE:U026_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"遭受损失、伤害或失败","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/news/operation-toral-draws-to-an-end-as-uk-transitions-to-new-phase-of-support-to-afghanistan"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_02a USE:U026_02 -->
In its 8 July 2021 statement, the Ministry of Defence remembered 457 members of the UK Armed Forces who had died in Afghanistan since 2001, with others sustaining life-changing injuries.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_02b -->
It said the country owed them a huge debt of gratitude and their sacrifice must never be forgotten.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_02 -->
在2021年7月8日的声明中，英国国防部缅怀了自2001年以来在阿富汗丧生的457名英国军人，并指出另有人员遭受了改变一生的伤害。声明说，国家深深感激他们，绝不能忘记他们的牺牲。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_02 -->
<!-- USE:U026_03 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 给予力量、鼓励或生活支持
`较正式，表示某人或某物给人帮助、力量、勇气或生活所需，使其撑过困难。宾语是被支持的人；精神信念、他人的帮助或资金都可作主语，不限于身体支撑。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_03 USE:U026_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"给予力量、鼓励或生活支持","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42759/42759-h/42759-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_03a USE:U026_03 -->
Acknowledging that his study of distributive justice falls short of adequate achievement, John A. Ryan says he is sustained by the hope that it will provoke discussion and prompt a more competent writer to treat the subject more thoroughly and fruitfully.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_03 -->
约翰·A·赖恩承认，自己关于分配正义的研究远未达到应有的成就；但他仍因一个希望而得到支撑：它能引发讨论，促使更有能力的作者把这一领域探究得更深入、更有成果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_03 -->
<!-- USE:U026_04 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 支撑并承受重量
`及物，指结构、材料等托住某物，尤其持续承受重量。sustain the weight of something 着眼于承重，不等同于形状受压后的回弹能力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_04 USE:U026_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"支撑并承受重量","sourceUrls":["https://whalesite.org/anthology/1891_Luce_Text-Book_of_Seamanship.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_04a USE:U026_04 -->
In a test of a tackle with two upper sheaves and one lower sheave, less force was required to sustain the weight in place than to raise it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_04 -->
在一套上方有两个滑轮、下方有一个滑轮的滑车组实验中，保持重物悬置所需的力，小于把它提升所需的力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_04 -->
<!-- USE:U026_05 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 以营养维持生命
`食物等作主语，指给人或生物营养，使其存活。区别于精神鼓励或资金支持；sustain somebody on something 表示靠某种食物维持。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_05 USE:U026_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以营养维持生命","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/40285/pg40285-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_05a -->
In The Syrian Christ (1916), Abraham Mitrie Rihbany recalls being brought up to regard bread as sacred: he would pick up a fallen piece rather than step on it.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_05b USE:U026_05 -->
Because bread sustained life, he saw it as God's own life made tangible for people to feed upon.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_05 -->
在1916年的《叙利亚的基督》中，亚伯拉罕·米特里·里赫巴尼回忆，自己从小就被教导要敬重面包，把它视为神圣之物：遇到掉在路上的面包，他会把它拾起来，而不会踩上去。因为面包维持着人的生命，他便把它看作上帝自身的生命化为可供人食用的具体之物。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_05 -->
<!-- USE:U026_06 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 经受住、忍受
`及物，表示承受压力、困难等而支撑住，与单纯“遭受伤害”的用法区别：这里着眼于受力或承压后能够经受，而非只报告伤害发生。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_06 USE:U026_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"经受住、忍受","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_06a -->
Elizabeth expected Darcy to leave when he learned that the Gardiners were her relatives.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_06b USE:U026_06 -->
He was surprised, but he sustained it with fortitude and stayed to talk with Mr Gardiner.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_06 -->
伊丽莎白以为，达西一知道加德纳夫妇是她的亲戚，就会离开。他确实感到意外，却镇定地承受了这种意外，留下来与加德纳先生交谈。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_06 -->
<!-- USE:U026_07 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 认可异议、裁定支持
`法律用语，法庭等对异议作出支持裁决，如 sustain an objection。它表示异议成立或获得支持，不能译成“继续提出异议”；反义裁决常用 overrule。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_07 USE:U026_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"认可异议、裁定支持","sourceUrls":["https://www.uscourts.gov/about-federal-courts/types-cases/civil-cases"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_07a -->
In a U.S. federal civil trial, the opposing attorney may object to an improper question.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_07b USE:U026_07 -->
If the judge sustains the objection, the witness does not answer it, and the attorney must move on to the next question.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_07 -->
在美国联邦民事审判中，对方律师可以对不当问题提出异议。如果法官支持这项异议，证人就不回答该问题，律师必须转问下一个问题。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_07 -->
<!-- USE:U026_08 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 以证据证实主张
`以事实或证据使陈述、指控成立，近于 substantiate/corroborate。主语可为 evidence；不能因为有人重复一项主张，就说其已被证实。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_08 USE:U026_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以证据证实主张","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/24931/pg24931-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_08a -->
In Elements of Agricultural Chemistry (1860), Thomas Anderson rejects the older view that plants' mineral constituents were merely accidental.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_08b USE:U026_08 -->
He says that the supposition could only be sustained when analysis was imperfect; repeated experiments had since shown that inorganic substances were necessary to plants.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_08 -->
在1860年的《农业化学原理》中，托马斯·安德森驳斥了一种旧观点，即植物中的矿物成分只是偶然混入的。他说，这一假说只有在分析技术不完善时才可能得到支持；此后的反复实验已经表明，无机物质对植物不可或缺。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_08 -->
<!-- USE:U026_09 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v. · 持续胜任并演绎角色
`指持续、恰当地扮演或表现一个角色、人物形象。着眼于演绎贯穿一段表现过程，不只是被分配到角色，也不同于“支持某位演员”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_09 USE:U026_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"持续胜任并演绎角色","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/700/pg700-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_09a USE:U026_09 -->
In The Old Curiosity Shop, a little terrier had once sustained the character of Toby, Punch’s dog.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_09b -->
When it sees Short again, it recognises him and barks furiously at the box containing the pasteboard nose, until its owner puts it back into his pocket.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_09 -->
在《老古玩店》中，一只小猎犬曾扮演过庞奇木偶戏里的狗托比。它再次看见肖特时，认出了他，又冲着装有纸板鼻子的箱子狂吠，直到主人把它塞回口袋里。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_09 -->
<!-- USE:U026_10 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ n. · 乐音延续的效果
`音乐用语，指一个音发出后延续的效果或结果，尤其可由电子设备实现。作名词时可谈乐器或音效的 sustain，不把它混同于动词“支撑”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_10 USE:U026_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"乐音延续的效果","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US4245540A/en"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_10a USE:U026_10 -->
Barry A. Groupp's US patent 4,245,540 explains that driving a guitar's steel strings with low audio levels can slow their decay and increase the sustain of the notes.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_10b -->
Adjusting the device's sustain control changes how long a note lasts; excessive drive can make its volume increase rather than fade.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_10 -->
巴里·A.格鲁普的美国第4245540号专利解释说，以较低的音频电平驱动吉他的钢弦，可以减缓振动衰减，延长乐音的延续效果。调节该装置的延音控制器，可以改变一个音持续的时间；驱动过强，则可能使音量不断增大，而不是逐渐消退。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_10 -->
<!-- USE:U026_11 -->
## sustained /səˈsteɪnd/ adj. · 持续而不中断的
`指努力、注意、增长或声音等在一段时间内保持下去，如 sustained effort。它也作 sustain 的过去式和过去分词；injuries sustained 的 sustained 属于“遭受”的动词结构，不是这个“持续的”形容词义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_11 USE:U026_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"持续而不中断的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_11a USE:U026_11 -->
Archer argues that the Western theatre’s two- or three-hour performance limit reflects the physical endurance and power of sustained attention that can be demanded of an audience.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_11b -->
A playwright who accepts that limit has already accepted a condition imposed by the audience, so cannot claim that mere self-expression is the only aim.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_11 -->
阿彻论证说，西方剧场将演出限制在两三个小时，反映的是观众的体力耐受程度以及能被要求保持的持续注意力。剧作家一旦接受这一时间限制，就已经接受了观众所施加的一项条件，因此不能再声称单纯的自我表达是唯一目的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_11 -->
<!-- USE:U026_12 -->
## sustainedly /səˈsteɪnɪdli/ adv. · 持续不断地
`表示某种动作或表现保持一段时间。词形来自 sustained；修饰行动的延续方式，不把它理解成一次支持或赞成。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_12 USE:U026_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"持续不断地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/38776/pg38776-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_12a USE:U026_12 -->
In closing his letter to Howells of 28 January 1898, Henry James says this “scrawl—or sprawl” is about all his poor hand can now sustainedly perpetrate.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_12 -->
在1898年1月28日写给豪威尔斯的信末，亨利·詹姆斯说，这通“潦草乱写——或者说横涂竖抹”，差不多已是他那只可怜的手眼下能坚持写出的全部了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_12 -->
<!-- USE:U026_13 -->
## sustainer /səˈsteɪnər/ n. · 维持、支撑某人或某物者
`指给予力量、生活支持，或使某种活动、声音等维持的人或事物。具体支撑什么由上下文交代；不能仅凭 -er 把它限定为某一种职业或设备。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_13 USE:U026_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"维持、支撑某人或某物者","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/30373/30373-h/30373-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_13a USE:U026_13 -->
In a religious dialogue collected in Life and Literature, the angels address God as “our Sustainer” and ask whether anything in creation is stronger than wind.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_13b -->
The answer is a person giving alms in secret—the passage’s climax after comparisons of mountains, iron, fire and water.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_13 -->
在《生活与文学》收录的一则宗教对话中，天使称上帝为“我们的维系者”，并问受造万物中是否有什么比风更强大。回答是暗中施舍的人——这段文字先比较了山、铁、火与水，最后以此作结。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_13 -->
<!-- USE:U026_14 -->
## sustainment /səˈsteɪnmənt/ n. · 维持、支撑的过程
`指保持某种活动、能力或生存状态所需的持续支撑。通常作抽象名词；在补给等专业语境中可指为持续行动提供的支持，但具体范围依相应文件而定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_14 USE:U026_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"维持、支撑的过程","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/tenancy-sustainment-and-complex-needs-a-rapid-evidence-assessment/tenancy-sustainment-and-complex-needs-a-rapid-evidence-assessment"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_14a USE:U026_14 -->
A 2025 evidence review commissioned by the UK housing ministry found relatively robust evidence that rapid rehousing with intensive support produces positive tenancy sustainment outcomes for homeless people with complex needs, at least in the short to medium term.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_14b -->
However, it could not draw definitive conclusions about why the model works so well or for whom.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_14 -->
一份由英国住房主管部门委托、于2025年发表并适用于英格兰的证据综述发现，相对扎实的证据表明，为有复杂需求的无家可归者迅速安置住房并提供密集支持，至少在短期至中期内能在维持租住关系方面取得较好的结果。不过，综述无法就这一模式为何如此有效、对哪些人有效得出确定结论。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_14 -->
<!-- USE:U026_15 -->
## self-sustaining /ˌsɛlfsəˈsteɪnɪŋ/ adj.; self-sustained /ˌsɛlfsəˈsteɪnd/ adj. · 能自行维持的
`指人、过程或系统靠自身条件持续运作，不必不断有外部投入或干预。两种形式都强调自我维持；不等于没有初始投入，也不保证永远不会失效。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_15 USE:U026_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"能自行维持的","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/sp-4533.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_15a USE:U026_15 -->
NASA's history of the Plum Brook reactor recounts how Enrico Fermi's team achieved the first self-sustaining chain reaction on 2 December 1942, after gradually withdrawing control rods.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_15b -->
It began at 3:25 p.m.; twenty-eight minutes later, Fermi ordered a rod inserted and the reaction was shut down.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_15 -->
美国国家航空航天局关于普拉姆布鲁克反应堆的历史著作回顾了恩里科·费米的团队如何在逐步抽出控制棒后，于1942年12月2日实现首次自持链式反应。反应于下午3点25分开始；28分钟后，费米下令插入一根控制棒，反应随即停止。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_15 -->
<!-- USE:U026_16 -->
## sustainable /səˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj. · 不耗尽资源的可持续的
`生态与资源利用语境中，指使用方式避免耗尽自然资源、破坏生态平衡，能够持续。不能仅因某产品可以反复使用，便无条件称其来源 sustainable。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_16 USE:U026_16 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"不耗尽资源的可持续的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/sustainable-forestry-cycle"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_16a USE:U026_16 -->
Learn about the benefits of sustainable forestry and how timber is a renewable resource for a sustainable future.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_16 -->
了解可持续林业的益处，以及木材如何作为可再生资源服务于可持续的未来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_16 -->
<!-- USE:U026_17 -->
## sustainable /səˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj. · 能够长期维持的
`指计划、方法、系统或活动的水平能够持续下去，不因自身条件而难以继续。也可表示某种负担承受得住；须说清所讨论的时间、资源或承受条件。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_19 USE:U026_17,U026_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"计划或水平能否持续的性质","sourceUrls":["https://www.cps.gov.uk/publication/cps-briefing-principles"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_19a USE:U026_19 -->
The Crown Prosecution Service’s Briefing Principles say that a mix of in-house and external advocacy provides flexibility, resilience and sustainability, giving it a choice of the best advocate for the role.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_19b USE:U026_17 -->
The document describes in-house advocates as a long-term, sustainable cadre committed to high-quality prosecution advocacy and public service.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_19 -->
英国皇家检察署的《出庭律师委任原则》认为，内部和外聘出庭律师相结合，可带来灵活性、韧性和可持续性，使检察署能够为岗位选择最适合的律师。文件将内部律师队伍描述为一支能够长期维系、致力于优质公诉与公共服务的队伍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_19 -->
<!-- USE:U026_18 -->
## sustainability /səˌsteɪnəˈbɪləti/ n. · 资源利用的可持续性
`通常不可数，生态语境中指在保护资源、生态平衡前提下继续利用或发展的能力与性质。environmental sustainability 不只是短期产量或利润。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_18 USE:U026_18,U026_23 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"资源利用的可持续性","sourceUrls":["https://www.ons.gov.uk/economy/environmentalaccounts/bulletins/marineaccountsnaturalcapitaluk/2021"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_18a -->
ONS notes that its measure can assess whether fishing for an individual stock is sustainable, but does not account for knock-on effects across the ecosystem.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_18b USE:U026_18,U026_23 -->
If a fish species that feeds other species is managed unsustainably, it may affect the sustainability of stocks higher up the food chain.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_18 -->
英国国家统计局指出，它采用的指标能评估某一种群的捕捞是否可持续，却没有计入对整个生态系统的连带影响。如果一种作为其他鱼类食物的鱼被以不可持续的方式管理，就可能影响食物链更高层其他鱼类种群的可持续性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_18 -->
<!-- USE:U026_19 -->
## sustainability /səˌsteɪnəˈbɪləti/ n. · 计划或水平能否持续的性质
`通常不可数，表示某种扩张、系统或活动能够保持下去的程度，常用 the sustainability of something。它问的是维持条件，不等于活动已实际长期持续。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C026_19 USE:U026_19 -->
<!-- USE:U026_20 -->
## sustainably /səˈsteɪnəbli/ adv. · 以保护资源的可持续方式
`用于生态与资源利用，表示以避免资源耗竭、维持生态平衡的方式开展活动。修饰生产、采购、管理等动作；它不是“源源不断地”的简单同义替换。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_20 USE:U026_20 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以保护资源的可持续方式","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/sustainable-forestry-cycle"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_20a USE:U026_20 -->
Find out how sustainably managed woodlands can deliver benefits to the environment, people and the economy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_20 -->
了解以可持续方式管理的林地如何为环境、民众与经济带来益处。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_20 -->
<!-- USE:U026_21 -->
## unsustainable /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj. · 无法继续维持或承受的
`指活动水平、负担或做法无法继续撑住，原因可包括资源消耗过快、经济条件不足或承受能力有限。讨论生态时可指不能长期维持的资源使用；需保留具体原因与条件。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_21 USE:U026_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"无法继续维持或承受的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/a-sustainable-future-reforming-club-football-governance/a-sustainable-future-reforming-club-football-governance"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_21a USE:U026_21 -->
The UK government's 2023 football-governance white paper argues that many clubs in the English football pyramid are operating in financially unsustainable ways.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_21b -->
They rely heavily on owners to fund continuing losses, leaving them at greater risk of insolvency if their owners' personal circumstances change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_21 -->
英国政府2023年的足球治理白皮书指出，英格兰足球联赛体系中的许多俱乐部正以财务上难以维持的方式运作。它们严重依赖所有者投入资金来填补持续亏损；如果所有者的个人境况发生变化，俱乐部便面临更大的无力偿债风险。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_21 -->
<!-- USE:U026_22 -->
## unsustainable /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj. · 无法用证据或论证支持的
`形容主张、立场或指控站不住脚，不能进一步支持、证实。这里不是“对环境不友好”，也不只是说话者不喜欢这一观点。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_22 USE:U026_22 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"无法用证据或论证支持的","sourceUrls":["https://tribunalsdecisions.service.gov.uk/utiac/hu-11609-2019"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_22a USE:U026_22 -->
The Upper Tribunal rejected as unsustainable an argument that the judge had failed to analyse or refer to an earlier case: the judge had in fact considered it and directed himself appropriately.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_22b -->
The tribunal noted that the reasons were brief and another judge might have reached a different conclusion, but all relevant authorities had been considered.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_22 -->
英国上级审裁处认为，关于原审法官未分析或援引一项先例的论点站不住脚：事实上，该法官已考虑这一先例，并正确把握了法律适用。审裁处指出，尽管理由表述简短，另一位法官也可能得出不同结论，但相关判例依据都已得到考虑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_22 -->
<!-- USE:U026_23 -->
## unsustainably /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnəbli/ adv. · 以无法维持的方式或程度
`修饰利用资源、增长、消费或某种水平，表示其速度、方式或负担不能继续保持。根据语境辨清是生态耗竭还是其他维持条件不足，不一律译为“不可持续发展地”。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C026_18 USE:U026_23 -->
<!-- USE:U026_24 -->
## unsustained /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnd/ adj. · 未持续或未保持的
`形容努力、增长、声音等没有保持下去，持续时间不足或发生中断。它描述已观察到的延续状态，区别于 unsustainable 对能否维持的判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_24 USE:U026_24 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未持续或未保持的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/28630/pg28630-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_24a USE:U026_24 -->
An 1867 Atlantic Monthly review praises Thomas William Parsons's translation of Dante for its striking successes, but calls it unequal and unsustained even in some of its best parts.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_24b -->
The reviewer objects that the translator can spoil the effect of a father's frantic appeal about his son by giving him melodramatic words.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_24 -->
《大西洋月刊》1867年的一篇书评赞赏托马斯·威廉·帕森斯翻译但丁时取得的出色效果，却又批评译作水准参差，即使在某些最好的部分也未能保持。书评作者认为，译者让一位急切打听儿子是否还活着的父亲说出过于戏剧化的话，反而破坏了这番恳切询问的整体效果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_24 -->
<!-- USE:U026_25 -->
## unsustained /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnd/ adj. · 未得到支持或证实的
`指人、事物或主张未获得所需的支持、支撑或证据。未被支持不必然等于无法被支持；后者可用 unsustainable 表达。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C026_25 USE:U026_25 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"未得到支持或证实的","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/45954/old/45954-h/45954-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_25a USE:U026_25 -->
Arguing against a bill to organise two new territories, Charles Sumner condemns an effort to open the region to slavery on “pretences unsustained by facts”.
<!-- SENTENCE:S026_25b -->
He points to the Act of Congress of 6 March 1820 as an existing prohibition of slavery there.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C026_25 -->
在反对一项设立两个新领地的法案时，查尔斯·萨姆纳谴责有人企图以“没有事实支持的借口”，把这片地区向奴隶制开放。他指出，1820年3月6日的国会法案已在这里明确禁止奴隶制。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C026_25 -->

<!-- WORD:W027 -->
# tackle

**词源**：最初是“装备、器具”，来自中古荷兰语或中古低地德语 takel“船的索具”。动词由名词发展，后来有抓住、阻截以及着手处理问题等意义；可用“拿出装备认真应对”辅助记忆，但这只是助记。

<!-- USE:U027_01 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ v. · 着手认真处理难题
`及物，表示有决心地处理困难或任务，可说 tackle a problem/task。重点在积极应对，不保证问题已经解决；不用 tackle with a problem 代替直接接宾语的结构。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_01 USE:U027_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"着手认真处理难题","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1661/pg1661-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_01a USE:U027_01 -->
“I find it hard enough to tackle facts, Holmes, without flying away after theories and fancies,” said Lestrade.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_01b USE:U027_01 -->
“You are right,” said Holmes demurely; “you do find it very hard to tackle the facts.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_01 -->
“福尔摩斯，光是应付事实就已经够难了，更不用说飞到理论和幻想上去。”雷斯垂德说。福尔摩斯一本正经地回答：“你说得对，处理事实对你来说确实很难。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_01 -->
<!-- USE:U027_02 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ v. · 在球赛中抢截或阻挡对手
`运动中试图夺走对手控制的球，或拦住持球推进的人。足球、曲棍球等的动作与橄榄球、美式橄榄球中的抱摔不同，必须按项目规则理解；不一律译为把人摔倒。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_02 USE:U027_02,U027_23 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"在球赛中抢截或阻挡对手","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/18048/18048-h/18048-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_02a USE:U027_02,U027_23 -->
In Football Days (1916), William H. Edwards recalls Princeton players learning to tackle through “live tackling”, throwing coach Johnny Poe to the ground.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_02b -->
He contrasts this punishing practice with tackling a dummy, which he describes as less injurious.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_02 -->
在1916年的《橄榄球岁月》中，威廉·H.爱德华兹回忆，普林斯顿的球员曾通过“真人擒抱训练”学习擒抱，把教练约翰尼·坡扑倒在地。他把这种令身体饱受摧残的练法与擒抱假人的方式作比较，认为后者对球员造成的伤害较小。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_02 -->
<!-- USE:U027_03 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ v. · 就难题与某人交涉
`直接与某人谈一件有争议或需要处理的事，通常希望改变现状。常用 tackle somebody about/on something；宾语是被找来谈的人，不是一般地聊一个话题。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_03 USE:U027_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"就难题与某人交涉","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/38646/pg38646-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_03a USE:U027_03 -->
Joan and Nancy want a pony; Nancy protests when Dick intervenes: “I was to tackle father about that.”
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_03b -->
Dick objects to her way of speaking about their father, and Joan explains that she has bet Nancy four weeks' pocket money that he will refuse.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_03 -->
琼和南希想要一匹小马；迪克插手时，南希抗议说：“这件事本来该由我去找爸爸谈的。”迪克不赞成她这样说自己的父亲，琼便解释，她已拿四个星期的零用钱与南希打赌，认为爸爸不会答应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_03 -->
<!-- USE:U027_04 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ v. · 扑住并与人扭打
`指攻击、抓住或扑住某人与其搏斗，语境可在体育之外。它不只是言语上质问；是否正当、是否成功制服都不能只凭 tackle 推定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_04 USE:U027_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"扑住并与人扭打","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/31377/pg31377-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_04a -->
In Hoffmann's “Signor Formica”, the artists have won a fight against the police, and Salvator wants to pursue the fleeing officers.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_04b USE:U027_04 -->
Maria Agli, who despite his age had tackled the police as stoutly as the others, warns that the city guard will already know of the affair and arrest them; the group takes refuge in Nicolo Musso's house instead.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_04 -->
在霍夫曼的《福尔米卡先生》中，艺术家们打赢了与警察的一场搏斗，萨尔瓦托尔还想追赶逃走的警员。马里亚·阿利虽然年纪大了，先前与警察扭打时却和其他人一样勇猛；此时他提醒大家，城卫队肯定已获知这场冲突，会把他们逮捕。于是众人转而躲进尼科洛·穆索的家中。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_04 -->
<!-- USE:U027_05 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ v. · 用滑车装置固定
`指借助绳索、滑轮等 tackle 装置固定或控制物体。这里的手段是起重或索具装置，区别于抓住人的动作用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_05 USE:U027_05 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"用滑车装置固定","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_05a USE:U027_05 -->
The crew tackled the loose spar to the mast before the ship began to roll.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_05 -->
船开始摇晃之前，船员用滑车装置把松动的桅杆部件固定在主桅上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_05 -->
<!-- USE:U027_06 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ n. · 任务或运动所需装备
`通常不可数，泛指某项活动所需的器具，尤指钓鱼装备，如 fishing tackle。不是单独一件器具必然可说 a tackle；具体器物可用 a piece of tackle。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_06 USE:U027_06,U027_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"任务或运动所需装备","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1342/pg1342-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_06a USE:U027_06,U027_13 -->
Mr Darcy invited Mr Gardiner to fish there as often as he wished while he remained in the neighbourhood, offering to supply him with fishing tackle and pointing out the most promising parts of the stream.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_06 -->
达西先生邀请加德纳先生在附近逗留期间随时来这里钓鱼，愿意给他提供钓具，还指出溪流中最容易钓到鱼的地方。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_06 -->
<!-- USE:U027_07 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ n. · 绳索滑轮等组成的装置
`指由绳索、滑轮组、钩等组成，用于起重、操帆等的机械装置。组成部件与整个 tackle 要分清；可与 block and tackle 表达相关的滑轮绳索系统。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_07 USE:U027_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"绳索滑轮等组成的装置","sourceUrls":["https://whalesite.org/anthology/1891_Luce_Text-Book_of_Seamanship.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_07a USE:U027_07 -->
To find the force needed to balance a weight with a tackle, divide the weight by the number of rope sections at the movable block, ignoring friction.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_07 -->
要计算用滑车组平衡某个重物所需的力，就用重物的重量除以活动滑轮处的绳索段数，暂不计摩擦。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_07 -->
<!-- USE:U027_08 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ n. · 带绳钩的辘轳装置
`指装有绳索与钩的辘轳或绞盘装置。它与一般滑轮组同属机械器具，但这里重点是绕轴卷绳的结构，不把所有 tackle 都画成同一装置。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_08 USE:U027_08 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"带绳钩的辘轳装置","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_08a USE:U027_08 -->
The old tackle consisted of a windlass fitted with a long rope and an iron hook.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_08 -->
这套旧辘轳装置由一架绞盘、一条长绳和一个铁钩组成。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_08 -->
<!-- USE:U027_09 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ n. · 球赛中的一次抢截或阻截
`可数，指一次 tackle 动作，可说 a tackle by a defender。具体是抢球、截停还是抱摔，由运动项目决定；一次尝试也可称 tackle，不必都夺球成功。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_09 USE:U027_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"球赛中的一次抢截或阻截","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/18048/18048-h/18048-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_09a -->
Recalling a Yale–Princeton game in Football Days, William H. Edwards describes De Saulles's remarkable fifty-five-yard run.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_09b USE:U027_09 -->
He evaded Princeton's tacklers until Billy Bannard made a tackle on Princeton's 25-yard line.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_09 -->
威廉·H.爱德华兹在《橄榄球岁月》中回忆耶鲁队与普林斯顿队的一场比赛时，描写了德索尔斯一次出色的55码推进。他接连避开普林斯顿队的擒抱者，直到比利·班纳德在普林斯顿队一侧的25码线上完成一次擒抱，将他拦下。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_09 -->
<!-- USE:U027_10 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ n. · 美式橄榄球锋线阻截位置
`美式橄榄球用语，指前线靠近端位的位置。这里是阵形中的位置，区别于一次擒抱动作；具体左右侧、攻守安排由赛事情境确定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_10 USE:U027_10,U027_11,U027_22 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"美式橄榄球锋线阻截位置","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/49100/pg49100-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_10a USE:U027_10 -->
An 1895 assessment of St. Paul's football team describes Gardiner, at tackle, as a strong man in the line who gains ground when given the ball.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_10b USE:U027_11,U027_22 -->
Symons, the other tackle, is new but learning rapidly—and is already one of the team's best tacklers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_10 -->
一篇发表于1895年的圣保罗队美式橄榄球评述说，打阻截锋位置的加德纳是锋线上的强将，拿到球时能向前推进。另一名阻截锋西蒙斯虽是新人，却进步很快，而且已是队中最出色的擒抱手之一。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_10 -->
<!-- USE:U027_11 -->
## tackle /ˈtækəl/ n. · 美式橄榄球该位置的队员
`可数，指担任 tackle 位置的队员，而不是任何做过擒抱的人。谈球员位置时的 tackle 与动作义 a tackle 须靠句子辨别。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C027_10 USE:U027_11 -->
<!-- USE:U027_12 -->
## block and tackle /ˌblɑk ən ˈtækəl/ n. · 滑轮与绳索构成的滑车组
`指一组滑轮与绕过它们的绳索，尤其用来提升重物。block 指滑轮组或滑车部件，整个词组指配合工作的系统，不是“阻挡并擒抱”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_12 USE:U027_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"滑轮与绳索构成的滑车组","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/56585/pg56585-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_12a USE:U027_12 -->
Dana and Pearl's engineering bulletin explains how block and tackle can let an operator pull downward while the load moves upward.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_12b -->
A gain in force comes at the cost of a longer pull: in its ideal two-to-one example, neglecting friction, the operator pulls two feet of rope to raise the load one foot.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_12 -->
达纳与珀尔的工程技术通报解释了滑车组如何让操作者向下拉绳，同时使重物向上移动。省力的代价是要拉过更长的距离：在通报所举的理想二比一装置中，忽略摩擦时，操作者拉动两英尺的绳，重物只会上升一英尺。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_12 -->
<!-- USE:U027_13 -->
## fishing tackle /ˈfɪʃɪŋ ˌtækəl/ n.; fishing-tackle /ˈfɪʃɪŋ ˌtækəl/ n. · 钓鱼用具的总称
`通常不可数，指钓竿、线、钩、饵等钓鱼装备。带连字符与不带连字符是同词的书写形式，不另造一种不同的捕鱼方法。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C027_06 USE:U027_13 -->
<!-- USE:U027_14 -->
## rugby tackle /ˈrʌɡbi ˌtækəl/ n. · 抱住腿或髋部使人倒地的动作
`可数，指用双臂抱住人的腿或髋部使其倒下的动作，可用于橄榄球或描述相似动作。不是所有体育项目的 tackle 都是这种抱摔。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_14 USE:U027_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"抱住腿或髋部使人倒地的动作","sourceUrls":["https://www.cps.gov.uk/sites/default/files/documents/publications/Householders-2018.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_14a -->
A 2018 joint CPS–police statement distinguishes chasing a fleeing intruder from self-defence, saying that the same degree of force may no longer be reasonable.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_14b USE:U027_14 -->
It says a rugby tackle would probably be reasonable when using reasonable force to recover property or make a citizen's arrest, while urging attention to personal safety and rejecting force intended as malicious revenge or punishment.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_14 -->
英国皇家检察署与警方2018年的一份联合声明，将追赶逃离的入侵者与自卫作了区分，指出同样程度的武力在这种情况下可能已不合理。声明说，在使用合理武力追回财物或实施公民逮捕时，抱摔一下很可能属于合理范围，但同时要求考虑自身安全，并反对为恶意报复或惩罚而使用武力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_14 -->
<!-- USE:U027_15 -->
## rugby tackle /ˈrʌɡbi ˌtækəl/ v. · 抱住腿或髋部把人摔倒
`及物，指对某人实施这种抱摔，可说 rugby tackled somebody，也可用被动。用作动词时仍须保动作性质；它本身不说明实施者是球员还是警员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_15 USE:U027_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"抱住腿或髋部把人摔倒","sourceUrls":["https://assets.publishing.service.gov.uk/media/5c027a9aed915d746f2e991b/Ms_J_Jones_v_Tesco_Stores_Limited_1401052_16_REASONS.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_15a USE:U027_15 -->
A 2017 employment tribunal's reasons recount a worker's allegation that a colleague had rugby tackled her at work.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_15b USE:U027_15 -->
After interviewing him and more than seven other employees, the investigator concluded that she had not been rugby tackled, though the colleague's conduct had still been inappropriate.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_15 -->
一份2017年的英国就业审裁处裁判理由记述，一名员工指称同事曾在工作场所将她抱摔。调查人员询问了该同事及另外七人以上的员工后，认为她并未遭到抱摔，但该同事的举动仍不恰当。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_15 -->
<!-- USE:U027_16 -->
## crash-tackle /ˈkræʃ ˌtækəl/ n. · 凶猛的足球抢截
`英式足球用语，可数，指猛烈的一次 tackle。它描述动作强烈，不自动判为犯规，也不是汽车碰撞。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_16 USE:U027_16 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"凶猛的足球抢截","sourceUrls":["https://wp.fromtowntotown.org.uk/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/nuneaton-borough-1970-79_part-1-approved2.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_16a USE:U027_16 -->
For a match at Margate, Nuneaton’s historical account lists three players booked, including “Howshall for a crash tackle on Margate winger Baber”.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_16 -->
在记述一场客场对阵马盖特的比赛时，纽尼顿的历史记录列出三名被记名警告的球员，其中“豪斯霍尔是因为对马盖特队边锋巴伯的一次凶猛抢截”。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_16 -->
<!-- USE:U027_17 -->
## ledger-tackle /ˈlɛdʒər ˌtækəl/ n. · 以铅坠把饵稳在水底的钓具
`钓鱼用语，指用铅质重物使饵留在水底的装备。这里的 ledger 与账簿无关；重点是配重使饵保持在底部。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_17 USE:U027_17 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"以铅坠把饵稳在水底的钓具","sourceUrls":["https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b4/An_angler%27s_year_%28IA_anglersyear00pattrich%29.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_17a USE:U027_17 -->
In An Angler's Year, Charles S. Patterson lists ledger tackle among the rigs used for carp and weighs its advantages against its drawbacks.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_17b -->
It can cast farther than light float tackle, but its lead can sink into a soft, muddy lake bed; in very weedy lakes, an inaccurate cast can leave it smothered in weeds.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_17 -->
查尔斯·S.帕特森在《钓鱼人的一年》中，将沉底铅坠钓组列为钓鲤鱼所用的装备之一，并权衡它的优缺点。它能比轻型浮漂钓组抛得更远，但铅坠可能陷入松软、泥泞的湖底；在水草很密的湖中，抛投不准还可能让钓组埋进水草。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_17 -->
<!-- USE:U027_18 -->
## nose tackle /ˈnoʊz ˌtækəl/ n. · 美式橄榄球防线中央队员
`可数，指列阵时处在防守锋线中央的队员。nose 不指用鼻子擒抱；它是位置名称。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_18 USE:U027_18 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"美式橄榄球防线中央队员","sourceUrls":["https://www.army.mil/article/249540/fort_knox_eagles_football_team_pursuing_winning_season_after_being_sidelined_in_2020"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_18a USE:U027_18 -->
In an August 2021 report, Fort Knox defensive nose tackle Thomas Hackett recalled how disappointed his team had been when the previous season was cancelled.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_18b -->
For him, getting back onto the field meant seeing everyone again and enjoying a game that was more than just a sport to the players.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_18 -->
在2021年8月的一篇报道中，诺克斯堡队的防守鼻卫托马斯·哈克特回忆，上一赛季被取消时，全队有多么失望。对他而言，重返球场意味着再次见到大家，享受一项对球员来说远不止是普通运动的比赛。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_18 -->
<!-- USE:U027_19 -->
## nose tackle /ˈnoʊz ˌtækəl/ n. · 美式橄榄球防线中央位置
`指上述防守阵位本身，可说 play nose tackle。与表示该位置队员的 a nose tackle 区别，不能把每处都译成一个人。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_19 USE:U027_19 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"美式橄榄球防线中央位置","sourceUrls":["https://www.army.mil/article/45455/child_youth_and_school_services_bring_back_tackle_football"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_19a -->
A 2012 Army report on youth football in Wiesbaden describes Tomekia Williams volunteering as a team mom for both her sons.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_19b USE:U027_19 -->
Thirteen-year-old Daniel plays nose tackle and left guard, while his younger brother plays on the team for nine- to eleven-year-olds; their mother says the family makes sacrifices so the boys can enjoy a familiar part of life in the States.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_19 -->
美国陆军2012年的一篇关于威斯巴登青少年美式橄榄球的报道，介绍了托米基娅·威廉斯为两个儿子的球队担任家长志愿者的情况。13岁的丹尼尔打鼻卫和左护锋位置，弟弟则参加9至11岁年龄组；母亲说，家里虽很忙，仍愿意作出牺牲，让孩子们享受在美国时那种熟悉的日常生活。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_19 -->
<!-- USE:U027_20 -->
## tackle-block /ˈtækəl ˌblɑk/ n. · 供绳索绕行的滑轮
`指让绳索从其上绕过的滑轮部件。它是 tackle 装置的一部分，不等于整个滑轮绳索系统。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_20 USE:U027_20 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"供绳索绕行的滑轮","sourceUrls":["https://whalesite.org/anthology/1891_Luce_Text-Book_of_Seamanship.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_20a -->
In lifting the heaviest boats, the upper block of the yard tackle hooks into a winding pendant.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_20b USE:U027_20 -->
The other end of the pendant has a thimble for the hook of the upper yard tackle block.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_20 -->
吊起最重的艇时，帆桁滑车组的上滑轮组钩在一根起吊短索上。短索的另一端装有绳环套，供帆桁上滑轮组的钩子钩入。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_20 -->
<!-- USE:U027_21 -->
## tackle-fall /ˈtækəl ˌfɔl/ n. · 滑车组中施力的绳索
`指用于向滑车组施加力的绳索，即滑车通索。fall 在这里不是跌倒，也不指被吊的重物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_21 USE:U027_21 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"滑车组中施力的绳索","sourceUrls":["https://whalesite.org/anthology/1891_Luce_Text-Book_of_Seamanship.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_21a USE:U027_21 -->
Nippering is making fast the two parts of a laniard or tackle-fall while the purchase is fleeted.
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_21b -->
The turns are taken crossways between the parts to jam them.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_21 -->
调整滑车组位置时，用细绳绑牢系索或滑车通索的两段，这一做法叫作临时扎缚。绳圈在两段之间交叉缠绕，把它们夹紧。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_21 -->
<!-- USE:U027_22 -->
## tackler /ˈtæklər/ n. · 实施抢截或应对任务的人
`由 tackle 构成，指进行抢截、拦截或处理任务的人。运动语境中常指作出 tackle 的防守者；须从宾语或上下文判断具体动作，不把它当固定球员位置名。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C027_10 USE:U027_22 -->
<!-- USE:U027_23 -->
## tackling /ˈtæklɪŋ/ n. · 抢截或着手应对的行为
`指进行 tackle 的行为或方式，如比赛中的抢截、对问题的处理。它也可作 tackle 的现在分词；这个行为名词义须与下述装备义区分，不能仅凭词尾认定它只表示动作。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C027_02 USE:U027_23 -->
<!-- USE:U027_24 -->
## tackling /ˈtæklɪŋ/ n. · 成套器具、索具
`指 tackle 或 gear，即活动所需的整套装备，尤其可见于航海、起重等语境。航海读法也常为 /ˈteɪklɪŋ/。它指器具本身，区别于现代运动语境中的“抢截行为”；阅读旧技术文献时不能一律当作现在分词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C027_24 USE:U027_24 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"成套器具、索具","sourceUrls":["https://whalesite.org/anthology/1891_Luce_Text-Book_of_Seamanship.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S027_24a USE:U027_24 -->
Ignoring friction, multiply the weight a single rope will bear by the number of rope sections at the moving block to find what weight the given tackling can raise.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C027_24 -->
暂不计摩擦，将一根绳索能承受的重量乘以活动滑轮处的绳索段数，就能算出这套索具能够提升多大的重量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C027_24 -->

<!-- WORD:W028 -->
# texture

**词源**：经中古法语并直接借自拉丁语 textura“织成的结构、织物”，与 texere“编织”有关。由线如何织在一起，扩展到材料内部构成、触感，以及音乐、文字和生活由各部分组合出的整体特点。

<!-- USE:U028_01 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 表面触感或外观质感
`指物体或物质表面摸起来、看起来的特性，如光滑、粗糙、丝滑。可数时可比较不同 textures；不可数时泛指这种性质。不是物体形状或颜色的简单同义词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_01 USE:U028_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"表面触感或外观质感","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/9939/old/8loc510h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_01a USE:U028_01 -->
The Woman's Institute Library of Cookery explains how to judge the quality of lemons, using the same criteria as for grapefruit: their weight, the texture of their skin, and their overall color and shape.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_01 -->
《女子学院烹饪文库》说明，判断柠檬品质可采用与葡萄柚相同的标准：重量、果皮的质感，以及整体颜色和形状。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_01 -->
<!-- USE:U028_02 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 材料内部的组织质地
`指食物、土壤等小组成部分的排列与结构，如疏松、有孔、密实、易碎。它会影响口感或触感，但重点是内部构成，而不只是表面是否粗糙。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_02 USE:U028_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"材料内部的组织质地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/12299/pg12299-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_02a -->
In soft pines, there is little contrast between the early wood and late wood within a growth ring.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_02b USE:U028_02 -->
As a result, the wood is very uniform in texture and easy to work.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_02 -->
软松木同一生长轮内的早材与晚材差别不大。因此，木材的组织质地很均匀，也容易加工。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_02 -->
<!-- USE:U028_03 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 纺织品的织纹结构
`指织物中纱线等如何排列、交织而形成的纹理。要区分织造结构与印在表面的图案；不同线的组织方式会产生不同 texture。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_03 USE:U028_03,U028_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"纺织品的织纹结构","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/77150/77150-h/77150-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_03a USE:U028_03 -->
Roberts Beaumont distinguishes a fabric's weave from its decorative form and color: crossing warp and weft yarns produces the fabric's texture.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_03b USE:U028_09 -->
In his four versions of a woven design, the textural effect becomes more distinct as the contrast between the shades of warp and weft becomes stronger.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_03 -->
罗伯茨·博蒙特把织物的织造结构与其装饰形态、颜色区分开来：经纬纱线交织，形成织物的组织质地。在他展示的同一织纹的四种配色中，经线与纬线的色调对比越鲜明，织纹效果就越清楚。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_03 -->
<!-- USE:U028_04 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 艺术作品对物体结构细节的表现
`艺术用语，指画作等如何表现对象的构造、细节和质地。画面能表现石头的粗糙，并不意味着画布表面本身一定同样粗糙。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_04 USE:U028_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"艺术作品对物体结构细节的表现","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/4672/pg4672-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_04a -->
In his review of the Panama-Pacific exhibition, Eugen Neuhaus finds only Renoir's still-life successful among the painter's two works on show.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_04b USE:U028_04 -->
He objects that the landscape's fuzziness prevents the viewer from seeing differences of texture among the objects in it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_04 -->
在对巴拿马—太平洋博览会画展的评论中，欧根·诺伊豪斯认为，雷诺阿参展的两幅作品里，只有静物画称得上成功。他批评另一幅风景画画得模糊，使观者看不出画中各物体之间的质感差异。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_04 -->
<!-- USE:U028_05 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 声部结合形成的音乐织体
`音乐用语，指声部怎样组合形成整体声音的组织与质量，如多条旋律的交织。它不是单个音的音高，也不只是音量大小。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_05 USE:U028_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"声部结合形成的音乐织体","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/30560/30560-h/30560-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_05a -->
Walter Raymond Spalding contrasts music led by one chief melody with polyphonic music, in which several melodic lines may be equally important.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_05b USE:U028_05 -->
Its interwoven texture requires broader listening: instead of following only the upper melody, the listener must also attend to the bass and inner voices.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_05 -->
沃尔特·雷蒙德·斯波尔丁将以一条主要旋律为中心的音乐，与几条旋律线可能同样重要的复调音乐作比较。复调音乐交织而成的织体，要求听者扩大聆听范围：不能只追随最上方的旋律，还须留意低音和中间声部。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_05 -->
<!-- USE:U028_06 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 文字由意象、音韵等形成的质感
`指一篇文字通过意象、头韵、措辞等组合产生的艺术特点与感受。它与故事梗概不同；谈 texture 时通常要指出哪些语言成分形成这种效果。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_06 USE:U028_06 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"文字由意象、音韵等形成的质感","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/42773/42773-h/42773-h.htm"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_06a USE:U028_06 -->
Genuine poetry differs from prose in the entire texture of its web.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_06b -->
From the same threads the loom may weave plain stuff or richest brocade; and thus of much the same words are made prose and poetry.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_06 -->
真正的诗歌与散文相比，整幅文字织物的质感都不同。同样的线，经织机之手，可以成为朴素的布料，也可以成为华美的锦缎；同样，散文与诗歌也可以由大体相同的词语写成。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_06 -->
<!-- USE:U028_07 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ n. · 各组成部分形成的独特面貌
`可比喻生活、社会等由不同活动或成分共同构成的特性，如 the texture of daily life。重点在相互交织的细节，不只是一个抽象评价“好”或“坏”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_07 USE:U028_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"各组成部分形成的独特面貌","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/10865/pg10865-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_07a -->
William Archer defends the chance encounter in Letty when Letchmere's party and Mandeville's party choose the same restaurant.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_07b USE:U028_07 -->
He calls it an everyday happening, desirable in drama as part of the ordinary surface-texture of life; excluding chance entirely would be an unreasonable restriction on the representation of life.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_07 -->
威廉·阿彻为《莱蒂》中的一次偶遇辩护：莱奇米尔一行人与曼德维尔一行人碰巧选了同一家餐馆。他认为这是日常生活中常有的事情，作为生活日常面貌的一部分，放进戏剧是可取的；若完全排除偶然，就会对生活的再现施加不合理的限制。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_07 -->
<!-- USE:U028_08 -->
## texture /ˈtɛkstʃər/ v. · 使表面具有特定质地
`及物，指给材料或表面赋予某种纹理、质感，通常以过去分词 textured 作形容词。动作义 texture a surface 与名词 the texture of a surface 区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_08 USE:U028_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"使表面具有特定质地","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US8846551B2/en"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_08a USE:U028_08 -->
In their laser-processing patent, Mool C. Gupta and Barada K. Nayak explain that intense, ultra-short laser pulses rapidly texture a material's surface.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_08b -->
The resulting surface texture increases light absorption, while subsequent crystallization changes the material's electronic properties.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_08 -->
在激光加工专利中，穆尔·C.古普塔和巴拉达·K.纳亚克解释说，高强度超短激光脉冲会迅速在材料表面形成纹理。形成的表面纹理增加了光的吸收，而随后的结晶会改变材料的电子性质。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_08 -->
<!-- USE:U028_09 -->
## textural /ˈtɛkstʃərəl/ adj. · 与质地或织体有关的
`表示涉及 texture 的性质，具体可为材料组织、表面质感、音乐织体等。它是关系形容词，不必然表示表面粗糙，也不等于 textured“做出纹理的”。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C028_03 USE:U028_09 -->
<!-- USE:U028_10 -->
## texturally /ˈtɛkstʃərəli/ adv. · 在质地或织体方面
`表示从 texture 的角度作比较或描述。食物语境谈组织口感，音乐语境谈声部组合；它不表示“用文字的方式”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_10 USE:U028_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"在质地或织体方面","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/4672/pg4672-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_10a -->
Eugen Neuhaus acknowledges the painstaking drawing in Luigi Bazzani's painting The Arch of Septimius Severus, yet rejects its effect.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_10b USE:U028_10 -->
Texturally, he argues, the canvas is wrong: sky, stone and everything else look like glass instead of the different things they should represent.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_10 -->
欧根·诺伊豪斯承认，路易吉·巴扎尼的《塞普蒂米乌斯·塞维鲁凯旋门》绘制得极为用心，却不认可它的画面效果。他认为，从质感表现上说，这幅画是失当的：天空、石头和其他一切都像玻璃，而不像它们各自应当表现的物体。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_10 -->
<!-- USE:U028_11 -->
## textured /ˈtɛkstʃərd/ adj. · 有明显纹理或特殊质感的
`通常放名词前，指表面并非完全平滑，而具有特定纹理，如 textured surface。也可用作 texture 的过去分词；具体纹理可能是处理而成，也可能是材料本身的特点。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_11 USE:U028_11 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"有明显纹理或特殊质感的","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US8846551B2/en"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_11a USE:U028_11 -->
Gupta and Nayak propose using the textured surface made by their laser process to manufacture an optical beam block designed to absorb substantially all light from the visible to the far-infrared.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_11 -->
古普塔和纳亚克提出，可利用其激光工艺制成的纹理表面制造光束阻挡器，设计目标是吸收从可见光到远红外波段几乎全部的光。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_11 -->
<!-- USE:U028_12 -->
## textureless /ˈtɛkstʃərləs/ adj. · 缺少可辨质感或组织变化的
`表示没有明显 texture。用于表面可指缺少可辨纹理；用于艺术等则须结合上下文理解其组织或质感的缺失，不等于绝对没有任何物理构成。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_12 USE:U028_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"缺少可辨质感或组织变化的","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US20230394740A1/en"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_12a -->
In a patent on 3D modeling, Chelhwon Kim and Nicolas Dahlquist use a hamburger and fries on a white plate to explain a reconstruction problem.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_12b USE:U028_12 -->
The food has features that can be tracked across viewing angles, but the plate's largely textureless surface looks almost the same from different angles.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_12c -->
Without enough matching points, the reconstructed plate may become distorted.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_12 -->
在一份三维建模专利中，切尔旺·金和尼古拉斯·达尔奎斯特用白盘子上的汉堡与薯条说明一种重建难题。食物具有能在不同视角间追踪的特征，但盘子大部分缺少可辨纹理的表面，从不同角度看都几乎一样。缺少足够的对应点，重建出的盘子就可能变形。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_12 -->
<!-- USE:U028_13 -->
## retexture /ˌriˈtɛkstʃər/ v. · 处理材料使恢复原有质地
`及物，指处理布料、衣物等，使其原来的质地恢复。re- 在这里强调恢复，不是任意把表面重新做成一种全新的质感。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_13 USE:U028_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"处理材料使恢复原有质地","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US6989037B2/en"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_13a -->
Robert S. Brown and Kenneth B. Higgins describe a way to refurbish used carpet tiles: heat the pile yarn in a steam chamber, then direct a cleaning solution at it to lift the yarn.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_13b USE:U028_13 -->
They report that, when combined with several cleaning devices of the type they describe, this method can retexture pile yarn and remove dirt or matting at substantially lower cleaning pressures than the earlier process.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_13 -->
罗伯特·S.布朗和肯尼思·B.希金斯介绍了一种翻新旧地毯方块的方法：先在蒸汽室内加热绒毛纱线，再朝其喷射清洗液，使纱线重新蓬起。他们报告说，在配合前文所述类型的多个清洗装置使用时，这种方法能恢复绒毛纱线的质地，去除污垢或结团，而且所需清洗压力明显低于先前工艺。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_13 -->
<!-- USE:U028_14 -->
## texturize /ˈtɛkstʃəraɪz/ v.; texturise /ˈtɛkstʃəraɪz/ v. · 加工织物或食品以赋予特定质地
`及物，表示通过处理使织物或食品形成需要的组织、质感。-ize 与 -ise 是拼写变体，不是两种不同工艺；常见过去分词 texturized 作形容词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_14 USE:U028_14,U028_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"加工织物或食品以赋予特定质地","sourceUrls":["https://patents.google.com/patent/US4095001A/en"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_14a -->
A 1978 patent by William M. Hildebolt and Murray T. Hundt describes a process for giving protein material a meat-like texture.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_14b USE:U028_14 -->
Heat is applied as the dough is extruded through a die to texturize both surfaces of the thin sheet.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_14c USE:U028_15 -->
A hot gas stream then breaks the surface-texturized protein into segments and carries them through a treatment zone for further processing.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_14 -->
威廉·M.希尔德博尔特和默里·T.亨特的一份1978年专利，介绍了一种让蛋白质原料获得近似肉类组织质地的工艺。糊状原料经模具挤压成薄片时接受加热，使薄片两面形成所需的组织质地。随后，热气流将表面已做过质地处理的蛋白质薄片截成小段，并带着它们通过处理区，继续加工。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_14 -->
<!-- USE:U028_15 -->
## texturized /ˈtɛkstʃəraɪzd/ adj. · 经过赋予特定质地处理的
`表示织物或食品已经过 texturize 处理，获得某种特定的组织、触感或口感。它也作动词过去分词，不能仅由这一词推定具体原料或加工方法。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C028_14 USE:U028_15 -->
<!-- USE:U028_16 -->
## textured vegetable protein /ˌtɛkstʃərd ˈvɛdʒtəbəl ˈproʊtin/ n.; TVP /ˌti vi ˈpi/ n. · 加工成近似肉质的大豆蛋白
`TVP 是 textured vegetable protein 的缩略形式；指从大豆取得、经加工形成类似肉，尤其肉末组织的蛋白食品。名称有商标使用背景；词组中的 textured 修饰整个植物蛋白产品，不能把它当一种天然肉类。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C028_16 USE:U028_16 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"加工成近似肉质的大豆蛋白","sourceUrls":["https://www.nutrition.va.gov/docs/Cookbooks/PracticalPlantBasedCookingMAY2023.pdf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_16a -->
The VA's Practical Plant-Based Cooking guide recommends choosing a variety of foods and balancing meals with a protein source, fiber-rich starch, non-starchy vegetables and fruit.
<!-- SENTENCE:S028_16b USE:U028_16 -->
Alongside whole soy foods such as tofu and tempeh, it lists seitan and textured vegetable protein (TVP) as other plant-based protein options.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C028_16 -->
美国退伍军人事务部的《实用植物性烹饪》指南建议选择多样食物，并在每餐中搭配蛋白质来源、富含膳食纤维的淀粉类食物、非淀粉类蔬菜和水果。除了豆腐、天贝等全大豆食品，指南还把面筋和组织化植物蛋白（TVP）列为其他植物性蛋白质选择。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C028_16 -->

<!-- WORD:W029 -->
# threshold

**词源**：古英语已有表示“门槛”的 threscold 等形式。前半部分与 thresh 相关，可能原与踩踏有关；后半部分来源未明，不把它拆成现代 hold“握住”编成故事。由跨过门槛，产生进入新阶段、达到反应临界点等比喻。

<!-- USE:U029_01 -->
## threshold /ˈθrɛʃhoʊld/ n. · 门槛或门口
`可数，通常单数，指门道底部跨过的一条木石等，也可指门口本身。常说 the threshold of a room、cross the threshold；不是整条走廊。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C029_01 USE:U029_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"门槛或门口","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1661/pg1661-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_01a -->
We stepped straight from the carriage into the hall, so that I could not get even a fleeting glimpse of the front of the house.
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_01b USE:U029_01 -->
The instant I crossed the threshold, the door slammed heavily behind us and I heard the carriage drive away.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C029_01 -->
我们几乎直接从马车踏进大厅，以至于我连房子的正面都没来得及看一眼。我刚跨过门槛，门就在身后重重关上，接着我听见马车驶走了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C029_01 -->
<!-- USE:U029_02 -->
## threshold /ˈθrɛʃhoʊld/ n. · 某事开始发生的临界水平
`可数，指达到后会发生变化或触发规则的数量、水平或界限。常用 threshold for something 或 reach/exceed a threshold；高低必须结合触发什么来理解。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C029_02 USE:U029_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"某事开始发生的临界水平","sourceUrls":["https://ukhsa.blog.gov.uk/2026/01/08/what-is-the-difference-between-weather-health-alerts-and-severe-weather-warnings/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_02a USE:U029_02 -->
UKHSA explains that, before its June 2023 update, Weather-Health Alerts were triggered when temperatures reached a specific threshold.
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_02b USE:U029_02 -->
The revised system considers temperature thresholds alongside the event’s expected duration, affected regions, possible escalation, compounding risks and existing pressure on services.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C029_02 -->
英国卫生安全局解释说，在2023年6月更新之前，天气健康警报是在气温达到某个特定阈值时触发的。修订后的系统在考虑温度阈值的同时，还会考虑事件预计持续多久、受影响的地区、情况可能升级的程度、叠加风险，以及服务系统已有的压力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C029_02 -->
<!-- USE:U029_03 -->
## on the threshold of /ɑn ðə ˈθrɛʃhoʊld əv/ prep. · 即将进入新阶段
`表示处在某件新事物的开端，或即将经历它，常带期待新局面的意味。of 后接阶段、时期或事件，不表示站在真实门槛上。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C029_03 USE:U029_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"即将进入新阶段","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/news-release/nasa-perseverance-mars-rover-to-acquire-first-sample/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_03a -->
As Perseverance prepared to collect its first Martian rock sample in 2021, NASA’s Thomas Zurbuchen expected it to transform understanding of Mars as Apollo’s first sample had transformed understanding of the Moon.
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_03b USE:U029_03 -->
“We are on the threshold of a new era of planetary science and discovery,” he said.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C029_03 -->
2021年，毅力号准备采集第一份火星岩石样本时，NASA的托马斯·祖布琴期待，这份样本能像阿波罗首次带回的样本改变人类对月球的认识那样，改变人类对火星的认识。他说：“我们正处在行星科学与发现的新时代的开端。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C029_03 -->
<!-- USE:U029_04 -->
## threshold /ˈθrɛʃhoʊld/ n. · 产生感觉或反应的刺激下限
`生理、心理用语，指刺激低于某一水平时不引起相关反应，达到该水平才被感觉到或触发反应。pain threshold 是个体开始感到疼痛的临界点，不等于所能忍受的最大疼痛；它涉及个人的疼痛体验，不能只凭外部刺激量就断定一个人的痛感。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C029_04 USE:U029_04 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"产生感觉或反应的刺激下限","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/621/pg621-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_04a USE:U029_04 -->
In The Varieties of Religious Experience, William James describes a threshold of consciousness: the amount of noise, pressure or other external stimulus needed to arouse attention.
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_04b USE:U029_04 -->
Someone with a high threshold may doze through noise that immediately wakes someone with a low one.
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_04c USE:U029_04 -->
He goes on to ask whether people living on different sides of a “pain-threshold” might need different sorts of religion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C029_04 -->
在《宗教经验之种种》中，威廉·詹姆斯谈到意识的阈值：唤起注意所需的噪声、压力或其他外部刺激量。阈值高的人可能在一片响声中仍然打盹，而同样的响声会立刻惊醒阈值低的人。接着，他提出一个问题：通常处在“痛苦阈值”不同两侧的人，是否可能需要不同类型的宗教？
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C029_04 -->
<!-- USE:U029_05 -->
## threshold /ˈθrɛʃhoʊld/ n. · 物理效应出现的最低限值
`物理学用语，指低于它不会发生某项反应的限值，尤其可指产生特定效应所需的最低辐射剂量。应说明具体效应和模型范围，不能把任何辐射风险一概说成存在安全阈值。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C029_05 USE:U029_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"物理效应出现的最低限值","sourceUrls":["https://www.osha.gov/ionizing-radiation/health-effects"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_05a USE:U029_05 -->
For deterministic radiation effects, a threshold dose must be reached before the particular effect is expected to occur.
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_05b USE:U029_05 -->
Stochastic health effects are assumed not to have a threshold dose below which they do not occur.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C029_05 -->
对于确定性辐射效应，剂量达到某个阈值后，才预期会出现相应效应。随机性健康效应则被假定不存在一个低于它就不会出现效应的阈剂量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C029_05 -->
<!-- USE:U029_06 -->
## threshold /ˈθrɛʃhoʊld/ n. · 工资或税收规则的起点
`尤见英式用法，指工资或税收尺度上在特定条件下生效的一个档位或起点，如 tax threshold。常作名词定语；具体地区、时期、税种或工资条件不能省掉后仍当通用规则。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C029_06 USE:U029_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"工资或税收规则的起点","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/maintaining-income-tax-and-equivalent-national-insurance-contributions-thresholds-until-5-april-2031","https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/maintaining-income-tax-and-equivalent-national-insurance-contributions-thresholds-until-5-april-2031/income-tax-maintaining-the-personal-allowance-and-the-basic-rate-limit-for-income-tax-and-equivalent-national-insurance-contributions-thresholds-unt"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S029_06a USE:U029_06 -->
For savings and dividend income across the UK, HMRC’s November 2025 tax note proposed keeping the higher rate threshold at £50,270 until 5 April 2031.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C029_06 -->
英国税务海关总署2025年11月的税务说明提出，对于英国各地的储蓄和股息收入，将较高税率档位的起点维持为50,270英镑，直至2031年4月5日。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C029_06 -->

<!-- WORD:W030 -->
# withdraw

**词源**：由较早的 with“向后、离开”和 draw“拉”组合，核心形象是“拉回、抽走”。这里的 with 不是现代最熟悉的“和”；从抽回物体扩展到撤兵、退出、撤回言论等。

<!-- USE:U030_01 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 把东西抽出、移开或收回
`较正式，及物，指从某处拿出、拉回或移走某物。常用 withdraw something from something；手从对方手中抽回、纸从口袋取出，都要靠宾语和 from 的起点判断方向。过去式 withdrew /wɪðˈdru/，过去分词 withdrawn /wɪðˈdrɔn/。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_01 USE:U030_01 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"把东西抽出、移开或收回","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1661/pg1661-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_01a -->
A hand appeared through a gap in the stone floor and felt about in the little area of light.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_01b USE:U030_01 -->
Then it was withdrawn as suddenly as it had appeared, and all was dark again except for a single spark of light between the stones.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_01 -->
一只手从石板地面的缝隙中伸出来，在那一小片亮光中摸索。随后，它又像出现时那样突然地缩了回去，四周重新陷入黑暗，只剩石缝间的一点亮光。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_01 -->
<!-- USE:U030_02 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 部队撤离或撤回部队
`可不及物，表示部队离开战斗或驻扎地点；也可及物，指撤回部队。withdraw from 是撤离起点，withdraw to 是转往地点；计划撤军、要求撤军与已经撤军须分清。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_02 USE:U030_02 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"部队撤离或撤回部队","sourceUrls":["https://armedservices.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=6572"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_02a USE:U030_02 -->
On 2 May 2026, U.S. committee chairmen Roger Wicker and Mike Rogers expressed concern about the Pentagon’s decision to withdraw approximately 5,000 troops from Germany.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_02b USE:U030_02 -->
They argued for moving those forces east within Europe instead of withdrawing them from the continent altogether.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_02 -->
2026年5月2日，美国国会委员会主席罗杰·威克与迈克·罗杰斯对五角大楼决定从德国撤出约5,000名军人表示担忧。他们主张将这些部队向欧洲东部调动，而不是完全撤离欧洲大陆。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_02 -->
<!-- USE:U030_03 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 从账户提取资金
`及物，指把钱从银行等账户中取出，常用 withdraw money from an account。取款不必只指现金动作，也不能把向账户存钱说成 withdraw。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_03 USE:U030_03 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"从账户提取资金","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/individual-savings-accounts/transferring-your-isa"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_03a -->
To switch ISA providers, contact the provider you want to move to and complete an ISA transfer form.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_03b USE:U030_03 -->
If you withdraw the money without doing this, you will not be able to reinvest that part of your tax-free allowance again.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_03 -->
要更换 ISA 账户服务商，请联系你想转入的服务商，并填写 ISA 转移表。如果不这样办理而直接取出资金，就无法再将这一部分免税额度重新投入。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_03 -->
<!-- USE:U030_04 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 退出活动或组织
`不及物，停止参加某项活动、谈判、比赛或组织，常用 withdraw from。表示不再参与，区别于暂时离开房间；及物时可把自己负责的人从某项活动中撤出，如 withdraw a child from school。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_04 USE:U030_04,U030_10 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"退出活动或组织","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/guidance/what-to-do-if-you-want-to-withdraw-from-your-course"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_04a -->
This guidance applies to England and to undergraduate courses started before 1 January 2027.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_04b USE:U030_04 -->
Speak to a student finance adviser at your university or college before you withdraw.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_04c USE:U030_10 -->
Any Maintenance Loan paid after your withdrawal date must be repaid straight away.
