<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_04 -->
这份指引适用于英格兰，且针对2027年1月1日之前开始的本科课程。退课之前，请先向所在大学或学院的学生资助顾问咨询。退课日期之后支付的任何生活费贷款，都必须立即归还。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_04 -->
<!-- USE:U030_05 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 撤回言论或声明
`较正式，及物，表示收回已经说过的话，希望不再将其作为有效主张。可说 withdraw a remark/statement；不是仅仅停止继续说话。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_05 USE:U030_05 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"撤回言论或声明","sourceUrls":["https://api.parliament.uk/historic-hansard/commons/1967/jun/15/rhodesia"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_05a USE:U030_05 -->
During Rhodesia questions in the Commons on 15 June 1967, the Speaker twice required Mr Winnick to withdraw his remark.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_05b USE:U030_05 -->
“I withdraw,” Winnick replied.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_05c -->
The Prime Minister then asked permission to answer an earlier part of the question, and the Speaker allowed it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_05 -->
在1967年6月15日英国下议院有关罗得西亚的质询中，议长两次要求温尼克议员收回他的那句话。温尼克回答：“我收回。”随后，首相请求允许回答问题较早的另一部分，议长同意了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_05 -->
<!-- USE:U030_06 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 取消支持、承诺或措施
`及物，表示终止或撤销已经给出的支持、许可、承诺等，也可把某物撤出使用。withdraw support、withdraw an offer 着眼于不再使其继续有效，不必都涉及曾说错话。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_06 USE:U030_06 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"取消支持、承诺或措施","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/supplements-to-the-nhs-constitution-for-england/the-handbook-to-the-nhs-constitution-for-england"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_06a USE:U030_06 -->
The NHS Constitution handbook for England says that, where consent is the legal basis for processing data, you can give or withdraw your consent after your initial decision.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_06b USE:U030_06 -->
Withdrawal applies to continuing and future uses of the information covered by that consent.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_06c -->
Removing the data entirely from previous uses may not be practicable, though anonymising it may be possible.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_06 -->
英格兰《NHS宪章手册》指出，如果处理数据的法律依据是同意，你可以在最初决定之后再给予或撤回同意。撤回适用于该同意所涉及的信息的持续使用和未来使用。对于过去的使用，可能无法把数据完全移除，不过可能可以将其匿名化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_06 -->
<!-- USE:U030_07 -->
## withdraw /wɪðˈdrɔ/ v. · 退开、离开原处
`不及物，指人退到一边、离开或向后移动，常用 from 或 to 说明位置。可以是从交谈场所退出，并不自动表示辞职或退出组织。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_07 USE:U030_07 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"退开、离开原处","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/1661/pg1661-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_07a -->
I found Holmes in deep conversation with an elderly gentleman.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_07b USE:U030_07 -->
With an apology for my intrusion, I was about to withdraw when Holmes pulled me abruptly into the room and closed the door behind me.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_07 -->
我发现福尔摩斯正与一位上了年纪的绅士深入交谈。我为自己的打扰道了歉，正准备退出去，福尔摩斯却突然把我拉进房间，关上了身后的门。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_07 -->
<!-- USE:U030_08 -->
## withdrawal /wɪðˈdrɔəl/ n. · 移除、撤回或终止
`较正式，指移走某物、撤回支持或终止某项安排的行为与过程，可用 the withdrawal of something。可抽象指过程，也可指一次撤回；具体撤的是什么必须由语境确定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_08 USE:U030_08 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"移除、撤回或终止","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/agreement-holders-guide-capital-grants-higher-tier-capital-grants-and-protection-and-infrastructure-grants-2025/agreement-holders-guide-capital-grants-higher-tier-capital-grants-and-protection-and-infrastructure-grants-2025"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_08a USE:U030_08 -->
The 2025 capital-grants agreement holder’s guide warns that withdrawal of support claimed may follow a serious breach, false evidence or negligent failure to provide necessary information.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_08b -->
If support must be withdrawn for these reasons, the agreement is terminated and the holder cannot reapply for the scheme for two years.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_08c -->
The guide also provides a right to appeal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_08 -->
2025年资本拨款协议持有人指南警告，严重违约、提供虚假证据，或因疏忽未提供必要信息，都可能导致所申请的资助被撤销。如果因这些原因必须撤销资助，协议就会终止，持有人两年内不得重新申请该计划。指南也规定了申诉权。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_08 -->
<!-- USE:U030_09 -->
## withdrawal /wɪðˈdrɔəl/ n. · 部队撤离
`指部队撤退、撤离驻地或战区的过程或一次行动，常用 troop withdrawal、withdrawal from。需分清宣布、开始、完成等不同阶段。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_09 USE:U030_09 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"部队撤离","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/government/news/operation-toral-draws-to-an-end-as-uk-transitions-to-new-phase-of-support-to-afghanistan"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_09a USE:U030_09 -->
In its 8 July 2021 announcement, the Ministry of Defence said the coordinated withdrawal of NATO forces from Afghanistan had begun on 1 May and that the UK had withdrawn most of its personnel.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_09b -->
A small number of UK military personnel would temporarily remain during the transition.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_09 -->
英国国防部在2021年7月8日的公告中表示，北约部队从阿富汗协调撤离已于5月1日开始，而英国已撤出了大部分人员。少量英国军人会在过渡期间暂时留下。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_09 -->
<!-- USE:U030_10 -->
## withdrawal /wɪðˈdrɔəl/ n. · 退出活动或组织
`通常不可数，表示决定停止参与某活动或组织，常用 withdrawal from。它是退出行为或状态，不是把其中某件物品拿走。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:C030_04 USE:U030_10 -->
<!-- USE:U030_11 -->
## withdrawal /wɪðˈdrɔəl/ n. · 从账户取出的一笔款或取款
`可数，指从银行账户中取出的一笔钱，也可指取款动作，如 make a withdrawal。与账户余额、向账户存入的 deposit 区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_11 USE:U030_11 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"从账户取出的一笔款或取款","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/individual-savings-accounts/withdrawing-your-money"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_11a USE:U030_11 -->
Check the terms of your ISA to see if there are any rules or charges for making withdrawals.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_11b -->
There are different rules for taking your money out of a Lifetime ISA.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_11 -->
查看你的 ISA 账户条款，确认取款是否受规则限制或需要收费。从终身 ISA 账户提取资金，则有不同的规则。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_11 -->
<!-- USE:U030_12 -->
## withdrawal /wɪðˈdrɔəl/ n. · 停用成瘾物质时的戒断过程
`通常不可数，指停止使用已成瘾物质的过程或出现不适反应的阶段，常用 withdrawal from a drug、withdrawal symptoms。不是单纯的药物无效或一般不舒服；具体风险和处理须按相应医疗情境判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_12 USE:U030_12 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"停用成瘾物质时的戒断过程","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/guidance/clinical-guidelines-for-alcohol-treatment/annex-3-alcohol-withdrawal-symptoms"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_12a USE:U030_12 -->
Guidance for alcohol treatment staff distinguishes mild withdrawal symptoms, including sweating, nausea and mild tremor, from acute withdrawal in moderate or severe dependence after abrupt stopping or substantial reduction of alcohol use.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_12b -->
It warns that severe cases can be life threatening and need urgent medical attention.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_12 -->
面向酒精治疗工作人员的指引，将出汗、恶心、轻微震颤等轻度戒断症状，与中重度依赖者突然停止或大幅减少饮酒后出现的急性戒断加以区别。它警告，严重病例可能危及生命，需要紧急医疗照护。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_12 -->
<!-- USE:U030_13 -->
## withdrawal /wɪðˈdrɔəl/ n. · 性交中在射精前抽出阴茎
`指 coitus interruptus，即性交中在射精前将阴茎从阴道抽出。这个性健康语境中的名词义与银行取款、戒断均不同；词义本身不承诺避孕效果。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_13 USE:U030_13 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"性交中在射精前抽出阴茎","sourceUrls":["https://www.cdc.gov/contraception/hcp/usmec/coitus-interruptus-withdrawal.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_13a USE:U030_13 -->
Coitus interruptus, also known as withdrawal, is a contraceptive method in which the penis is completely removed from the vagina and away from the external genitalia before ejaculation.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_13b -->
It does not protect against sexually transmitted infections, including HIV infection.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_13 -->
性交中断法，也称体外排精，是指在射精之前，将阴茎完全移出阴道并远离外生殖器的一种避孕方法。它不能预防性传播感染，包括 HIV 感染。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_13 -->
<!-- USE:U030_14 -->
## withdrawn /wɪðˈdrɔn/ adj. · 沉默退缩、不愿交往的
`形容人十分安静、怕羞或与人疏离，常作表语，如 become withdrawn。它不自动等于某一种精神疾病；withdrawn 也作 withdraw 的过去分词，the withdrawn offer 不是“害羞的提议”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_14 USE:U030_14 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"沉默退缩、不愿交往的","sourceUrls":["https://help-for-early-years-providers.education.gov.uk/health-and-wellbeing/mental-health-for-early-years-children"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_14a -->
DfE guidance notes that all children sometimes need a break from interaction, and some are naturally quieter and more reserved.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_14b USE:U030_14 -->
To support a withdrawn child, practitioners should build up interactions slowly, show consistent interest in the child’s play and communication, give them space, and let them approach new experiences in their own time.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_14 -->
英国教育部指引指出，所有儿童有时都需要暂时停止互动，有些儿童天生更安静、内敛。为了支持一个沉默退缩的孩子，保育教育人员应慢慢建立互动，持续关注孩子的游戏和交流，给他们所需的空间，并让他们按自己的节奏接近新体验。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_14 -->
<!-- USE:U030_15 -->
## withdrawing room /wɪðˈdrɔɪŋ ˌrum/ n. · 旧称供退席后休息会客的房间
`古义，指 drawing room，即住宅中用来休息、接待客人的客厅。这里 withdraw 是从原先活动处退开；不是取款室，也不把所有 drawing room 都解释为画画的房间。`

<!-- CONTEXT:C030_15 USE:U030_15 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"旧称供退席后休息会客的房间","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/5999/pg5999-images.html"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_15a -->
Lewis Bertram pulled down part of the family’s ruins and used the stones to build a narrow, three-storey house.
<!-- SENTENCE:S030_15b USE:U030_15 -->
A door in the middle led to a parlour and withdrawing-room full of cross lights.
<!-- TRANSLATION:C030_15 -->
刘易斯·伯特伦拆下家族旧宅的部分残垣，用那些石料建起一幢狭窄的三层房子。正面中央的门通向起居室和退席会客厅，里面各种方向的光线交错。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:C030_15 -->
